# Uncertain rendering decisions: ja

Generated by `python3 scripts/review_checklist.py ja --write`; do not edit by hand. 2021 decisions in 61 books, every ja rendering decision the drafters marked uncertain. Tick an item once a reviewer has settled it, and regenerate the list after the content changes.

## 創世記 (83)

- [ ] [GEN 1:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/1/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "第一の日" (L); "一日目" (B, R), and so "第二の日" / "二日目" for the following days — The Hebrew uses a cardinal, "day one" (m2), but "一つの日" is not natural Japanese and "一日" can be read as the first of a month; Literal therefore uses the plain ordinal 第一.
- [ ] [GEN 1:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/1/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "天蓋" (L, B); "大きな丸天井" (R) — The word means a solid-looking expanse beaten out like metal that holds back the waters above (m1). 大空 suggests only open air; 天蓋 (a canopy over the world) keeps the picture of something spread out overhead, and Readable says it plainly as a great dome.
- [ ] [GEN 1:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/1/#v27) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "これを" ... "彼らを" (L); the pronouns left out (B, R), with no gender span — Hebrew says "him" and then "them" of humankind, one and many (m2). Literal keeps the shift with the neutral これ for the one and 彼ら for the many; Japanese has no need of the English gender setting here.
- [ ] [GEN 2:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/2/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "人" ... "土" (L), "土地" (B), "畑" (R) — Hebrew links the human (adam) with the ground (adamah) by sound (m2). Japanese cannot keep the play on words; the link is made plain in verse 7, where the man is formed from the dust of the ground.
- [ ] [GEN 2:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/2/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "香りのよい樹脂" for bdellium; "しまめのう" for onyx — Bdellium is most likely a fragrant resin, though some read it as a pearl (m1); the resin is said in plain words rather than as an unfamiliar name. The stone is not certainly identified (m2); しまめのう (onyx) is written in kana because its kanji are hard.
- [ ] [GEN 2:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/2/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "わき" (L); "あばら骨" (B, R) — The word means a side, here most likely a rib, though it may mean one side of him (m2). Literal keeps the wider word as the English does; あばら骨 is the everyday word for a rib.
- [ ] [GEN 2:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/2/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "女" ... "男" — Hebrew ishah and ish sound alike (m2); Japanese 女 and 男 do not, but they keep the pairing, and the reason given (taken out of man) still makes the link. A footnote-style transliteration in the text would break the poem.
- [ ] [GEN 3:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/3/#v1) L/B/R · register · the serpent and the woman speak to each other in the plain style; the serpent uses "神は……言われた" with the respect form — Neither is a servant or petitioner to the other, so the plain style suits their talk. Speakers in the story keep the light respect form when they speak of God, as the narrator does.
- [ ] [GEN 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/3/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "日の風の吹くころ" (L); "日中の風が吹くころ" (B); "夕方になって、風が吹いてきたころ" (R) — The phrase most likely means the cool of the evening, when the breeze rises, but this is not certain (m3). Literal and Balanced keep the wind; Readable names the evening as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [GEN 3:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/3/#v14) L/B/R · register · God addresses the serpent as "おまえ" — God speaks in judgment to the serpent, which is not questioned (m3); おまえ marks the distance and condemnation, while God keeps あなた for the man and the woman.
- [ ] [GEN 3:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/3/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "子孫" for offspring; "打つ" for strike, the same verb for both blows (L, B) — The same verb is used for both blows, and the struggle ends with the serpent struck on the head (m3); Literal and Balanced keep one verb for both. 子孫 can mean many descendants or one (m2).
- [ ] [GEN 4:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/4/#v7) L · word_choice · "[顔を]上げる" for lifting up; "その願いはあなたに向かうが、あなたはそれを治めなければならない", in the words of 3:16 — Lifting up may mean being accepted with the face lifted, against the fallen face of verse 5 (m1); Literal supplies 顔 so that the link can be heard. The last words repeat 3:16, so Literal uses the same Japanese "願い ... 向かい ... 治める" there and here (m3).
- [ ] [GEN 4:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/4/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · names "ヤバル", "ユバル", "トバル・カイン", "ナアマ", "アダ", "ツィラ"; Naamah as his "妹" — Japanese must choose older or younger sister; nothing marks her as older, and she is named after him, so 妹 is used. Uncertain, as the Hebrew does not say.
- [ ] [GEN 4:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/4/#v23) R · register · Lamech boasts in rough plain speech, calling himself "おれ" (R); "わたし" (L, B) — Lamech's song is a boast of vengeance (m1); Readable lets a young reader hear its tone in the rough first person.
- [ ] [GEN 5:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/5/#v2) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "人" as the name for man and woman together, with no gender span; quoted as 「人」 at Readable — The name given is for man and woman together (m1); 人 says that, so no setting is needed. Readable marks it as a name with brackets.
- [ ] [GEN 5:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/5/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "ケナン" for Kenan, and in this chapter "マハラルエル", "イエレド", "メトシェラ" (as Luke 3:37), "ノア", "セム", "ハム", "ヤフェト" — Names already in the Japanese New Testament keep that form. Luke's カイナム follows a Greek spelling that differs from the Hebrew; Genesis transcribes the Hebrew ケナン, so the same man has two forms, as he does in English (Kenan, Cainan).
- [ ] [GEN 6:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/6/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "神の子ら" (L, B), "神の子たち" (R), with no explanation in the text — The phrase most likely means heavenly beings, but this is uncertain (m1); Japanese keeps the phrase as the English does and leaves the sense to the footnote.
- [ ] [GEN 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "ネフィリム" left untranslated, with "大男たち" added at Readable; "勇士たち" (L, B), "英雄たち" (R) — The Nephilim were a people of great size and strength, and the name is left untranslated as in the English (m1); Readable explains it as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [GEN 6:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/6/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "悔やまれ" for regretted; "心を痛められた" for grieved to his heart — The text speaks of God's sorrow in human terms (m1, m2); 悔やむ carries sorrow over what was done without suggesting a mistake corrected, as 後悔 can.
- [ ] [GEN 6:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/6/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "堕落し" (L, B), "腐りきって" (R) for corrupt; "暴虐" (L, B), "暴力" (R) for violence — Corrupt means ruined, spoiled (m1), and violence is wrongdoing that harms others (m2). The Hebrew uses the same verb for corrupt (verses 11-12) and destroy (verse 13); Japanese needs two verbs, and the link is left to the note.
- [ ] [GEN 6:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/6/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "箱舟" (L, B), "とても大きな舟" / "舟" (R); "ゴフェルの木" (L, B), "じょうぶな木" (R); "瀝青" (L), "タール" (B, R) — The ark is a chest-shaped boat (m1), which 箱舟 pictures; the wood is not known today (m2), so its name is transcribed. God commands Noah with 〜しなさい at every level.
- [ ] [GEN 8:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/8/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "……からである" (L), "たとえ……としても" (B, R) — Follows the meaning decision: Literal keeps the Hebrew "for", Balanced and Readable read it as "even though".
- [ ] [GEN 9:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/9/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "弓" (L, B), "虹" (R) — The Hebrew calls the rainbow by the word for a warrior's bow; Literal and Balanced keep "弓", Readable names the rainbow, as the English does.
- [ ] [GEN 9:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/9/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "広げ" (L), "広い土地を与え" (B, R) for enlarge — The verb plays on the name Japheth, which Japanese cannot show; Balanced and Readable give its sense of room to spread out.
- [ ] [GEN 10:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/10/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の前に" (L, B) kept; Readable "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}にも知られるほど" — The phrase is uncertain (in the LORD's sight); Literal and Balanced keep it, Readable follows the English Readable.
- [ ] [GEN 10:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/10/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヤフェトの兄" (Shem the elder) — Japanese must choose between older and younger brother; this follows the meaning decision that Shem is the elder.
- [ ] [GEN 11:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/11/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "東から" (L, B); Readable "東の方であちこち" — Follows each English level; the meaning decision footnotes "eastward" as the other reading.
- [ ] [GEN 12:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/12/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "モレの大木" (L, B), "モレの大きな木" (R); Shechem "シケム" as in Acts 7:16 — Matches "マムレの大木" for the same Hebrew word in 13:18; the tree may be an oak or a terebinth, so Japanese does not name the kind.
- [ ] [GEN 14:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/14/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · Amraphel "アムラフェル", Shinar "シンアル", Arioch "アルヨク", Ellasar "エラサル", Kedorlaomer "ケドルラオメル", Elam "エラム", Tidal "ティドアル", Goyim "ゴイム" — Names not in the NT are transcribed from the Hebrew in the usual modern Japanese pattern; settled here for Genesis.
- [ ] [GEN 14:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/14/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · Bera "ベラ", Birsha "ビルシャ", Shinab "シンアブ", Admah "アドマ", Shemeber "シェムエベル", Zeboiim "ツェボイム", Bela "ベラ" — Transcribed from the Hebrew; the king Bera and the town Bela fall together as ベラ in Japanese, which the context keeps apart (王ベラ, ベラの王).
- [ ] [GEN 14:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/14/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · Rephaites "レファイム人", Zuzites "ズジム人", Emites "エミム人"; places "アシュテロト・カルナイム", "ハム", "シャベ・キルヤタイム" — Peoples take 人 after the Hebrew plural form, as the names are known in Japanese; two-part place names are joined with a middle dot (style guide 5).
- [ ] [GEN 14:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/14/#v20) L/B · supplied_words · "アブラムは" at L and B as well as R — The Hebrew says only "he gave him"; Japanese needs a subject at every level after the speech, and the English Balanced and Readable already name Abram. Literal keeps 彼に for the receiver.
- [ ] [GEN 15:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/15/#v5) L/B/R · supplied_words · subject: "[主は]" (L), "主は" (B), "神は" (R) — The Hebrew has only "he". Japanese needs a subject after the shift from Abram's speech; Balanced names him 主 (Adonai, no span: the English Balanced has no divine-name span here), Readable 神 as the English Readable says God. Never 彼 for God (style guide 4), so Literal supplies 主 in brackets.
- [ ] [GEN 15:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/15/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · Kenites "ケニ人", Kenizzites "ケナズ人", Kadmonites "カドモニ人" — Peoples transcribed from the Hebrew with 人, as カナン人 in 13:7.
- [ ] [GEN 16:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/16/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · the angel of the LORD: "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の使い" — 使い is the word for God's messenger at every level, as Genesis 22 and 32 use (主の使い, 神の使い); 天使 is kept for the plural angels of 19:1. The narrator gives the messenger no respect forms (style guide 4: not for angels), but his words in 16:10-11 speak as God.
- [ ] [GEN 16:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/16/#v13) L · word_choice · Literal keeps "後ろ姿" for the Hebrew "after" (achare) — The line is obscure (meaning decision m2); Literal shows the Hebrew word that suggests she saw God only from behind, as Moses in Exodus 33:23; Balanced and Readable follow the English.
- [ ] [GEN 17:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/17/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · covenant: "契約" at every level, Readable too (the English Readable says "promise") — 契約 is an everyday Japanese word, and keeping it at Readable lets a reader follow the one covenant through chapters 15 and 17; Readable adds 守る and 約束 where the sense is God's keeping his word.
- [ ] [GEN 17:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/17/#v22) R · supplied_words · Readable says "天に" (to heaven) — Japanese 上って行かれた wants a goal; Readable names it, since God's going up ends the appearance (meaning decision m1). Literal and Balanced keep the bare verb.
- [ ] [GEN 18:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/18/#v3) L/B · word_choice · my lord: "わが主よ" (L, B) — Japanese 主 can be a courteous "lord" or the Lord, which keeps the ambiguity the meaning decision footnotes; no divine-name span, as the Hebrew has Adonai.
- [ ] [GEN 18:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/18/#v15) L/B/R · register · Sarah speaks to the LORD politely ("笑いませんでした"); the LORD answers plainly; Literal supplies [主は] and Balanced says 主 — A person to God speaks politely (OT supplement). Balanced names the speaker 主 without a span, as the English Balanced has only a pronoun here.
- [ ] [GEN 18:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/18/#v19) L/B/R · register · God speaks of "the LORD" in the third person: no respect form at L ("実現する"), light respect at B and R ("実現される", "かなえられる") — Literal follows the speaker's own words strictly; Balanced and Readable read the third-person name as the narrator's respect for God, as the NT drafts do when God is named in a quotation (Hebrews 7:21).
- [ ] [GEN 19:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · blindness (sanwerim): "目くらまし" (L); "まばゆい光で目をくらませた" (B); "目が見えないようにしました" (R) — The rare word may mean a sudden dazzling (meaning decision m1); Literal keeps a word that allows both senses, Balanced follows the English "blinding light".
- [ ] [GEN 19:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v12) B · register · The angels speak plainly to Lot with authority; Literal "おまえ" softened to "あなた" at B — Angels speak plainly (as Luke 1 in the NT drafts); Balanced uses あなた so their urgency reads as care, not contempt.
- [ ] [GEN 19:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v13) B · supplied_words · Balanced names the sender "主は" without a span where the English has "he" — Japanese would need a subject for 遣わされた; the English Balanced has one divine-name span in this verse, so Japanese keeps one span and writes the second mention 主 plainly. Readable leaves the subject out.
- [ ] [GEN 19:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · my lord: "わが主よ" (L, B); "だんなさま" (R) — As 18:3: 主 keeps open whether Lot speaks to the angel or to the Lord (the Masoretic reading, meaning decision m1); no divine-name span.
- [ ] [GEN 19:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · loyalty (hesed): "誠実" (L); "いつくしみ" (B, R) — Steadfast kindness (meaning decision m1), shown by the LORD's messengers in saving Lot: the book's forms for chesed, "誠実" at Literal and "いつくしみ" for God's chesed at Balanced and Readable (24:12).
- [ ] [GEN 19:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/19/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · kiln: "かまど" (every level) — A furnace for pottery or lime (meaning decision m1); Japanese かまど in kana is the familiar word for a fire that sends up thick smoke.
- [ ] [GEN 20:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/20/#v16) L · word_choice · "目のおおい" at Literal — Literal keeps the Hebrew picture, whose sense is uncertain (m2); Balanced and Readable say what it is taken to mean: proof that she did no wrong.
- [ ] [GEN 21:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/21/#v9) L · word_choice · "笑っている" at Literal, "からかっている" (B, R) — Literal keeps the verb's plain sense and its play on Isaac's name (21:6); Balanced and Readable take it as mocking, as the English does, with the meaning note's other reading in its footnote.
- [ ] [GEN 22:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/22/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · the place name translated: "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}は見てくださる" (L), "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}が備えてくださる" (B), "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}が用意してくださる" (R) — As the English, the name is given in translation so the reader hears its link with Abraham's words in 22:8; Literal keeps the verb 'see' (m1), as it did there. The saying's other reading is in the meaning note's footnote (m2).
- [ ] [GEN 23:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/23/#v6) L · word_choice · "神の君侯" (L), "力ある首長" (B), "大切な指導者" (R) — Literal keeps the Hebrew phrase; Balanced and Readable take it as a superlative, a mighty prince (m1), as the English does.
- [ ] [GEN 24:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/24/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "兄" for Laban — Japanese must say older or younger; the text does not say, but Laban acts as the head of Rebekah's household beside her father (24:50, 55), which suits an older brother.
- [ ] [GEN 24:63](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/24/#v63) L/B · word_choice · "思いにふける" (L), "思いめぐらす" (B) — The verb's meaning is uncertain (m1); the Japanese, like the English "meditate" and "reflect", takes it as quiet thought without the religious technical sense of "瞑想".
- [ ] [GEN 25:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/25/#v18) B/R · word_choice · "すべての親族に逆らって" (B); "親族のだれとも対立しながら" (R) — As the English Balanced and Readable, which read "over against" as hostility (m2); Literal keeps the open "向かい合って".
- [ ] [GEN 25:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/25/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "妹" (L, B); "兄ラバン" (R) — Japanese names a sibling as older or younger; the Hebrew does not say. Laban acts as the head of Rebekah's household in chapter 24, so he is taken as the elder brother, the usual reading.
- [ ] [GEN 25:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/25/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "もしそうなら、なぜわたしはこうなのか" (L); "わたしの身にいったい何が起きているのか" (B); "わたしはいったいどうなっているのでしょう" (R) — Her broken words (m2) are kept bare at Literal; Balanced and Readable give the plain sense of distress, as the English does, without choosing "why do I live?".
- [ ] [GEN 25:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/25/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "穏やかな" (L, B); "物静かな" (R) — tam here is set against the wild hunter and means settled, even-tempered (m1); the moral sense "全き" would mislead.
- [ ] [GEN 27:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/27/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "人を欺く者" (L); "からかっている" (B); "だまそうとしている" (R) — The rare word means one who makes a fool of someone (m1). Readable says "trick", as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [GEN 27:39](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/27/#v39) L/B/R · word_choice · "……から離れ" (L, B); "遠く離れた所に住む ... 露もない" (R) — As the meaning decision reads min here, "away from" (m1); the same nouns as in Jacob's blessing (27:28, "地の豊かさ", "天の露") are kept so the contrast is heard.
- [ ] [GEN 27:40](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/27/#v40) L/B/R · word_choice · tarid "振り切る" (L, B); "自由になろうと戦い" (R) — The rare verb seems to mean to break loose like an animal refusing the yoke (m1); the Japanese gives that sense without settling the exact meaning.
- [ ] [GEN 28:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/28/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "ネバヨトの妹" — Nebaioth was Ishmael's firstborn (25:13), so Mahalath is taken as his younger sister; Japanese must choose.
- [ ] [GEN 28:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/28/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · sullam "階段" at every level — As the meaning decision: a stairway or ramp rather than a ladder (m1). The familiar "はしご" is not wrong but suits angels going up and down side by side less well.
- [ ] [GEN 28:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/28/#v21) L/B/R · word_order · the condition ends at "帰るなら"; "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}はわたしの神となり" opens the promise — As the English ("then"): the reading in the text, with the other noted in the footnoted meaning decision (m1).
- [ ] [GEN 29:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/29/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "弱かった" (L); "弱々しかった" (B); "優しい目" (R) — As the English levels, which keep the uncertain word (m1) open in different ways; Readable follows the English "gentle".
- [ ] [GEN 31:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/31/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "点々のある" for mottled (barod), beside "しま", "ぶち" and "まだら" — A fourth marking needs its own word; "まだら" is already used for spotted (30:32), so mottled is "点々のある" at every level.
- [ ] [GEN 32:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/32/#v25) L/B/R · register · "ある人", with no respect forms in the narration of 32:25-33; the man speaks plainly, Jacob answers him politely ("放しません", "教えてください") — The text calls him only "a man" and leaves who he is to Jacob's words in verse 31 (m1); light respect forms would answer the question the story keeps open. Jacob's polite speech shows that he knows he is dealing with someone greater.
- [ ] [GEN 34:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/34/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "取り引きし" (L); "自由に行き来し" (B); "好きな所を行き来し" (R) — The verb may mean to trade or to move about freely (the meaning decision is uncertain); Literal keeps the trading sense, Balanced and Readable the moving about, as the English levels do.
- [ ] [GEN 34:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/34/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "安心しきった町に" (L); "何の抵抗も受けずに" (B); "だれにも止められずに" (R) — betach can describe the unsuspecting city or the brothers' unopposed entry (the meaning decision is uncertain); Literal keeps the first reading, Balanced and Readable the second, which the English also follows.
- [ ] [GEN 35:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/35/#v18) B/R · word_choice · "わたしの苦しみの子" for Ben-oni; "右手の子" for Benjamin — The English brackets the meanings of both names; the meaning decisions mark both senses as uncertain, so Japanese gives them plainly, without explaining "右手" further.
- [ ] [GEN 36:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/36/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "温泉" (L, B); "温かい水がわき出る泉" (R) — The Hebrew word is otherwise unknown; the meaning decision reads it as hot springs, probably, and Japanese follows that reading.
- [ ] [GEN 36:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/36/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · "川のほとりのレホボト", without naming the river — Which river is meant is uncertain (meaning decision m1), so Japanese does not say "ユーフラテス川" here.
- [ ] [GEN 37:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/37/#v2) B · word_choice · na'ar as "手伝いとして働いていた" (B) — The meaning decision reads it, uncertainly, as a junior helper; Literal keeps "若者".
- [ ] [GEN 37:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/37/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · ketonet passim as "手首[と足首]まで[届く]長衣" (L); "袖の長い上着" (B); "袖の長い特別な服" (R) — The meaning decision reads it, uncertainly, as a long robe reaching to wrists and ankles; Japanese avoids the colorful coat of older tradition. The same words return in 37:23, 31-33.
- [ ] [GEN 37:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/37/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "おまえの母" kept as it stands — Rachel has died (35:19); the meaning decision leaves open whom Jacob means, so Japanese does not explain or change it.
- [ ] [GEN 37:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/37/#v25) L/B · word_choice · "樹脂と香油と没薬" (L, B); "香料や、良い香りのする樹脂" (R) — Costly plant resins whose exact identification is uncertain (meaning decision m1); Readable groups them, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [GEN 37:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/37/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · The subject of "pulled up" and "sold" left as unclear as the Hebrew ("彼らは" L, B; passive R) — Who drew Joseph out and who sold him is uncertain (meaning decision m1); Japanese does not decide it.
- [ ] [GEN 38:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/38/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "エナイムの入り口" (L); "エナイムの門" (B, R) — petach Enaim may be a town gate or a fork in the road (meaning decision m2); Literal keeps the open word "入り口".
- [ ] [GEN 38:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/38/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · qedeshah as "聖なる女" (L); "神殿娼婦" (B); "神殿で体を売る女" (R) — A woman connected with a shrine (meaning decision m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the root sense "consecrated", as the English Literal does, and Balanced and Readable keep the shrine apart from "娼婦" (zonah, 38:15).
- [ ] [GEN 38:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/38/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしより正しい" at every level — tsadeqah mimmenni may mean she is in the right and he is not (meaning decision m1, uncertain); "正しい" holds both the comparison and the verdict, as the book's word for righteous.
- [ ] [GEN 43:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/43/#v18) L · word_choice · "転がりかかり" keeps the uncertain image of 'roll himself upon us' at Literal — The sense is probably 'attack' (see the meaning decision); Balanced says "言いがかりをつけて襲いかかり".
- [ ] [GEN 46:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/46/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヨブ" for Iob, as the Hebrew text has it — Transcribes the name in the Hebrew text (see the meaning decision's footnote on the variant "Jashub"); it is not the Job of the book of Job, though Japanese spells both alike.
- [ ] [GEN 48:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/48/#v22) L · word_choice · "一つの肩[の地]" (L) keeps the Hebrew word "shoulder"; Balanced and Readable name the ridge and Shechem — The play on the name Shechem ("shoulder") cannot be heard in Japanese; the meaning decision's footnote explains it.
- [ ] [GEN 49:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gen/49/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "それが属する者" (L), "それが本来属する方" (B), "それが本当にふさわしい方" (R) — Follows the meaning decision's reading of the disputed word; Balanced and Readable use "方", a respectful word for the coming ruler, without deciding a messianic reading in the wording. The name "シロ" is left to the meaning decision's note.

## 出エジプト記 (54)

- [ ] [EXO 1:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/1/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "助産婦" (L, B); "お産を手伝う女性" and then "二人" (R) — 助産師 is the term for today's licensed profession; the older 助産婦 suits women of the ancient world. Readable describes what they did instead.
- [ ] [EXO 3:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/3/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしは、わたしであるところの者である" (L), "わたしは、わたしである者だ" (B, R); the name as "わたしはある", without inner brackets — The name is explained from the verb "to be" (m1) and then used as a name (m2). "わたしはある" is the form the Japanese John 8:58 already uses for the Greek echo of this verse; "わたしである" keeps the same verb in the explanation. Japanese cannot also use one verb for "I will be with you" (3:12, "いる"). The …
- [ ] [EXO 6:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/6/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · El Shaddai as "全能の神" (as Genesis), and the name as "わたしの名、{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}" with the span kept — The verse contrasts a title with the personal name (m1, m2). With the default 主 the reader sees the usual Japanese form; the setting shows that a name, ヤハウェ, is meant.
- [ ] [EXO 8:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/8/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "[虫の]群れ" (L), "あぶの群れ" (B), "あぶ" (R) for the swarm ('arov) — The Hebrew says only "the swarm"; Literal keeps that, and Balanced and Readable name a biting fly as the English names flies (uncertain, see the meaning footnote).
- [ ] [EXO 10:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/10/#v7) B/R · word_choice · "あの者たち" (B), "あの人たち" (R) for "the men"; "その男たち" at Literal — Here the word may mean the people as a whole (uncertain, see the meaning footnote), so Balanced and Readable keep it open; in 10:11 Pharaoh's "men" are only the males and stay "男たち".
- [ ] [EXO 15:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/15/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · Miriam "ミリアム", "アロンの姉"; prophetess "女預言者" (L, B), "預言者" (R); tof "小太鼓" (L, B), "タンバリン" (R) — Japanese must choose older or younger sister; Miriam is usually taken as the eldest of the three (2:4-8), but the Hebrew does not say.
- [ ] [EXO 17:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/17/#v16) L/B/R · setting_alternative · Yah: plain "ヤハ" at Literal, a divine_name span (主 / ヤハ) at Balanced and Readable, as the English; kes yah 'throne of Yah' kept — The English has the span only at Balanced and Readable; the phrase is very uncertain (meaning decision m1) and Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [EXO 18:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/18/#v11) L/B/R · supplied_words · "[その方は彼らにまさっておられた]" (L) for the English Literal's supplied words; no divine name added inside the brackets — The Hebrew sentence is incomplete (meaning decision m1, uncertain); Japanese supplies only what the English does, with a pronoun-like "その方" rather than a second name.
- [ ] [EXO 19:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/19/#v1) B/R · word_choice · "三か月目に入ったその日" (B), "三か月目の初め" (R) for 'in the third month ... on this day' — Follows the meaning decision (the first day of the third month); Literal keeps the Hebrew's 'on this day'.
- [ ] [EXO 20:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/20/#v3) B/R · word_choice · 'on my face': "わたしと並べて" (B), "わたしのほかに" (R); Literal "わたしの顔の前に" — The meaning decision allows 'besides me' and 'in my presence' (uncertain); "並べて" keeps both: no other god set beside God.
- [ ] [EXO 20:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/20/#v6) L/B · word_choice · "幾千にまで" (L, B) for 'to thousands' — Leaves open whether thousands of people or the thousandth generation is meant (meaning decision, uncertain).
- [ ] [EXO 21:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/21/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "神のもとに" (L, B), "神の前に" (R) for 'to (the) God' — The meaning decision reads 'before God, perhaps at the place of worship' (uncertain); the Japanese keeps "神" as the Hebrew and the English do.
- [ ] [EXO 21:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/21/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · naqam: "復讐がなされる" (L), "報いがなされる" (B), "主人は罰を受ける" (R) — The meaning decision: the slave's death must be punished (uncertain how); Balanced keeps the idea of avenging without naming the penalty.
- [ ] [EXO 21:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/21/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "子どもたちが出て来た" (L), "子どもが早く生まれてしまった" (B, R) — Follows the meaning decision (premature birth, uncertain) and the English; "流産" would choose the other reading.
- [ ] [EXO 22:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/22/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · ha-elohim "神" at every level (22:7-8), as the English — The meaning decision reads God himself, before whom the case is brought (uncertain; some read 'the judges'). Respect forms are not used because God is the speaker.
- [ ] [EXO 22:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/22/#v28) L · word_choice · "あなたの満ちたものと、あなたのしたたるもの" (L) — Literal keeps the two obscure nouns as they stand; Balanced and Readable give the meaning decision's reading (the harvest and the juice of the presses, uncertain).
- [ ] [EXO 23:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/23/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'azav ... 'azav: "任せる" ... "解いてやる" (L), "放っておく" ... "助け起こす" (B, R) — The meaning decision notes the same verb used two ways (uncertain); Japanese cannot keep one word for both senses, so the two senses are given.
- [ ] [EXO 23:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/23/#v25) L/B/R · supplied_words · 'he will bless': "[主は]……祝福する" (L); passive "祝福される" (B, R) — Japanese does not use "彼" for God; the passive keeps the shift from third to first person (meaning decision, uncertain) without a second divine-name span.
- [ ] [EXO 23:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/23/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · tsir'ah "すずめばち" (in kana at every level) — Follows the English 'hornet'; the meaning of the word is uncertain (meaning decision). The kanji 雀蜂 is not Jōyō.
- [ ] [EXO 25:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/25/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · tahash: "上等の革" at every level, as the English "fine leather" — The animal is not certainly known (meaning decision m1); the English gives the sense, not a guess at the species.
- [ ] [EXO 25:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/25/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "皿", "小皿", "水差し", "杯" (L, B) / "鉢" (R); drink offerings "注ぎの供え物" (as Genesis 35:14) — The exact shape of each vessel is not certain (meaning decision m1).
- [ ] [EXO 25:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/25/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · lampstand "燭台" (as Hebrews 9:2); base "台座", shaft "支柱" (L, B); cups "杯"; kaftor "節" (L), "つぼみ" (B, R); blossoms "花" — Decorations shaped like parts of a flower (meaning decision m3); the exact forms are uncertain.
- [ ] [EXO 26:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/26/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · qerashim: "枠板" at every level — Upright frameworks or perhaps solid boards (meaning decision m1); "枠板" leaves room for both.
- [ ] [EXO 26:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/26/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · masakh: "幕" ("入り口の幕"); the work of a worker in colors "刺繍をする者" (L, B), "ししゅうをする人" (R) — Embroidered or woven in coloured patterns; which is meant is not certain (meaning decision m2).
- [ ] [EXO 27:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/27/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · karkov: "張り出し" (L, B), "祭壇の周りを囲む出っ張り" (R) — A rim or border around the altar, halfway up; the word is not certainly understood (meaning decision m1).
- [ ] [EXO 27:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/27/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · chashuqim: "帯輪" (L, B), "帯" (R) — Bands around or between the pillars; the exact sense is uncertain (meaning decision m2).
- [ ] [EXO 28:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/28/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · me'il "外衣" (L, B), "上着" (R); ketonet "長衣" (L, B, as Genesis 37:3), "長い服" (R); mitsnefet "ターバン"; avnet "帯" — The high priest's garments (meaning decision m1); the weave of the tunic is not certain (m2).
- [ ] [EXO 28:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/28/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · The twelve stones (28:17-20): "赤めのう", "トパーズ", "緑柱石"; "トルコ石", "サファイア", "エメラルド"; "ヒヤシンス石", "めのう", "紫水晶"; "かんらん石", "しまめのう", "碧玉" (R "へきぎょく") — Several identities are uncertain (meaning decision m1); the Japanese names follow the English identifications.
- [ ] [EXO 28:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/28/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · Urim and Thummim: "ウリムとトンミム" at every level — Objects by which God's decisions were sought; their form is unknown (meaning decision m1), so the Hebrew names are kept.
- [ ] [EXO 28:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/28/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · tachra: "よろいの襟口" (L, B), "兵士のよろいの首回り" (R) — A strong, reinforced neck; the word is not certainly understood (meaning decision m1).
- [ ] [EXO 29:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/29/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · yoteret: "肝臓の上の小葉" (L, B), "肝臓をおおっている部分" (R) — The part is uncertain (m2); Readable follows the English Readable's "the piece that covers the liver".
- [ ] [EXO 29:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/29/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · ishsheh: "火によるささげ物" (L, B), "火で焼いてささげる物" (R) — Follows the reading of meaning decision m3, itself marked uncertain.
- [ ] [EXO 29:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/29/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · tenufah: "揺り動かすささげ物" (L, B); Readable "揺り動かしてささげる" — Presented to God with a motion of the hands (m1, uncertain). Readable's added explanation (English r-en-1) speaks of God as "わたし", since God is the speaker and Japanese has no deity_pronoun span.
- [ ] [EXO 29:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/29/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · chitte (piel of chata): "罪を清める" (L, B), "清める" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (cleanse from sin), itself uncertain; the verb shares its root with "罪のためのささげ物".
- [ ] [EXO 29:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/29/#v43) L/B/R · supplied_words · "それは" (L) left open; "その天幕" (B), "その場所" (R) — What is made holy is uncertain (m1); Balanced and Readable follow the English.
- [ ] [EXO 30:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/30/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · tsela'ot / tsiddim: "両脇" and "両側" (L); "向かい合う両側" (B, R) — One ring on each of two opposite sides (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the two Hebrew words apart.
- [ ] [EXO 30:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/30/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "流れ出る没薬" (L, B), "純粋な没薬" (R); qinnamon "肉桂" (L, B), "シナモン" (R); qeneh bosem "香りのよい葦" — The spices are not certainly identified (m2, m3); Readable uses the everyday word for cinnamon.
- [ ] [EXO 30:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/30/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · qiddah (cassia): "桂皮" (L, B), "カシアの皮" (R) — A fragrant bark, not certainly identified (m1).
- [ ] [EXO 30:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/30/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · nikhrat: "断たれる" (L, B), "除かれる" (R) — Removed from the community (m2), as in chapter 12; Readable uses the plainer verb.
- [ ] [EXO 30:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/30/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · nataf, shechelet, chelbenah: transliterated "ナタフ", "シェヘレト", "ヘルベナ" (with "香" at B); levonah "乳香" — The three are not certainly identified (m1-m3); the Hebrew names avoid guessing at a modern substance, where the English uses the traditional stacte, onycha, galbanum. Frankincense has a settled Japanese name.
- [ ] [EXO 31:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/31/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · bigdei ha-serad: "織物の衣服" (L), "精巧に織った衣服" (B), "細かく織った服" (R), the same in 35:19 and 39:1, 41 — The meaning of serad is uncertain (m1); Balanced and Readable follow the English's "finely woven".
- [ ] [EXO 31:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/31/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · ak: "必ず" (L), "何をおいても" (B), "何があっても" (R) — Above all, or nevertheless (m1, uncertain); the Balanced and Readable wordings follow the English's "Whatever else you do" / "No matter what".
- [ ] [EXO 32:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/32/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · elohim here: "神々" (plural) at every level — The verb with it is plural (m1, uncertain); as the English.
- [ ] [EXO 32:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/32/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · cheret: "のみ" (L), "彫刻刀" (B), "道具" (R) — The tool and the process are uncertain (m1).
- [ ] [EXO 32:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/32/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · The repeated "声" carries the verse's rhythm; the play on one Hebrew word (shouting / crying / singing) is not reproduced — The last sense of the word is uncertain (m1); Japanese has no single word for all three.
- [ ] [EXO 32:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/32/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · leshimtsah beqameihem: "彼らに逆らって立つ者たちの間で物笑い" (L), "敵の物笑いの種" (B), "敵に笑われてしまいます" (R) — A disgrace before their enemies (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [EXO 32:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/32/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · fill your hand: "手を満たした" (L), "自分自身を任職した" (B), "仕えるために取り分けられた" (R) — Read as a statement of what the deed has done (m1, m2, uncertain), as the English.
- [ ] [EXO 39:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/39/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · bigde serad "織物の衣服" (L), "精巧に織った衣服" (B), "細かく織った服" (R), as 31:10 and 35:19 — Finely woven garments for priestly service (meaning decision m2); the same words as the command (31:10) and Moses' list (35:19).
- [ ] [EXO 39:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/39/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · The twelve stones as in 28:17-20: "赤めのう", "トパーズ", "緑柱石" … "かんらん石", "しまめのう", "碧玉" (R "へきぎょく") — The same identifications as the instructions in chapter 28, so the making matches the command; several identifications are uncertain (meaning decision m1).
- [ ] [EXO 39:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/39/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · tachra "よろい" (a coat of armor) — The English reads it as a coat of mail (meaning decision m1); the point of the comparison is a reinforced neck opening.
- [ ] [EXO 39:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/39/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · qodesh la-YHWH "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}に聖なる者" (R "…にささげられた聖なる者"), as in 28:36 — The same inscription as in the command of 28:36; the plate marks Aaron as set apart to God (meaning decision m2).
- [ ] [EXO 40:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/40/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "据えた" / "置きました" (mounted the lamps) — The English reads ya'al as setting the lamps in place; lighting them is the alternative in the footnote (meaning decision m1).
- [ ] [EXO 40:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/40/#v31) L/B · word_choice · "洗うのであった": a repeated practice — The English reads the verb as habitual ("would wash"), with verse 32 (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [EXO 40:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/exo/40/#v38) R · word_choice · "雲の中に火がありました": the fire within the cloud — The English reads "in it" as in the cloud (meaning decision m2, uncertain).

## レビ記 (52)

- [ ] [LEV 1:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/1/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · mur'ah "餌袋"; benotsatah "羽と共に" — Both words are uncertain (meaning decisions m1, m2); Japanese follows the reading of the English. 餌袋 is the ordinary word for a bird's crop.
- [ ] [LEV 2:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/2/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · marcheshet "深鍋" (L, B), "深い鍋" (R) — Probably a deep pan in which the dough was cooked in oil (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [LEV 2:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/2/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · aviv "穂" (L), "熟したばかりの穂" (B), "新しい穂" (R); geres "ひき割り" (L), "砕いたもの" (B, R) — Ripe grain parched and then crushed (meaning decision m2, uncertain); Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [LEV 3:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/3/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · kesalim "腰"; yoteret "肝臓の上の小葉" (L), "肝臓の小葉" (B), "肝臓をおおっている部分" (R) — The appendage of the liver is probably its long lobe (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the forms of Exodus 29:13.
- [ ] [LEV 4:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/4/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · ashem "責めを負う" (L, B), "罪がある" (R), through Leviticus — They incur guilt though they do not yet know it (meaning decision m3, uncertain); 責めを負う names the state without a court verdict.
- [ ] [LEV 4:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/4/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "あるいは……知らされたなら" (L), "気づかされたなら" (B), "気づくこともある" (R) — Either he realizes his guilt or someone tells him (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the passive keeps both open.
- [ ] [LEV 5:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/5/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · asham here "償い[の代価]" (L), "償うべきもの" (B), "罪の埋め合わせ" (R) — Here asham is what the guilty person owes to set the wrong right (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the animal itself is a sin offering, so the offering name 償いのささげ物 is kept for 5:15-26.
- [ ] [LEV 5:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/5/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · malaq "ひねる" (L), "ひねって折る" (B), "折る" (R), with the head left attached — Here the neck is broken but the head stays on (meaning decision m1, uncertain), unlike 1:15.
- [ ] [LEV 5:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/5/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · ma'al "不実を働く" (L), "背く" (B), "不誠実なこと" (R); "聖所のシェケル" (L, B), "聖なる天幕で使われる正式なシェケル" (R) — Wronging God by misusing what is his (meaning decision m2); the valuation clause is uncertain (m3) and Japanese follows the English reading. The English Balanced deity pronoun ("His holy things") has no Japanese span; 聖なるもの is said plainly.
- [ ] [LEV 5:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/5/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · pikkadon "預かり物"; tesumet yad "担保" (L, B), "支払いの約束として渡した物" (R) — Denying that one holds what was entrusted (meaning decision m2, uncertain); Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [LEV 5:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/5/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · beyom ashmato "彼の償いの[ささげ物の]日に" (L), "償いのささげ物をささげる日に" (B, R) — The day he is found guilty or brings his guilt offering (meaning decision m3, uncertain); Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [LEV 6:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/6/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "触れるもの" (kana もの), leaving open a person or a thing — The Hebrew does not say whether people or objects are meant, and how holiness passes by touch is uncertain (meaning decision m3).
- [ ] [LEV 6:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/6/#v13) B/R · word_choice · beyom "油注がれる日から" (B, R), "日に" (L) — The phrase is read as "from that day on" (meaning decision m1, uncertain), as the English Balanced and Readable do.
- [ ] [LEV 6:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/6/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · tufine "焼いた……を細かく砕いたもの" (L), "細かく砕いた" (B, R) — The exact sense of the word is unknown (meaning decision m2); Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [LEV 7:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/7/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · karet "その民から断たれる" (L), "民の中から断たれる" (B), "民の中から除かれる" (R), through Leviticus — Losing one's place among God's people (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the Japanese keeps the image without deciding between death and exclusion, as the English does. The form of Genesis 17:14 and Exodus 12.
- [ ] [LEV 7:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/7/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · sheqets "忌むべきもの" (L), "忌むべき生き物" (B), "いやな生き物" (R) — The text follows the reading "detestable thing"; some manuscripts read "swarming thing" (meaning decision m2).
- [ ] [LEV 7:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/7/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · tenufah "揺り動かすささげ物" (L, B), "揺り動かしてささげる物" (R), through Leviticus — Presenting it to God by a motion before the altar (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the form of Exodus 29:24-27.
- [ ] [LEV 7:35](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/7/#v35) L/B/R · word_choice · mishchah "分" (L), "与えられる分" (B, R) — The share assigned to the priests, perhaps "the anointed portion" (meaning decision m1, uncertain); Japanese follows the English reading. The one who presented them is Moses (m2), so Literal's 彼 is not God.
- [ ] [LEV 7:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/7/#v36) L · word_choice · "油を注がれた日に" with a respect form and no pronoun — The one who anointed them is Yahweh, through Moses (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the respect form shows God as subject without 彼, and Japanese has no deity-pronoun span.
- [ ] [LEV 8:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/8/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · tenufah "揺り動かすささげ物" (L, B), "揺り動かしてささげる" (R) — As LEV-1 (7:30). The subject stays Moses as the English reads it (meaning decision m2).
- [ ] [LEV 8:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/8/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · "fill your hand": "あなたがたの手を満たす" (L), "任職" (B), "祭司にする" (R) — As EXO 28:41 (手を満たす L, 任職する B, 祭司の務めに任じる R); the idiom is explained in meaning decision m1.
- [ ] [LEV 8:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/8/#v34) B/R · supplied_words · "再び" (B), "毎日" (R), as the English "again" / "each day" — Meaning decision m1: the rite is repeated on each of the seven days.
- [ ] [LEV 9:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/9/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · ranan "叫び声を上げ" (L), "喜びの声を上げ" (B), "喜んで叫び" (R) — Meaning decision m2 (a ringing cry of joy, uncertain): Literal keeps the bare shout, B and R the joy as the English.
- [ ] [LEV 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/10/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · machtah "火皿" (L, B), "香をたくための皿" (R); esh zarah "異なる火" (L), "定めに外れた火" (B), "ふさわしくない火" (R) — As EXO 25:38 (fire pans) and EXO 30:9 (strange incense); meaning decision m1 (not permitted) is uncertain and footnoted.
- [ ] [LEV 10:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/10/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · nasa avon "咎を負う" (L, B), "罪を取り除く" (R) — As EXO 28:38; meaning decision m1 (the priests carry the guilt away) is uncertain and footnoted; Readable follows the English "take away".
- [ ] [LEV 11:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · shafan "イワダヌキ" (the rock hyrax) — Meaning decision m1 (probably the rock hyrax; uncertain, footnoted); イワダヌキ is its Japanese name.
- [ ] [LEV 11:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · Bird names in katakana at every level (ワシ, ヒゲワシ, クロハゲワシ, …), the usual Japanese species names closest to the probable identifications; raven 烏 (L, B), カラス (R) … — Meaning decisions m1-m2: most identifications are uncertain and footnoted; species names are written in katakana in modern Japanese, and kanji such as 鷲 are not Jōyō.
- [ ] [LEV 11:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v16) L · word_choice · bat ha-ya'anah "ダチョウ" at every level — Meaning decision m1: literally "the daughter of the desert bird", probably the ostrich (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [LEV 11:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · arbeh, sal'am, chargol, chagav "イナゴ" (B "群れをなすイナゴ"), "ハゲイナゴ", "コオロギ", "バッタ"; Readable folds them as the English "every kind of" — Meaning decision m1: four kinds of leaping insect, identifications uncertain and footnoted.
- [ ] [LEV 11:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · mafreset parsah ve-shesa einennah shosa'at "ひづめが分かれているが、割れ目が裂けておらず" (L), "ひづめが完全には割れておらず" (B, R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, footnoted): hooves not split fully in two.
- [ ] [LEV 11:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · choled, akhbar, tsav "イタチ", "ネズミ", "大型のトカゲ" (R "大きなトカゲ", kept apart from koach オオトカゲ in 11:30); the list runs on to 11:30 as the English — Meaning decision m2: probable identifications (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [LEV 11:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · Lizard names in katakana (ヤモリ, オオトカゲ, カベトカゲ, スナトカゲ, カメレオン) — Meaning decision m1: probably all kinds of lizard (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [LEV 11:35](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v35) L/B/R · word_choice · tannur "かまど"; kirayim "こんろ" — Meaning decision m1: fixed cooking places made of clay (uncertain); こんろ is the everyday word for a small stove.
- [ ] [LEV 11:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/11/#v46) L/B/R · word_choice · torah "規定" (L, B), "きまり" (R), as for every "this is the law of ..." in Leviticus — Meaning decision m1: instruction on a subject; the book-wide form for torah of a case (6:2, 14:2), so the summary formulas of chapters 6-15 read alike.
- [ ] [LEV 12:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/12/#v7) R · word_choice · kipper "罪の償いをする" (R) also where no sin is in view — Meaning decision m1: atonement here removes uncleanness; the Readable keeps LEV-1's one term for kipper and the meaning decision explains the sense.
- [ ] [LEV 13:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/13/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · netheq "たむし" (L, B), "かゆくてかさかさした部分" (R) — Meaning decision m1: a scaly, itching patch on the scalp or chin; たむし is the everyday Japanese name for such a patch, but the identification is not certain.
- [ ] [LEV 13:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/13/#v48) L/B/R · word_choice · sheti / erev "縦糸" / "横糸" (L, B), "縦の糸" / "横の糸" (R) — Meaning decision m1: the threads of a weave, or yarn for weaving (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [LEV 14:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/14/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · tsara'at: "重い皮膚病" at every level, for the scale disease of chapters 13-14; the one who has it "重い皮膚病の者" (L), "重い皮膚病にかかった人" (B, R) — The disease is not leprosy (Hansen's disease); "重い皮膚病" says what the reader can picture and matches the English Balanced and Readable sense. Literal keeps the same term because a coined calque of "scale disease" would not be Japanese.
- [ ] [LEV 14:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/14/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · nega' tsara'at in a house: L keeps the same words as for the skin, "重い皮膚病の患部"; B and R say "かび" (mold), as the English Balanced and Readable say "mold" — The Hebrew uses the same term for skin and walls; Literal shows that, while Balanced and Readable name the spreading growth a reader can picture (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [LEV 14:54](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/14/#v54) L/B/R · word_choice · netek (scall): "たむし" (L, B; B "かゆみのあるたむし"), "頭やあごにできる、かゆくてかさかさした部分" (R), as 13:30 — The meaning decision describes an itching, scaly patch where hair grows; "たむし" is the everyday word for such a scaly skin patch, and Readable describes it as the English does.
- [ ] [LEV 15:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/15/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · zov (discharge): "漏出" (L, B), "流れ出るもの" / "液が流れ出る" (R), through chapter 15 — An abnormal flow from the body (meaning decision m3, uncertain); "漏出" is the usual word for it in a reading, and Readable describes it plainly rather than naming an illness.
- [ ] [LEV 16:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/16/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Azazel: transliterated "アザゼル" at every level, kept as a name — Its meaning is unknown (meaning decision m1, footnoted); the English keeps the name, and so does the Japanese.
- [ ] [LEV 17:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/17/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · se'irim (goat-demons): "やぎの悪霊" (L), "やぎの姿をした悪霊" (B, R) — Beings worshipped in the form of goats (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the bare "雄やぎ" would read as the animals of chapter 16.
- [ ] [LEV 19:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/19/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · biqqoret "償い" (L), "賠償" (B), "償いとして、お金を払う" (R) — Follows the meaning decision (compensation); the rare word is uncertain and has a footnote in English.
- [ ] [LEV 21:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/21/#v4) L · word_choice · to profane himself "自分を汚れたものとする", kept apart from "身を汚す" (make himself unclean) — The Hebrew has two verbs, unclean and profane; Japanese "汚す" serves both, so Literal varies the phrase.
- [ ] [LEV 21:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/21/#v12) L/B · word_choice · nezer "聖別のしるし" (L, B) — The word means consecration and can name a crown; the meaning decision gives the sense "mark of being set apart".
- [ ] [LEV 21:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/21/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · hunchbacked "背の曲がった者" / "背中が曲がっている者"; dak (English "dwarf") "発育の不十分な者" (L, B), "体がとても小さい者" (R) — The older Japanese words for both are now felt as slurs; the descriptive forms keep the sense, which is uncertain in the Hebrew.
- [ ] [LEV 22:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/22/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "who makes them holy" read of the holy gifts: "それらを聖なるものとする" — The meaning decision leaves both readings open; Japanese must choose "もの" or "者", and all levels follow the nearest antecedent, the holy gifts.
- [ ] [LEV 22:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/22/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · yabbelet "ただれ" (L, B); garav "膿の出る湿疹" (L, B); yallefet "かさぶた" (L, B), as at 21:20 — The exact conditions are unknown (the English footnote); the same words as for the priests' defects.
- [ ] [LEV 23:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/23/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · soothing aroma "なだめの香り" (L), "喜ばれる香り" (B), "主が喜ぶ香り" (R) — The book-wide forms of Leviticus 1:9; in God's own speech his acts take no respect form (喜ぶ).
- [ ] [LEV 23:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/23/#v24) B/R · supplied_words · teru'ah "角笛を大きく吹き鳴らす" (B, R); Literal "吹き鳴らし" — A Japanese verb of blowing needs an instrument; the ram's horn is the likely one (as Exodus 19:13, 16), though the word may mean a shout (the English footnote).
- [ ] [LEV 23:40](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lev/23/#v40) L/B/R · word_choice · fruit of majestic trees "美しい木の実" (L), "立派な木の実" (B), "いちばん良い実" (R); palm "なつめやし" as Exodus 15:27 — The phrase may mean fruit or boughs (the English footnote); Japanese follows the meaning decision's first reading.

## 民数記 (44)

- [ ] [NUM 1:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/1/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · Gamliel "ガムリエル", from the Hebrew — This leader of Manasseh is not Paul's teacher; the Hebrew form keeps them apart, and the reader may still see they share a name.
- [ ] [NUM 2:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/2/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "レウエル", as the Hebrew text spells the name here — Most Hebrew manuscripts read Reuel here and Deuel in 1:14 for the same man (meaning decision m1); Japanese follows the text of each verse, as the English does.
- [ ] [NUM 3:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/3/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · "エリツァファン" for Elizaphan — The same man as Elzaphan in Exodus and Leviticus, but the Hebrew spells the name in full here; Japanese follows each spelling, as the English does.
- [ ] [NUM 4:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/4/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · put its poles "担ぎ棒を差し入れる" (L, B, R) — Set its poles in place for carrying (meaning decision m3, uncertain: the poles stayed in the rings by Exodus 25:15, so the act may be adjusting them).
- [ ] [NUM 4:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/4/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · mot "担い台" (L, B), "運ぶための台" (R) — A pole or frame for bearing a load (meaning decision m1, uncertain); 担い台 says a carrying frame without fixing its shape.
- [ ] [NUM 4:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/4/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · as a swallowing "ひと飲みする間でも" (L), "ほんの一瞬でも" (B), "ほんの少しの間でも" (R) — For as long as it takes to swallow, a moment (meaning decision m1, uncertain and footnoted); Literal keeps the swallowing.
- [ ] [NUM 4:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/4/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · by names "名を挙げて" (L, B), "名前を挙げて" (R) — Item by item, or man by man (meaning decision m2, uncertain); 名を挙げて leaves both open.
- [ ] [NUM 4:49](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/4/#v49) L/B/R · word_choice · he enrolled them "彼らは……登録された" (passive, every level); his enrolled ones "[これらが]彼の登録された者たち" (L), "こうして……登録されたのである" (B, R) — The singular subject is impersonal: they were enrolled (meaning decision m1); the last phrase is read as so they were enrolled (m3, uncertain and footnoted).
- [ ] [NUM 5:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/5/#v21) L/B · word_choice · 'your thigh falling' "ももを衰えさせ" (L, B); R follows the English R "子宮を縮ませ" — The meaning decision (uncertain, with a footnote) reads it as harm that ends her ability to bear children; L and B keep the Hebrew's 'thigh', R names the sense as the English R does.
- [ ] [NUM 7:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/7/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'eglot tsav "覆いのある車" (L, B), "覆いのついた車" (R) — The meaning decision gives covered carts, with a footnote on the uncertain word; 車 is the Genesis word for agalah.
- [ ] [NUM 15:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/15/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · 'arisah "練り粉" (L, B), "パン生地" (R); challah "輪形のパン" (L, B, as Leviticus), "パン" (R); threshing floor "打ち場" (L, B), "脱穀場" (R) — The word may mean dough or coarse meal (meaning decision m1, uncertain); "練り粉" follows the English reading.
- [ ] [NUM 16:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/16/#v14) R · word_choice · "目をくらませて、ごまかせる" for 'gouge out the eyes' (R); L and B keep "目をえぐり出す" — As the English R, Readable gives the probable sense, deceiving the people (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [NUM 17:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/17/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · negef "疫病" at every level (17:11-15) — The usual Japanese word for a deadly outbreak among the people; the Hebrew 'blow, striking' (meaning decision m5) does not name a disease, and a reviewer may prefer "災い".
- [ ] [NUM 17:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/17/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "アロンの杖も……中にあった" leaves open whether Aaron's staff is one of the twelve — The Hebrew is ambiguous (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the Japanese "中に" keeps both readings.
- [ ] [NUM 18:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/18/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · terumah "献納物" (L, B), "取り分けてささげる物" (R); moshchah "分け前" (L), "取り分" (B, R); choq 'olam "永遠の定めの分" (L), "いつまでも定められた取り分" (B) — terumah and choq as Leviticus 7 and 10; moshchah is uncertain (meaning decision m3), and "分け前" leaves its sense open.
- [ ] [NUM 18:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/18/#v10) L · supplied_words · "聖なるものの中の聖なる[所]" (L) — The Hebrew may mean 'among the most holy things' or 'in a most holy place' (meaning decision m1, uncertain); the bracket marks the reading the English takes.
- [ ] [NUM 19:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/19/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · zaraq "振りかける" (L, B), "ふりかける" (R) for the water of impurity — The verb elsewhere means to throw or dash ("浴びせかける" for blood); for water on a person "振りかける" is the natural Japanese, as the English 'sprinkle'.
- [ ] [NUM 21:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/21/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · the fiery snakes "燃える蛇" (L), "炎の蛇" (B), "かまれると焼けるように痛む毒へび" (R); snake "蛇" (L, B), "へび" (R) as Genesis 3 — Meaning decision m1 leaves open whether "fiery" is the burning bite or the colour; Literal and Balanced keep the image, Readable gives the bite, as the English Readable says "poisonous".
- [ ] [NUM 21:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/21/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "those who speak in proverbs" "ことわざを語る者たち" (L), "詩人たち" (B, R); the call "来るがよい ... されるがよい" (B) keeps the possible taunt — Meaning decision m2 reads the song as a taunt over a fallen city (uncertain); "〜がよい" can carry irony without bungo forms.
- [ ] [NUM 22:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/22/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · "reckless" "無謀" at every level — Meaning decision m1 is uncertain between headlong and twisted; "無謀" follows the English reading.
- [ ] [NUM 23:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/23/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · a bare height "裸の高台" (L), "木のない丘" (B), "木の生えていない丘の上" (R) — Meaning decision m3 reads the rare word as a treeless hill, and marks it uncertain.
- [ ] [NUM 24:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/24/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · utterance (ne'um) "言葉" (L, B), "語ること" (R); "whose eye is opened" "目の開かれた者" — "託宣" is kept for mashal (23:7); ne'um is the solemn word that opens the saying. Meaning decision m3 is uncertain; the Japanese keeps the plain image.
- [ ] [NUM 24:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/24/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · aloes "沈香の木" (L, B), "香りのよい木" (R) — Meaning decision m2: fragrant trees; "アロエ" would suggest the succulent plant to Japanese readers.
- [ ] [NUM 24:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/24/#v17) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "the sons of Sheth" "セトの子ら" (L), "セトの民" (B, R), with no gender span; Sheth "セト" (the same Hebrew name as Genesis 4:25) — "子ら" and "民" already include women and men; meaning decision m8 leaves the people's identity open, so the name is given in its Genesis form.
- [ ] [NUM 24:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/24/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · Kain "カイン" (the Kenites' name for themselves, the same Hebrew as Cain), "カインの人々" (R); Asshur "アシュル" (L, B), "アッシリア" (R), as Genesis 10 — Meaning decision m4 takes Asshur as most likely Assyria (uncertain); Readable names it as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [NUM 25:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/25/#v4) L/B · word_choice · hoqa' "さらす" (L), "杭にさらす" (B) — The exact sense is uncertain (the meaning decision footnotes it); "さらす" keeps the public display without deciding the method, B adds the stake as the English B does.
- [ ] [NUM 25:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/25/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · qubbah "奥の天幕" (L, B), "テントの奥の部屋" (R) — The word is rare and its exact sense uncertain (footnoted in the meaning decisions); maggefah is "疫病" at every level, as NUM 17:11-15.
- [ ] [NUM 26:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/26/#v4) L/B/R · word_order · The last clause is a heading for the list: "[次のとおりである]" (L), "次のとおりである" (B), "次のとおりです" (R) — The meaning decision reads it as the heading of the list (uncertain, footnoted); Japanese needs a predicate, so it is supplied.
- [ ] [NUM 26:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/26/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · Zelophehad "ツェロフハド"; daughters Mahlah "マフラ", Noah "ノア", Hoglah "ホグラ", Milcah "ミルカ" (as Nahor's wife in GEN 11), Tirzah "ティルツァ" — The woman's name is spelled differently in Hebrew from the Noah of the flood, but the usual Japanese form is the same "ノア"; the meaning decision tells the reader who she is.
- [ ] [NUM 27:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/27/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · ruach "霊" (every level), as NUM 11:17-25 — The meaning decision leaves open what spirit is meant (uncertain, footnoted); "霊" without a qualifier keeps it open.
- [ ] [NUM 27:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/27/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · Urim "ウリム" (as "ウリムとトンミム", LEV 8:8); "ウリムの裁き" (L), "ウリムによって判断を求める" (B), "ウリムを使って尋ねる" (R) — Whose "mouth" is meant is uncertain; L keeps "その口", B and R follow the meaning decision (Eleazar's word from the LORD). R's deity_pronoun span becomes plain respect form "決められた".
- [ ] [NUM 28:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/28/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · shekhar "強い酒" (L, B), "強いお酒" (R) — The meaning decision reads it here as the strong wine of the drink offering (uncertain); "強い酒" keeps the Hebrew word without naming the drink.
- [ ] [NUM 31:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/31/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · mei niddah "汚れの水" (L), "汚れを除く水" (B), "汚れを取り除く水" (R); "the water" "水" (L), "その水" (B, R) — The NUM-4 forms for the water of chapter 19; Balanced and Readable read the last "water" as the same water (meaning decision m3, uncertain), as the English does.
- [ ] [NUM 31:50](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/31/#v50) L/B/R · word_choice · jewelry: "腕輪", "腕飾り", "印章の指輪" (R "指輪"), "耳輪"; kumaz "飾り物" (L), "首飾り" (B, R) — The last item is uncertain (meaning decision m2); Literal keeps a general word, Balanced and Readable follow the English "necklaces".
- [ ] [NUM 32:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/32/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · chushim "急いで" (L), left out at B ("先頭に立って進んで行き"), "すぐに" (R) — The word is uncertain (meaning decision m2); Balanced folds the eagerness into the verb, as "go eagerly" in the English B does.
- [ ] [NUM 32:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/32/#v38) L/B/R · word_choice · Baal-meon "バアル・メオン", Sibmah "シブマ" (the Sebam "セバム" of verse 3), Kiriathaim "キルヤタイム" (as GEN 14:5); the aside in （ ） at B — The name change is uncertain (meaning decision m1); Balanced keeps the English B aside in Japanese parentheses, as NUM-3 does.
- [ ] [NUM 33:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/33/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · al pi YHWH "DNの口に従って" (L), "DNの命令によって" (B, R) — The NUM-3 forms; the phrase is read with the writing of the list (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [NUM 33:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/33/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · Terah (the camp) "テラ" — The same Japanese as Abraham's father (GEN 11), though the Hebrew spellings differ; a reviewer may prefer "タラ" to keep them apart.
- [ ] [NUM 34:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/34/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · hor hahar "ホルという山" (L), "ホル山" (B, R), the same forms as 20:22 — A northern mountain, not where Aaron died (meaning decision m2, uncertain); Japanese, like the English, keeps the one name and leaves the difference to the footnote.
- [ ] [NUM 34:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/34/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Lebo-hamath "レボ・ハマト" (as 13:21), Zedad "ツェダド" — Read as a place name, as the English does (meaning decision m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [NUM 35:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/35/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · the 2,000 cubits kept as the Hebrew gives them, beside the 1,000 of verse 4 — How the two measures fit together is not certain (meaning decision m1, footnote); Japanese, like the English, does not try to reconcile them. Span count follows the English per level.
- [ ] [NUM 35:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/35/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · edah "会衆" (L), "共同体" (B, R), also in 35:24-25 — The book-wide form for 'edah (LEV-consistency, NUM 1:2); the community sitting as a court is still the same 'edah, so Balanced keeps 共同体.
- [ ] [NUM 35:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/35/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "大祭司が死ぬまで" kept plain, without explaining why the stay ends then — The reason is uncertain (meaning decision m2, footnote).
- [ ] [NUM 36:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/num/36/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · Mahlah "マフラ", Tirzah "ティルツァ", Hoglah "ホグラ", Milcah "ミルカ" (as GEN 11:29), Noah "ノア" — Noah the daughter has a different Hebrew name from Noah of the flood, but the usual Japanese form is the same; check against the forms chosen in NUM 26:33 and 27:1.

## 申命記 (20)

- [ ] [DEU 1:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/1/#v46) B/R · word_choice · "あれほど長い間" (B) and "長い間" (R) for "many days, like the days that you stayed". — The Hebrew idiom means a long time, however long it was (m1, uncertain); a repeated clause reads oddly in Japanese.
- [ ] [DEU 4:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/4/#v48) L/B/R · word_choice · Siyon "シイオン". — Kept apart from Zion (シオン), a different Hebrew name.
- [ ] [DEU 14:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/14/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · roe deer "ノロジカ", wild goat "野やぎ", ibex "アイベックス", antelope "レイヨウ", mountain sheep "野生の羊" — Probable identifications, as the English and its footnote say; species names in katakana or plain words, as LEV 11.
- [ ] [DEU 14:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/14/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · Bird names in 14:12-18 as the Japanese of LEV 11:13-19 (ワシ, ヒゲワシ, クロハゲワシ, アカトビ, クロトビ, 烏 / カラス, ダチョウ, コミミズク, カモメ, ハヤブサ, コキンメフクロウ, ワシミミズク, メンフクロウ, 荒れ野のフクロウ, … — The same list as LEV 11, with the same probable identifications; katakana for species names.
- [ ] [DEU 18:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/18/#v1) L · word_choice · "その嗣業" at Literal, leaving open whose inheritance "his" is — The meaning note marks the referent uncertain; Literal keeps the Hebrew's "his" as "その". Balanced and Readable follow the English reading (what is given to the LORD is their share).
- [ ] [DEU 19:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/19/#v16) L · word_choice · ed chamas "暴虐の証人"; sarah "背いた" at Literal — Literal keeps the Hebrew phrase; sarah can mean turning away from the LORD, which "背く" allows.
- [ ] [DEU 20:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/20/#v19) L/B · word_order · The rhetorical question is placed last, with "？" after the purpose clause ("…入るとは？") — Japanese puts the question first and the clause that explains it after, keeping the Hebrew's question that expects "no".
- [ ] [DEU 21:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/21/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · nachal eitan "絶えず水の流れる谷" (L), "水の涸れることのない谷" (B), "いつも水が流れている谷" (R) — The meaning note reads eitan as ever-flowing and marks it uncertain; all three levels follow that reading.
- [ ] [DEU 21:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/21/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · pi shenayim "二倍の分" (every level) — The meaning note reads the phrase as a double share and marks it uncertain; Readable spells it out as twice what each other son gets.
- [ ] [DEU 21:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/21/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "神の呪い" (L), "神に呪われた者" (B), "神にのろわれた人" (R) — The meaning note reads it as under God's curse and marks it uncertain; the wording is composed fresh, not taken from Galatians 3:13.
- [ ] [DEU 23:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/23/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · mamzer "禁じられた生まれの者" (L), "禁じられた結びつきから生まれた者" (B), "禁じられた結婚から生まれた人" (R) — The meaning note marks the sense uncertain; "私生児" would narrow it to any child born out of wedlock.
- [ ] [DEU 23:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/23/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · qedeshah / qadesh "神殿娼婦" / "神殿男娼" (L, B), "神殿で売春をする者" (R) — The meaning note marks the sense uncertain; the words name the shrine and the act without a technical term.
- [ ] [DEU 23:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/23/#v19) L/B · word_choice · mechir kelev "犬の代価" (L), "男娼の稼ぎ" (B) — Literal keeps the Hebrew phrase; Balanced follows the meaning note's reading, marked uncertain.
- [ ] [DEU 26:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/26/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'oved "さすらいの" (Literal, Balanced), "住む土地もなくさまよっていた" (Readable) — A man adrift without land of his own; the meaning note leaves room for 'close to perishing', which the footnote carries.
- [ ] [DEU 26:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/26/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · he'emir "言い表した" (Literal, Balanced), "言った" / "約束した" (Readable), and in 26:18 "言い表された" / "言われた" for the LORD — One verb for the two declarations of the covenant, with the light respect form for the LORD's side.
- [ ] [DEU 28:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/28/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · shiddafon and yeraqon "立ち枯れと黒穂病" (Literal, Balanced), "作物をだめにする病気" (Readable) — Two diseases of grain; the exact identification is not certain, and Readable gives their effect.
- [ ] [DEU 28:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/28/#v48) L/B/R · word_choice · "神は" for 'he' who puts the yoke (every level) — The meaning decision reads the subject as the LORD (uncertain); Japanese names him with 神, since the English pronoun has no divine_name span.
- [ ] [DEU 32:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/32/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · "岩" for the enemies' god too (Literal, Balanced), "敵の神" (Readable) — Japanese has no capital letter to set "our Rock" apart from "their rock"; the contrast is carried by わたしたちの / 彼らの, and Readable names the enemies' rock as a god.
- [ ] [DEU 32:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/32/#v43) L/B/R · word_choice · "贖いをする" (Literal, Balanced), "罪の償いをする" (Readable) for kipper — The Leviticus pair, kept here for the cleansing of land and people.
- [ ] [DEU 34:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/deu/34/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "葬られた" with no subject named: at Literal an active verb in the respect form (the LORD as the likely subject), at Balanced and Readable read as "Moses was … — The Hebrew does not name who buried Moses; Japanese can leave the subject unsaid, and the respect form points to God without adding a name the text lacks.

## ヨシュア記 (20)

- [ ] [JOS 8:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/8/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · kidon "投げ槍" (L, B), "槍" (R); the same in 8:26 — Meaning decision m1 reads a short spear, with a curved sword possible (uncertain).
- [ ] [JOS 9:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/9/#v4) L/B · word_choice · "made themselves out to be envoys": "使節を装った" (L, B) — Meaning decision m3 keeps envoys as the reading, marked uncertain.
- [ ] [JOS 9:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/9/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · niqqudim "ぼろぼろになっていた" (every level) — Meaning decision m1: crumbled, or spotted with mold (uncertain); "ぼろぼろ" follows the reading the English takes.
- [ ] [JOS 10:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/10/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "threw into panic" "かき乱し" (L), "混乱に陥れ" (B), "あわてさせ" (R); the LORD kept as subject with respect forms ("打たれた"); Beth-horon "ベト・ホロン", Azekah "アゼカ", Makkedah "マケダ" — Meaning decision m2 reads the LORD as the subject of all the verbs (uncertain); no deity_pronoun spans in Japanese. Names are standard modern transcriptions, added to the notes.
- [ ] [JOS 10:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/10/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · dom "静まれ" (L), "とどまれ" (B), "止まれ" (R), a plain imperative to the sun and moon; Aijalon "アヤロン" — Meaning decision m1: stop, stay where you are (uncertain). The plain imperative fits a poetic address to the sky; it is not a command from God, so the 〜なさい rule does not apply.
- [ ] [JOS 12:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/12/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Aphek "アフェク"; Lasharon "ラシャロン", read as a town name — Meaning decision m1 reads a town name, though it may mean "of the Sharon" (uncertain).
- [ ] [JOS 12:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/12/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · Naphath-dor "ナファト・ドル", Goiim "ゴイム", Gilgal "ギルガル" — Transcribed as names, as the English has them; meaning decision m2 keeps "Goiim in Gilgal" as the Hebrew text reads (uncertain).
- [ ] [JOS 19:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/19/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · Shion "シイオン" — Written with イ so that it is not read as Zion ("シオン").
- [ ] [JOS 19:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/19/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · from the region toward Achzib "アクジブの方の地域から" (L), "アクジブの地域" (B), "アクジブのあたり" (R) — The meaning decision reads the phrase as the area around Achzib; the Hebrew is unclear.
- [ ] [JOS 19:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/19/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · and Judah at the Jordan "ヨルダン[川]のところでユダに", kept as the Hebrew has it — The meaning decision keeps the Hebrew text, which is hard to explain; Japanese does not smooth it.
- [ ] [JOS 19:47](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/19/#v47) L/B/R · word_choice · the territory went out from them "彼らから出て行った" (L), "彼らの手から失われた" (B), "守りきれなくなりました" (R) — The meaning decision reads the phrase as the land slipping out of their control; Literal keeps the Hebrew picture.
- [ ] [JOS 21:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/21/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · Gath-rimmon "ガト・リモン" kept as the Hebrew text has it, the same name as in 21:24 — The meaning decision keeps the Hebrew reading; Japanese does not correct it.
- [ ] [JOS 22:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/22/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · the districts (gelilot) of the Jordan "ヨルダン[川]の地域" (L), "ヨルダン川沿いの地域" (B), "ヨルダン川のそばの…地域" (R) — The meaning decision reads the word as the land along the river, perhaps a place name (Geliloth); Japanese keeps the common noun.
- [ ] [JOS 22:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/22/#v20) L · supplied_words · he did not perish [alone] "[一人で]死んだのではなかった" (L) — The meaning decision reads the line as 'not alone'; Literal brackets the word the Hebrew leaves to be understood.
- [ ] [JOS 22:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/22/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · El, Elohim, YHWH "力ある方、神、{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}！" twice, at every level, with ！ — Three names of God called out together, as the English 'The Mighty One, God, the LORD!'; El as "力ある方" so that it is not read as a second "神".
- [ ] [JOS 22:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/22/#v34) L/B/R · supplied_words · named the altar [Witness] "その祭壇を[証し]と呼んだ" (L), "証しと名づけた" (B, R), the name written without quotation brackets — The Hebrew gives the reason but not the name; brackets 「」 round the name would add a quotation the English does not have, so the name stands in the sentence.
- [ ] [JOS 23:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/23/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · hinneh "見よ" (L), not rendered (B, R); the way of all the earth "全地の道" (L), "すべての人が行く道" (B), "もうすぐ死ぬ" (R) — The idiom for dying kept at Literal; "見よ" here is Joshua's solemn 'behold', not a call to look, so the guide's "見なさい" is not used.
- [ ] [JOS 24:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/24/#v7) L/B/R · register · God speaks of himself by name and then as "わたし"; in his own speech the verbs stay plain ("置き", "覆った"), no respect forms, and the English deity_pronoun spans … — God names himself without honorifics in his own speech, as LEV-consistency and NUM-4; Literal brackets "神" as the subject the Hebrew leaves unspoken.
- [ ] [JOS 24:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/24/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · his hand "彼の手" (L, B), "彼の力" (R), leaving 'his' as open as the Hebrew — The meaning decision: most likely Balak's power, possibly Balaam's.
- [ ] [JOS 24:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jos/24/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · the hornet "すずめばち" (every level), as DEU 7:20 — The DEU form; the meaning decision notes that the word is uncertain.

## 士師記 (52)

- [ ] [JDG 1:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/1/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · Othniel "オトニエル"; "the brother of Caleb, younger than he": "カレブの兄弟で彼より年下の" (L), "カレブの弟" (B, R), placed so that it can, like the Hebrew, belong to Othniel — Meaning decision m1: most likely Othniel is Caleb's younger brother; the Japanese order keeps the phrase next to Othniel.
- [ ] [JDG 1:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/1/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · "the ascent of Akrabbim": "アクラビムの上り坂" (L), "アクラビムの坂" (B), "アクラビム峠" (R); Sela "セラ" as a place name — Meaning decisions m1 and m2: the Scorpion Pass; Sela is read as a place name, as the English does (uncertain).
- [ ] [JDG 2:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/2/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "[thorns in] your sides": "脇腹の[とげ]" (L), "脇腹のとげ" (B), "脇腹に刺さったとげのように…苦しめる" (R); "snare" "わな" in kana — Meaning decisions m2 and m3; "罠" is not a Jōyō kanji, so the kana form is used at every level.
- [ ] [JDG 2:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/2/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "the slave of the LORD" as Joshua's title: "主の奴隷" (L), "主のしもべ" (B, R), as the English and as JOS 24:29 / DEU 34:5 — Meaning decision m1 makes it the same title as Moses has; the Japanese follows the English level (slave at Literal, servant at Balanced and Readable), as the Joshua and Deuteronomy consistency passes settled for eved YHWH.
- [ ] [JDG 3:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/3/#v16) B/R · setting_alternative · "a cubit": "{{units:約45センチ|一キュビト}}" (B, R), metric by default with the cubit as the alternative; Literal "一キュビト" — Japanese settings give metric by default (style guide section 7), as Exodus does for the cubit (≈ 45 cm); meaning decision m2: the gomed was a short length, probably a short cubit.
- [ ] [JDG 3:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/3/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · "the carved images" (pesilim): "彫像" (L), "刻んだ像" (B), "石の像" (R), the same in 3:26 — Meaning decision m1: carved stones or idols marking a place, uncertain; Readable avoids deciding that they were idols.
- [ ] [JDG 5:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/5/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "when locks hung loose": "髪が長く垂らされたとき" (L), "戦士たちが長い髪を垂らしたとき" (B), "戦う者たちが髪を長く伸ばした" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain): warriors with uncut hair, given to the LORD's war; the other reading is in the footnote.
- [ ] [JDG 5:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/5/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "this [One] of Sinai": "シナイのあの方" (L), "シナイの方" (B), "シナイ山に来られた神" (R) — Meaning decision m2 (uncertain): a title of the LORD, the God who appeared at Sinai.
- [ ] [JDG 5:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/5/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "the righteous acts of the LORD": "主の正しいわざ" (L, B), "主がしてくださった、正しく、人を救うわざ" (R); "his villagers" "その村人たち" (L, B), "村々に住む神の民" (R), never "彼" — Meaning decisions m2 and m3: his saving deeds for his people in the open villages.
