- [ ] [JDG 6:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/6/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "dens ... caves ... strongholds": "隠れ穴", "洞穴", "要害" (L), "隠れ場", "洞穴", "要害" (B), "隠れ場所", "洞穴", "敵が近づきにくい場所" (R) — Meaning decisions m1 and m2: shelters in the hillsides (uncertain) and high places hard to reach.
- [ ] [JDG 6:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/6/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "my Lord" (adonai) "主よ" at every level, with no divine_name span; "my clan" "わたしの氏族" — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain): the vowels mark the title used for God (style guide 7b: Adonai is 主 without a span).
- [ ] [JDG 7:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/7/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "depart from Mount Gilead": "ギレアドの山から去る" (L), "ギレアド山から立ち去る" (B, R "帰る") — Meaning decisions m2 and m3 (both uncertain, with footnotes): the verb may mean leave quickly, and the place is unclear; Japanese keeps the plain sense "leave" and the name as the Hebrew has it.
- [ ] [JDG 7:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/7/#v6) L/B/R · supplied_words · "lapped with their hand to their mouth": "手で口に[水を運んで]なめた" (L), "手で水を口に運んでなめた" (B), "水を手ですくって、それをなめて飲んだ" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): the 300 scooped water in a hand and lapped it from the hand.
- [ ] [JDG 8:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/8/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "princes" (sarim): "首長たち" (L), "役人たち" (B), "指導者たち" (R); "the palm … in your hand": "手のひらが…あなたの手の中に" (L), "手首を、もうつかんだ" (B), "まだ…捕まえていない" (R) — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain, with a footnote) and m3. Balanced keeps the picture of a hand gripped; Readable says plainly that they have not captured them, as the English does.
- [ ] [JDG 8:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/8/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "the ascent of Heres": "ヘレスの坂" (L), "ヘレス峠" (B), "ヘレス峠を通って上る道" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): a pass or slope by that name.
- [ ] [JDG 8:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/8/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "he taught": "思い知らせた" at every level — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): Gideon made them learn by punishment; "思い知らせる" says exactly that.
- [ ] [JDG 8:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/8/#v22) L/B/R · register · The men of Israel ask Gideon in です・ます ("治めてください", "救ってくださった"); Readable "わたしたちの王になってください" — A people asking a man to found a ruling house; meaning decision m1. Readable says "be our king", which is what the request means; the Hebrew verb (mashal) is "rule", kept as "治める" at L and B.
- [ ] [JDG 8:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/8/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · ephod "エフォド" (R adds "礼拝に使う聖なる物", as the English); Ophrah "オフラ" — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): a sacred object of gold; the Exodus form of the word.
- [ ] [JDG 9:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · Beth-millo "ベト・ミロ"; "the oak of the pillar": "石柱の大木" (L), "石の柱のそばの大木" (B), "石の柱のそばの大きな木" (R), with "大木" for 'elon as JOS 24:26 and GEN 12:6 — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain) and m2: the place where Israel had sworn to serve the LORD; the same tree word as in Joshua keeps the link.
- [ ] [JDG 9:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v9) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "God and men": "神と人々" (L, B), "神や人" (R), with no gender span (also 9:13) — Meaning decision m2 (uncertain, with a footnote): God and human beings; "人々" already includes everyone.
- [ ] [JDG 9:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "an evil spirit": "悪い霊" at every level — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): a spirit that brings harm and strife. "悪霊" is the NT word for demons (style guide table) and would say more than the Hebrew.
- [ ] [JDG 9:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "set men lying in wait for him": "彼に対して…待ち伏せする者たちを置き" (L), "アビメレクを狙って…伏兵を置き" (B), "アビメレクを襲わせようと" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain): to waylay Abimelech, and to his harm; "伏兵" as in JOS 8.
- [ ] [JDG 9:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v28) L/B/R · register · Gaal's boast in rough plain speech, with the plain imperative "仕えよ" (L, B), "仕えるがいい" (R); Zebul "ゼブル"; paqid "役人" (L), "手下" (B, R, Gaal's sneer); Hamor "ハモル" … — Meaning decisions m2 (uncertain, with a footnote: a command in the Hebrew) and m4. A drunken taunt; the house "〜なさい" would sound too measured.
- [ ] [JDG 9:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "who would give this people into my hand!": "だれかこの民をわたしの手に与えてくれないものか" (L), "この民がわたしの指揮下にあればよいのに" (B, R); the dare in rough speech "出て来い" (L), "出て来て戦え" (B, R), … — Meaning decisions m1 and m3, and m2 (uncertain, with a footnote): Gaal's challenge to an absent rival.
- [ ] [JDG 9:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v31) L/B/R · register · Zebul's message to Abimelech in です・ます, hinneh "ご覧ください" (L); "in deceit" (betormah) "ひそかに" (L, B), "こっそり" (R) — A governor reports to his master politely. Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): secretly, by stealth.
- [ ] [JDG 9:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · "the navel of the land": "地のへそ" (L), "この地の真ん中の高み" (B), "国の真ん中の高い所" (R); "the oak of the soothsayers": "占い師たちの大木" (L), "占い師の大木" (B), "占い師たちの大きな木" (R) — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain, with a footnote) and m2: a high point at the centre of the land, and a tree where people sought omens.
- [ ] [JDG 9:39](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v39) L/B/R · word_choice · "before the lords of Shechem": "…の先に立って" (L), "…の先頭に立って" (B), "…を率いて" (R) — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain): at their head, leading them.
- [ ] [JDG 9:44](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v44) L/B/R · word_choice · "the companies with him": "彼と共にいた隊" (L), "アビメレクと共にいた部隊" (B), "アビメレクとその部下たち" (R), without a number — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): one company with Abimelech; Japanese leaves the number open, as the Hebrew plural does.
- [ ] [JDG 9:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v46) L/B/R · word_choice · Migdal-Shechem "シケムの塔"; tseriach "砦" (L), "堅固な奥の間" (B), "がんじょうな奥の部屋" (R); El-berith "エル・ベリト" — Meaning decisions m1 and m2 (uncertain, with a footnote): an inner chamber or vault of the temple; m3: the god of verse 4.
- [ ] [JDG 9:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v48) L/B/R · word_choice · Mount Zalmon "ツァルモン山"; "the axes" "斧" (one axe) — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain) and m2: the plural form names one tool.
- [ ] [JDG 9:50](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/9/#v50) L/B/R · word_choice · Thebez "テベツ" — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain): a town north of Shechem.
- [ ] [JDG 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/10/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · Tola "トラ", Puah "プア", Dodo "ドド", Issachar "イサカル", Shamir "シャミル" (Tola and Issachar as GEN 46:13; this Puah is spelled with aleph, unlike Puvah "プワ" of GEN … — Meaning decisions m2 and m3 (uncertain, with a footnote): Dodo is taken as a personal name, as the English does.
- [ ] [JDG 10:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/10/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "his soul became short with the misery of Israel": "[神の]魂は、イスラエルの苦しみに耐えられなくなった" (L), "…もはや見るに忍びなくなられた" (B), "…もうそれ以上見ていられなくなりました" (R) — Meaning decisions m2 (uncertain, with a footnote) and m3: he could no longer bear their suffering. "魂が短くなる" is not Japanese, so Literal keeps "soul" and says what it means; Literal supplies "[神の]" rather than "彼の" for God (no second divine-name span); Balanced leaves the subject to the respect …
- [ ] [JDG 10:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/10/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · "the people, the princes of Gilead": "民、ギレアドの首長たち" (L), "民とギレアドの役人たち" (B), "民とギレアドの指導者たち" (R); rosh "head" "かしら" (every level, through chapter 11) — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain) and m3: the leader of the whole region; sarim as for Succoth in chapter 8. "かしら" in kana avoids reading "頭" as the body part.
- [ ] [JDG 11:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/11/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · Chemosh "ケモシュ" (as NUM 21:29), with no respect forms for him ("所有させる", "与える", R "くれる"), while the LORD has them ("追い払われた") — Meaning decisions m1 (uncertain, with a footnote) and m2: an argument on the king's own terms; Jephthah honours only the LORD.
- [ ] [JDG 11:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/11/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · "the one coming out": "出て来るもの" (L), "出て来る者、あるいは出て来るもの" (B), "だれでも、また何でも" (R), keeping person or animal open — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with a footnote): whoever or whatever comes out, left open; Japanese "もの" can cover both at Literal.

- [ ] [JDG 12:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/12/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · Shibboleth / Sibboleth: "シボレト" / "スィボレト" — The test turns on sh against s; "スィ" keeps the s-sound apart from "シ" in katakana, so the reader hears the difference the story depends on (m1).
- [ ] [JDG 13:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/13/#v19) L/B/R · supplied_words · The doer of the wonder: "[その方は]" (L), "その使いは" (B, R) — Meaning decision m1: the angel performs the wonder of 13:20; Japanese needs a subject, as the English Balanced and Readable supply one.
- [ ] [JDG 14:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/14/#v4) L/B · supplied_words · "he was seeking": no subject in L and B; the respect form "求めておられた" shows that the LORD is meant; Readable names him, as the English Readable does — Meaning decision m2; the English L and B have one divine_name span, so Japanese does not add a second one.
- [ ] [JDG 15:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/15/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "a heap, two heaps": "一山、二山" (L), "ひと山、ふた山と" (B), "山のように積み上げた" (R); the Hebrew wordplay of donkey and heap is not reproduced — Japanese has no pair of words for donkey and heap that sound alike; the footnote of m1 explains the play.

- [ ] [JDG 18:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/18/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · Laish "ライシュ"; Sidonians "シドン人" (L, B), "シドンの人々" (R); "putting to shame … possessing restraint": "辱める者も、権力を握る者も" (L), "押さえつけたり、…害を加えたりする者" (B), "困らせる支配者" (R) — Meaning decision m2 (uncertain): no ruler held power over them or wronged them.
- [ ] [JDG 18:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/18/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · Jonathan "ヨナタン", Gershom "ゲルショム" (as Exodus 2:22), Manasseh "マナセ" as written; the chain runs from the ancestor down ("マナセの子ゲルショムの子ヨナタン" L, B; "マナセの息子ゲルショムの息子" … — Meaning decision m1 keeps Manasseh as the text is written and gives Moses in the footnote; the Japanese follows it.

- [ ] [JDG 19:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/19/#v28) L/B · word_choice · "there was no one answering": "答える者はなかった" (L), "答えはなかった" (B), "彼女は答えませんでした" (R) — Japanese, like the Hebrew, does not say whether she was already dead (meaning decision m1, uncertain, with its footnote).

- [ ] [JDG 20:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/20/#v43) L/B/R · word_choice · menuchah as a place name: "メヌハ" (L, B), "メヌハという所" (R) — Most likely a place name, as the English reads it; the word can also mean "rest" or "easily" (meaning decision m1, uncertain, with its footnote).
- [ ] [JDG 20:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jdg/20/#v48) L · word_choice · "from the entire city" (me'ir metom): "町全体から" (L), as the Hebrew text — Meaning decision m1 (uncertain, with its footnote); Balanced and Readable say "the people of the towns" as the English does.

## ルツ記 (3)

- [ ] [RUT 1:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rut/1/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "testified against me" "不利な証言をされ" (L), "責める証しをされ" (B), "反対して語られ" (R) — The Hebrew is read as a witness speaking against her; the footnote gives the other reading.
- [ ] [RUT 2:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rut/2/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "her sitting in the house" "家で座っていたのはわずか" (L), "小屋で少し休んだだけ" (B, R); sheaves (omarim) "束" — The Hebrew is hard; Literal stays with the words, Balanced and Readable take "the house" as the field shelter, as the English does.
- [ ] [RUT 2:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rut/2/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · chesed here is the LORD's, as the meaning decision reads it: "誠実" (L), "いつくしみ" (B, R) — Follows the reading of the meaning decision; the footnote gives Boaz as the other.

- [ ] [1SA 14:45](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/14/#v45) L/B/R · word_choice · padah "贖い出す" (Literal, Balanced), "命を救う" (Readable) — Set free by a price or a substitute; the means is not stated.

- [ ] [1SA 15:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/15/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · ma'adannot "安心して" (L), "安心しきって" (B), "助かるだろうと思って" (R), following the meaning decision — The word is uncertain (some read 'in fetters' or 'trembling'); the footnote gives the other readings
- [ ] [1SA 16:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/16/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · nasov "[食卓を]囲む" (L), "食事の席に着く" (B), "宴会の席に着く" (R) — Follows the meaning decision (sit down to the meal); the word is uncertain and the footnote gives the other reading
- [ ] [1SA 16:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/16/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · admoni "赤みを帯び" (L), "血色がよく" (B), "顔色がつやつやとして健康そう" (R) — Literal keeps the colour word; the sense is uncertain and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 17:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/17/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · mitschah "すね当て" (L, B), "防具" (R); kidon "投げ槍" (every level, here and in 17:45) — Follows the meaning decision; the kind of weapon is uncertain (some read a sword) and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 17:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/17/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · charitsei hechalav "乳のかたまり" (L), "チーズ" (B, R); sar ha'elef "千人隊の長" (L), "部隊の長" (B), "隊長" (R); 'arubbatam "しるし" — Follows the English per level; the pledge is uncertain and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 18:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/18/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · shalishim "三弦の楽器" (L), "楽器" (B, R) — The kind of instrument is not known; the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 18:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/18/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · bishtayim "二度目に" (L), "もう一度……機会" (B, R) — Follows the meaning decision (a second chance after Merab); the word is uncertain and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 19:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/19/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · teraphim "家の守り神" (L), "家の守り神の像" (B, R), as 15:23; kevir ha'izzim "やぎの[毛の]編み物" (L), "やぎの毛で編んだもの" (B), "やぎの毛" (R) — Balanced adds "像" because a single figure is laid on the bed; the goat-hair item is uncertain and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 19:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/19/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Naioth "ナヨト" (the read form, as the margin directs) — Transcribes the Hebrew; the meaning of the name is uncertain and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 19:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/19/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · bor "水ため" (L, B), "雨水をためておく大きな穴" (R); Secu "セク" — A cistern cut to hold rainwater; Secu is otherwise unknown and the footnote says so
- [ ] [1SA 25:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/25/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · tseror hachayyim "生きている者たちの袋" (L), "命の袋" (B), "宝物を袋に入れて結んでおくように" (R); kaf haqqela "石投げのくぼみ" (L), "石投げで石を投げるように" (B, R) — The images are kept (m1, m3); sling 石投げ as 1SA 17.
- [ ] [1SA 27:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/27/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Geshurites "ゲシュル人" (as JOS), Gizrites "ギズル人", Amalekites "アマレク人", Shur "シュル" (R "シュルの荒れ野") — Gizrites follows the margin's reading, transcribed from the Hebrew root; the form is a first proposal.
- [ ] [1SA 28:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/28/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · elohim (the figure) "神" (L), "神々しい者" (B), "神のような者" (R) — Literal keeps the word the Hebrew uses; Balanced and Readable make clear it is a being from beyond the human world, not the God of Israel, as the English.
- [ ] [1SA 31:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1sa/31/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · vayyachel "ひどくもだえ苦しんだ" (L), "ひどい傷を負った" (B), "ひどいけがをしました" (R) — The verb pictures writhing in pain; Balanced and Readable say he was badly wounded, as the English (the meaning decision is uncertain).

## サムエル記下 (40)

- [ ] [2SA 1:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/1/#v9) L/B · word_choice · "激しい苦しみ" (L), "ひどい苦しみ" (B) for hashavats — The word is rare and its sense uncertain (cramp, agony or faintness); a general word for agony does not commit to one.
- [ ] [2SA 1:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/1/#v18) L/B/R · supplied_words · "弓[の歌]" (L), "弓の歌" (B, R); "ヤシャルの書" as Joshua 10:13 — Quotation brackets would be read as speech, so the song's title is named as "the song of the Bow".
- [ ] [2SA 1:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/1/#v19) L/B · word_choice · hatstsevi "誉れ" (L, B), "いちばんすぐれた人たち" (R) — The word may mean splendor or gazelle; "誉れ" keeps the sense the English reads, Israel's pride in Saul and Jonathan.
- [ ] [2SA 1:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/1/#v21) L/B · word_choice · "油を塗られることもなく" (L), "もう油を塗られることもなく" (B) — Literal keeps the phrase open, as the Hebrew may describe the shield or Saul; Balanced follows the English reading, the shield no longer oiled.
- [ ] [2SA 2:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "アシュル人" — The text names the Ashurites; whether Asher is meant is left to the footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 2:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v14) L/B · word_choice · sachaq "競わせよう" (L), "腕比べをさせよう" (B), "戦わせよう" (R) — The "play" is a contest of arms; "遊ぶ" would sound like a game. The sense is uncertain.
- [ ] [2SA 2:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヘルカト・ハツリム"; Readable adds "鋭い刃の野という意味です" without brackets — The name is transliterated; Readable explains it as the English does, and leaves out brackets, which would be read as speech.
- [ ] [2SA 2:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v23) L/B · word_choice · "槍の石突き" (L, B), "槍の後ろの端" (R) — The butt end of the spear, which could be pointed; Readable uses everyday words.
- [ ] [2SA 2:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v27) L · word_choice · "朝になってようやく…引き上げただろう" — Literal follows the English reading of a difficult clause: without Abner's words the pursuit would have gone on until morning.
- [ ] [2SA 2:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/2/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "アラバ" (L, B), "ヨルダン川の谷" (R); "ビトロン" — As the English per level; the Bitron is transliterated, its sense uncertain.
- [ ] [2SA 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/3/#v8) L/B · word_choice · "犬の頭" (L, B), "つまらない犬" (R) — The idiom for something worthless is kept at Literal and Balanced; its exact sense is uncertain.
- [ ] [2SA 3:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/3/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · tachtav "自分の代わりに" (L, R), "自分の代理として" (B) — The English reading; the phrase is uncertain and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 3:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/3/#v29) L/B · word_choice · "糸巻き棒を握る者" (L), "糸巻き棒を手にする者" (B), "糸をつむぐような女の仕事しかできない者" (R) — The English reading of an uncertain phrase, a man fit only for women's work; some read a crutch.
- [ ] [2SA 4:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/4/#v6) L · supplied_words · "小麦を取る者として" (L), "小麦を取りに来たふりをして" (B, R) — The Hebrew is hard and the old Greek differs; "taking wheat" is read as a pretext, as the English does.
- [ ] [2SA 5:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · tsinnor "水路" (L, B), "水を引くトンネル" (R) — A channel or shaft that brought water into the city; the sense is uncertain and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 5:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v8) L · word_choice · "達せよ" for the unfinished order — Literal keeps David's broken-off command as the Hebrew gives it; Balanced and Readable complete it as the English does.
- [ ] [2SA 5:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "ミロ"; Readable adds "（土と石を盛り上げた台地）" in full-width parentheses, as the English — The Millo is probably a built-up terrace; the sense is uncertain and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 5:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · hammetsudah "要害" at every level here — This stronghold is probably a place in the hills of Judah, not Zion (5:7, 5:9); Readable keeps "要害" so the two are not merged.
- [ ] [2SA 5:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "バアル・ペラツィム"; parats "破る" (L), "打ち破る" (B, R); Readable gives the meaning as 「打ち破る主」, a quotation as the English has — The place name plays on the verb; Readable keeps the English quotation marks so the levels keep the same quotation count.
- [ ] [2SA 5:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · bekha'im "バルサムの木" — The English reading; the tree is not certainly known and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 5:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/5/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "ゲバ", "ゲゼル" as Joshua — The Hebrew of Samuel has Geba (1 Chronicles 14:16 has Gibeon); the footnote notes the variant.
- [ ] [2SA 6:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/6/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "バアレ・ユダ" — The Hebrew name as written; it is another name for Kiriath-jearim (Joshua 15:9), noted in the meaning decisions.
- [ ] [2SA 6:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/6/#v7) L/B · word_choice · hashal "過ち" (L, B), "まちがったこと" (R) — The rare word is read as a rash fault; it is uncertain and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 6:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/6/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "オベド・エドム"; haggitti "ガト人" (L, B), "ガトから来た" (R) — Name settled here for 2 Samuel; Gath as 1 Samuel 17.
- [ ] [2SA 6:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/6/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · 'eshpar "なつめやしの菓子" — The English reading; the word is uncertain and has a footnote.
- [ ] [2SA 12:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/12/#v25) L · supplied_words · L and B leave the subject of "sent" unnamed but use the respect form (送られた), and give "called" to Nathan (彼 / ナタン) — The Hebrew leaves both subjects open (m1); Japanese cannot use 彼 for God, so the respect form marks the LORD as the sender.
- [ ] [2SA 19:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/19/#v15) L/B · word_choice · "彼は" with no name for "he turned" — The subject may be David (through his message) or Amasa; Japanese keeps the pronoun open as the English does, and Readable uses a passive.
- [ ] [2SA 20:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/20/#v3) L · word_choice · "生きながらのやもめの身" (L) for "widowhood of living" — The phrase is obscure; Literal keeps its two words, and Balanced and Readable say "widows while their husband was alive" as the English does.
- [ ] [2SA 20:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/20/#v6) L · word_choice · "わたしたちの目から逃れる" (L) for "snatch away our eye" — The idiom is uncertain; Literal keeps the eye, Balanced says "目の届かない所へ逃れる", Readable "見失う".
- [ ] [2SA 20:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/20/#v14) B/R · word_choice · "アベル・ベト・マアカ" (B, R), "アベルとベト・マアカ" (L); the Berites "ベリ人" — The Hebrew has "and" between the names; verse 15 names one town, Abel Beth-maacah, and Balanced and Readable follow it as the English does. Who the Berites were is not known.
- [ ] [2SA 20:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/20/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · Adoram "アドラム" — The usual Japanese form of the name; it happens to sound the same as Adullam (アドラムの洞穴, 1 Samuel 22:1), a different Hebrew name. A reviewer may prefer a form that keeps them apart.
- [ ] [2SA 21:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/21/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · hoqia "さらし者にする" (L), "処刑して、さらし者にする" (B), "殺して…遺体を柱にかける" (R) — The exact form of the execution is uncertain (the English says impale); Japanese keeps the sense of putting to death and displaying the bodies, more explicit at Readable as in the English.
- [ ] [2SA 21:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/21/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · Ishbi-benob "イシュビ・ベノブ"; the Rapha "ラファ" (R "ラファと呼ばれる巨人の一族") — The Rapha are kept as a name, as the English does; Readable explains them, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [2SA 21:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/21/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · Elhanan "エルハナン", Jaare-oregim "ヤアレ・オレギム", Goliath "ゴリアト" as 1 Samuel 17 — The names follow the Hebrew here; the meaning decisions note the textual questions.
- [ ] [2SA 23:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/23/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Josheb-basshebeth "ヨシェブ・バシェベト", Tahchemonite "タクケモニ人", Adino "アディノ", Eznite "エツニ人" — Transcribed from the Hebrew as it stands; the meaning decisions note that the text is uncertain here.
- [ ] [2SA 23:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/23/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · ariel "勇者" (L, B), "いちばん強い戦士" (R) — The sense of the word is uncertain (meaning decision); Japanese follows the English reading "champions".
- [ ] [2SA 23:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/23/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヤシェンの子ら、ヨナタン" as the Hebrew stands — The meaning decision notes the text is uncertain here; Japanese keeps the entries as the English does.
- [ ] [2SA 24:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/24/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "タフティム・ホドシ", "ダン・ヤアン" transcribed from the Hebrew — Both names are uncertain (meaning decisions); Japanese keeps them as names, as the English does.
- [ ] [2SA 24:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/24/#v14) L/B/R · register · David speaks to Gad plainly at every level — David is king and Gad his court prophet, as David speaks plainly to Abiathar (1 Samuel 22:22); a reviewer may prefer です・ます to a prophet.
- [ ] [2SA 24:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2sa/24/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · hammal'akh "使い" and mal'akh YHWH "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の使い" at every level — As Genesis 16, 21 and Judges 13, where Readable too says 使い for the angel of the LORD and the bare noun; the same word in 24:16-17 shows it is one messenger. 天使 is kept for the simile "like an angel of God" (14:17, 19:28).

## 列王記上 (22)

- [ ] [1KI 8:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/8/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · shiddafon "焼け枯れ", yeraqon "白さび病" (L, B; Readable explains as hot wind and mildew); arbeh / chasil "いなご" / "ばった" — Plain words for the grain diseases the meaning decision names; famine "飢饉" (L, B), "ききん" (R), as Genesis.
- [ ] [1KI 11:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/11/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · achot "姉妹" (L), "妹" (B, R); Tahpenes "タフペネス"; hagevirah "王妃" — The Hebrew does not say which sister is older; Balanced and Readable choose 妹, the more natural reading for a younger bride (flagged). gevirah, the great lady of the court, is 王妃.
- [ ] [1KI 13:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/13/#v8) L/B/R · register · The man of God to the king plain at Literal, です・ます at Balanced and Readable — He refuses firmly as God's messenger; Literal keeps the bare plain form of his vow, while Balanced and Readable let him refuse a king's kindness with plain courtesy. The speech stays open into verse 9, as the English.
- [ ] [1KI 14:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/14/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · 'atsur ve'azuv "つながれた者も解き放たれた者も" (L), "つながれた者も自由な者も" (B), "奴隷も自由な人も" (R) — A pair meaning everyone, whatever his standing; the exact sense is uncertain.
- [ ] [1KI 14:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/14/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "これがその日である。そして何か？今すでに。" (L), "今日がその日である。いや、すでに始まっている。" (B, R alike) — The Hebrew is abrupt and its sense uncertain; Literal keeps its broken shape, Balanced and Readable follow the reading of the meaning decision.
- [ ] [1KI 14:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/14/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · qadesh "神殿男娼" (L, B), "礼拝所で体を売る男たち" (R) — The male counterpart of qedeshah (GEN 38:21 神殿娼婦); the exact role is uncertain, as the meaning decision says.
- [ ] [1KI 15:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · Maacah daughter of Abishalom "アビシャロムの娘マアカ" — Abishalom is kept as the Hebrew spells it (not アブサロム), as the English does; it is probably David's son Absalom, and "daughter" may mean granddaughter.
- [ ] [1KI 15:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v6) B/R · supplied_words · "アビヤムの生涯" (B, R) — "His life" is Abijam's, as the English Balanced and Readable say; the verse repeats 14:30.
- [ ] [1KI 15:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · immo "母" at every level, as 15:2 — Probably his grandmother, who stayed queen mother; the text says mother, and the meaning decision explains it.
- [ ] [1KI 15:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · mifletset "忌まわしいもの" (L), "忌まわしい像" (B), "ひどく汚らわしい偶像" (R) — Something meant to cause horror; what it was is uncertain.
- [ ] [1KI 15:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · shochad "贈り物" at every level — The English says bribe; in Japanese 賄賂 sounds like a private crime, while this is a payment between kings to change an alliance. The meaning decision's footnote keeps the sense.
- [ ] [1KI 15:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/15/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · chalah et raglav "足を病んだ" (L, B), "足の病気になった" (R) — An illness of the feet; the meaning decision notes it may be a euphemism, so the plain words are kept.
- [ ] [1KI 16:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/16/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · ma'aseh yadav "彼の手のわざ" (L, B), "自分の手で作ったもの" (R) — His idols, or all his deeds; Literal and Balanced keep the open phrase.
- [ ] [1KI 16:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/16/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · The accession formula of 15:33 and 16:8 kept as written — The thirty-first year and the twelve years probably count his sole rule and his whole reign; the Japanese does not harmonize them.
- [ ] [1KI 16:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/16/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · be'Aviram "アビラムによって" (L), "アビラムを失って" (B), "…が死にました" (R) — At the cost of his sons, as Joshua 6:26 foretold; Literal keeps the bare preposition.
- [ ] [1KI 18:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/18/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · posechim 'al shtei hasse'ippim "二つの枝の上でよろめいている" (L), "二つの道の間でよろめいている" (B), "どっちつかずのまま、ふらふらしている" (R) — Wavering between two sides; the same verb returns in 18:26 (よろめき回った), so Literal and Balanced keep よろめく.
- [ ] [1KI 18:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/18/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · sig "用を足しに行っている" (L), "席を外している" (B), "ちょっと出かけている" (R) — A going aside, perhaps to relieve himself, as the meaning decision suggests; Literal gives that reading, Balanced and Readable stay with the English (stepped aside / stepped out).
- [ ] [1KI 18:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/18/#v32) B/R · setting_alternative · {{units:約15リットル|二セア}} (B, R); L 二セア — Metric by default; 1 seah is about 7.3 liters, as GEN 18:6 (三セア 約22リットル), so the figure differs from the English US quarts. What the measure gives (the trench's capacity or the area it encloses) is uncertain.
- [ ] [1KI 18:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/18/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · hasibbota et libbam achorannit "彼らの心を引き戻された" (L), "立ち返らせておられる" (B), "ご自分のもとに連れ戻しておられる" (R) — You are bringing them back to yourself, as the meaning decision reads it; the other reading (you let them turn away) is in its footnote.
- [ ] [1KI 19:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/19/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · qol demamah daqqah "かすかな静けさの音" (L), "静けさのようにかすかな音" (B), "ほとんど静けさのような音" (R) — As the English, a sound so faint it is almost silence, not the traditional "静かにささやく声"; 音 keeps the link to the 声 of verse 13 loose, as the footnote explains.
- [ ] [1KI 22:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/22/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "よろいの継ぎ目と胸当ての間" (L), "うろことじのよろいの継ぎ目と胸当ての間" (B), "よろいのつなぎ目" (R) — As the English per level; the meaning decision marks the armor words as uncertain.
- [ ] [1KI 22:48](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ki/22/#v48) L/B/R · word_choice · nitstsav "代官" (L, B), "ユダから任命された総督" (R) — An appointed officer, as the English deputy / governor; R makes the context explicit (Edom under Judah, as the meaning decision says).

## 列王記下 (30)

- [ ] [2KI 1:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/1/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "毛深い人" (L, B), "毛のとても多い人" (R) — "Owner of hair" most likely describes the man himself; it may mean a garment of hair (the English footnote), which a reviewer may prefer.
- [ ] [2KI 1:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/1/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · Jehoram (Yehoram) of Israel and of Judah both "ヨラム"; Jehoshaphat "ヨシャファト" (MAT 1:8) — The usual Japanese form, the same as the Judean king in MAT 1:8; the Hebrew uses the long and short forms of the name for both kings.
- [ ] [2KI 1:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/1/#v18) L/B/R · register · Regnal source formula as fixed in the 1 Kings notes (1KI-3): "その他の事績 … 年代記の書に記されているではないか？" (L), "その他の事績 … 年代記に記されている" (B), "ほかのこと … 記録に書かれています" (R) — One form for the formula through 1-2 Kings; Balanced and Readable state it, as the English does.
- [ ] [2KI 2:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/2/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · ne'arim qetannim "小さな若者たち" (L), "少年たち" (B, R) — Their age is not certain (meaning decision); 少年 covers boys and youths.
- [ ] [2KI 3:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/3/#v7) L/B/R · register · Jehoshaphat's pledge in the words of 1 Kings 22:4 ("わたしはあなたと同じ、わたしの民はあなたの民と同じ、わたしの馬はあなたの馬と同じ" L; "わたしはあなたと一つ、わたしの兵はあなたの兵、わたしの馬はあなたの馬" B; … — The same pledge as 1 Kings 22:4, where Jehoshaphat speaks politely to Ahab; here he speaks to Ahab's son as an equal, so only the copula and the request differ. A reviewer may prefer the polite register of 1 Kings 22.
- [ ] [2KI 4:39](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/4/#v39) L/B/R · word_choice · paqqu'ot sadeh "野のうり" (L), "野うり" (B), "野生のつる草…の実のうり" (R) — A wild gourd, probably a bitter, poisonous kind; the exact plant is not known (meaning decision).
- [ ] [2KI 5:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/5/#v8) L/B/R · register · Elisha's message to the king plain at L, with a light respect form at B (引き裂かれた), です・ます at R — A message sent to the king by a servant; the prophet keeps his authority while observing court form at B and R.
- [ ] [2KI 6:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/6/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · tachanoti "わたしの陣" (L), "陣を敷こう" (B, R) — Where the king meant to station his troops; the word is uncertain (footnoted), so Japanese keeps the general sense.
- [ ] [2KI 6:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/6/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · dive yonim "鳩の糞" every level — The Hebrew as read; it may name a plant (footnoted).
- [ ] [2KI 6:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/6/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · ben hamratseach "人殺しの子" (L, B), "人殺しの息子" (R); zeqenim "長老たち" (L, B), "町の指導者たち" (R) — Ahab's son; the elders as leaders of the city, as the English Readable. hinneh in plain speech いいか (B) as 2SA-consistency, at L too.
- [ ] [2KI 6:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/6/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · "he said" read as the king (B, R "王は言った"); Literal keeps the bare "彼は" — The meaning decision takes the words as the king's (uncertain, footnoted); the English B and R leave the subject as "he", so a reviewer may prefer 彼は at B.
- [ ] [2KI 13:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/13/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヨアハズ" for Jehoahaz and "ヨアシュ" for Joash/Jehoash, kings of Judah and of Israel alike; the synchronism and reign formula as 1KI 15-16 (治世第N年に B; …になってN年目に R) — The usual modern Japanese forms of these names. Hebrew Yo'ash and Yeho'ash are one name (13:25 uses both for one king), so Japanese writes one form for both, as for ヨラム; a reviewer may prefer to mark the long form.
- [ ] [2KI 15:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/15/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "ティフサ" as 1KI 5:4, following the Hebrew; the other reading (m1) is left to the footnote — Japanese follows the text and the English here.
- [ ] [2KI 15:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/15/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "レマルヤの子ペカ"; shalish "士官" (L, B), "将校" (R) as 1KI 9:22; armon "高殿" (L, B), "いちばん守りの固い所" (R); Argob "アルゴブ", Arieh "アルエ" as two men (m2) — Settled forms; the two names are read as men killed with the king, as the English does.
- [ ] [2KI 16:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/16/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · shochad "贈り物" at every level, as 1KI 15:19 — The English says bribe; 1 Kings settled "贈り物" for this payment between kings, and the footnote (m1) gives the sense of a payment to buy help.
- [ ] [2KI 16:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/16/#v15) L/B/R · register · The king's orders to the priest in plain imperatives ("焼け", "振りかけよ") at every level; levaqqer "伺う" (L), "伺いを立てる" (B), "神のみこころを尋ねる" (R) — A king commanding a subject, as the royal orders in 1KI 2:29; the sense of levaqqer is uncertain (m4), so each level keeps it general.
- [ ] [2KI 16:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/16/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · musakh hashshabbat "安息日の覆いのある所" (L), "安息日のための覆いのある所" (B), "安息日に使う屋根のある場所" (R); "because of the king of Assyria" "アッシリアの王のため" (L, B), R "アッシリアの王を怒らせないため" as the … — The structure and the reason are uncertain (m1, m4); each level keeps to what the words say.
- [ ] [2KI 17:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/17/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "エジプトの王ソ" for So, as the Hebrew writes the name — The ruler is not clearly identified (m1); Japanese keeps the name as written and leaves the rest to the footnote.
- [ ] [2KI 17:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/17/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "バビロン", "クト" (Cuthah and Cuth, 17:30, one form), "アワ" (kept apart from Ivvah "イワ", 2KI 18:34), "ハマト", "セファルワイム" (as 2KI 18:34) — Usual modern Japanese forms; one form for one place.
- [ ] [2KI 18:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/18/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · Hezekiah "ヒゼキヤ", Ahaz "アハズ" (MAT 1:9); Hoshea "ホシェア" — The Gospel forms of the kings of Judah are kept; Hoshea follows the Hebrew and is kept apart from the prophet Hosea (ホセア, Greek form in ROM 9:25).
- [ ] [2KI 18:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/18/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · Shalmaneser "シャルマネセル" — Transcription of the Hebrew, the usual Japanese form; to match 17:3 in the chunk before this one.
- [ ] [2KI 18:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/18/#v17) B/R · word_choice · "総司令官" / "宮廷の役人の長" / "将軍" (B); "軍の総司令官" / "王宮の役人たちの長" / "アッシリアの将軍" (R) — The Rabshakeh acts as the army's spokesman in the field, so "将軍" names his role; a literal "chief cupbearer" would mislead. Kept for Isaiah 36-37.
- [ ] [2KI 19:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/19/#v21) L/B/R · register · The oracle against Sennacherib is plain poetry at every level, and he is addressed as "おまえ" — A taunt against an arrogant enemy king; "あなた" would sound too courteous.
- [ ] [2KI 22:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/22/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · nevi'ah "女預言者" (L), "預言者" (B, R) — L keeps the feminine form of the Hebrew; B and R say "預言者", as the English, since the office is the same.
- [ ] [2KI 24:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/24/#v14) L/B/R · word_sense · charash "職人", masger "鍛冶屋" (L, B); "腕のいい職人や金属を扱う職人" (R) — The trade of the masger is uncertain; the English says smiths, metalworkers, and 鍛冶屋 is the plain word for that.
- [ ] [2KI 24:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/24/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · Mattaniah "マタンヤ", Zedekiah "ツィドキヤ" (as 1CH 3 and 1KI 22) — One form for this name across the books, as the 1 Chronicles notes give it for this king; many readers know ゼデキヤ, so a reviewer should confirm.
- [ ] [2KI 25:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/25/#v12) L/B/R · word_sense · "ぶどうを作る者と畑を耕す者" (L), "ぶどう畑の世話と畑仕事" (B, R) — The second word is probably plowmen or farm laborers; the exact sense is unsure.
- [ ] [2KI 25:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/25/#v15) L/B/R · idiom · "金のものは金として、銀のものは銀として" (L), "金でできたものも銀でできたものも" (B, R) — The doubled words most likely mean everything of gold and of silver; "pure gold" is another reading.
- [ ] [2KI 25:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/25/#v19) L/B/R · word_sense · "軍の長の書記" (L), "軍の司令官の書記" (B, R) — The Hebrew can mean the commander's secretary or a secretary who was the commander; the English takes the first.
- [ ] [2KI 25:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ki/25/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · Evil-Merodach "エビル・メロダク" — The Hebrew form of the Babylonian king's name (Awel-Marduk); the footnote gives the Babylonian form.

## 歴代誌上 (45)

- [ ] [1CH 2:53](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/2/#v53) L/B/R · word_choice · "ツォルアト人" (Zorathites) kept apart from "ツォルア人" (Zorites, 2:54); "プト人" for the Puthites — Both peoples take their name from Zorah ("ツォルア", JOS 15:33), but the Hebrew forms differ, and the English keeps them apart; Readable says "ツォルアとエシュタオルの人々", as its English does. The Puthites share their Japanese form with Put (1:8); a reviewer may prefer "プティ人".
- [ ] [1CH 4:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/4/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · "アッロン" (Allon), kept apart from Aaron ("アロン") — Japanese cannot tell l from r; the doubled consonant of the Hebrew is shown, so the name is not read as Aaron.
- [ ] [1CH 5:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/5/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "ティルガト・ピルネセル" at every level, the Chronicles spelling (2 Kings 15-16 "ティグラト・ピレセル") — Japanese keeps one form of a name across the levels and follows this book's spelling where it differs from Samuel and Kings (Ornan, Tou, Hadoram, Shophach); the meaning decision says it is the king of 2 Kings.
- [ ] [1CH 5:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/5/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · Pul "プル"; "ティルガト・ピルネセル" at every level (as 5:6); Literal joins the two names by そして, Balanced "すなわち", Readable "とも呼ばれます" as the English. — Follows the English rendering decision and the meaning decision that one king is meant (uncertain).
- [ ] [1CH 5:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/5/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · Halah "ハラ", Habor "ハボル", Gozan "ゴザン" as 2KI 17:6 (2KI-4 notes); Hara "ハラア", with the final aleph, to keep it apart from Halah. — Both names would otherwise be ハラ in Japanese; a reviewer may prefer another way to distinguish them.
- [ ] [1CH 6:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/6/#v43) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヒレズ" as the Leningrad Codex spells it (JOS 21:15 has Holon "ホロン"). — Follows the Hebrew and the English; the footnote on the meaning decision gives the variant.
- [ ] [1CH 6:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/6/#v46) L/B/R · word_choice · Literal keeps the broken phrase "部族の氏族から、部族の半分、マナセの半分から"; Balanced "その部族の氏族と、マナセの半部族から"; Readable "いくつかの部族と" as the English. — The name of a tribe seems to be missing (meaning decision m1, uncertain); Japanese does not fill it in.
- [ ] [1CH 7:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/7/#v21) B/R · supplied_words · Balanced and Readable say whose livestock: "ガトの人々の家畜", following the meaning decision that Ephraim's sons raided Gath (uncertain). — Japanese needs an owner for the possessive; Literal keeps the Hebrew "彼らの家畜".
- [ ] [1CH 7:35](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/7/#v35) L/B/R · word_choice · Imna "イムナ" and Shelesh "シェレシュ" coincide in Japanese with Imnah (7:30) and Sheresh (7:16), whose Hebrew spellings differ. — Japanese kana cannot show the difference (final ayin / he; l / r); a reviewer may want a distinguishing form.
- [ ] [1CH 8:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/8/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · The unnamed agent of "carried them into exile": "[人々は]…捕らえ移した" (L), passive "捕らえ移された" (B), "移らされました" (R). — The meaning decision leaves the agent unnamed (uncertain); Japanese passives say this without supplying a subject.
- [ ] [1CH 8:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/8/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · shillach "去らせる" (L, B, as DEU 24 L), "離婚する" (R) as the English Readable. — The meaning decision reads the sending away as divorce (uncertain); Balanced keeps the Hebrew verb.
- [ ] [1CH 8:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/8/#v12) B/R · supplied_words · Balanced and Readable name Shemed as the builder (the English Readable; the meaning decision, uncertain); benoteha "その娘たち" (L), "その周りの村々" (B, R). — Japanese needs a subject where the English Balanced keeps "he"; the nearest name is meant.
- [ ] [1CH 8:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/8/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · Shimeah "シムア" (as Shimea of 1CH 2:13, 3:5, 6:15; the Hebrew ending differs only in the final letters); Mikloth "ミクロト". — Japanese cannot show the difference; kept with the existing form.
- [ ] [1CH 8:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/8/#v38) L/B/R · word_choice · Bocheru "ボケル" read as a name, giving six sons (meaning decision, uncertain); Ishmael "イシュマエル", Azrikam "アズリカム" (1CH 3:23). — Follows the meaning decision; settled forms for the rest.
- [ ] [1CH 9:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/9/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "シロ人" (L, B), "シェラの一族" (R). — The Hebrew gentilic is the same as for Ahijah the Shilonite (1 Kings); the English Readable reads it as the clan of Shelah シェラ (GEN 38).
- [ ] [1CH 9:44](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/9/#v44) L/B/R · word_choice · "ボケル" as a name. — The English reads the word as a name, with a footnote that it may mean "his firstborn"; the Japanese follows.
- [ ] [1CH 11:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/11/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · bashnayim "二倍に" (L), "倍も" (B), left out at R as the English R does. — The sense of "by two" is uncertain (meaning decision); L and B keep the word, R says only "more honor".
- [ ] [1CH 11:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/11/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "ギゾン人ハシェムの子ら" as the Hebrew stands (L, B), "息子たち" (R). — The text is uncertain (meaning decision, footnote); the Japanese keeps it as written, as 2SA 23:32 keeps "ヤシェンの子ら".
- [ ] [1CH 11:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/11/#v46) L/B/R · word_choice · "マハビム人" for hammachavim. — The meaning is unknown; the Japanese transliterates the Hebrew form (plural ending kept), as the English "Mahavite" names it.
- [ ] [1CH 12:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/12/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "百人に…千人に[当たった]" (L), "立ち向かうことができた" (B), "相手に戦うことができ" (R). — The meaning decision reads the phrase as each man's strength (uncertain, with a footnote); the Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [1CH 14:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/14/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "バアル・ペラツィム"; parats "破る" (L), "打ち破る" (B, R); Readable gives the meaning as 「打ち破る主」, as in 2 Samuel 5:20 — Kept from 2 Samuel 5:20; the quotation for the meaning matches the English quotation count.
- [ ] [1CH 14:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/14/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · bekha'im "バルサムの木", as 2 Samuel 5:23 — The English reading; the tree is not certainly known and has a footnote.
- [ ] [1CH 15:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/15/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · Settled for Chronicles, Ezra and Psalms: nevel "琴" every level; kinnor "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R); metsiltayim "シンバル" every level; kelei shir "歌の楽器" (L), "楽器" (B, … — The forms of 1 Samuel 10:5 and 2 Samuel 6:5 (竪琴, 琴, シンバル), kept for the Levites' music so the same instruments have the same names wherever they return.
- [ ] [1CH 15:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/15/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Ben "ベン", kept as a name — As the English, which keeps the Hebrew text (footnoted).
- [ ] [1CH 15:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/15/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-alamot "アラモトに合わせて" (L, B), "高い声に合わせて" (R) — A musical term of uncertain sense (footnoted); Literal and Balanced transliterate it, Readable gives the likely sense as the English does. Psalm 46 heading uses the same term.
- [ ] [1CH 15:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/15/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-hasheminit "シェミニトに合わせて" (L, B), 「第八」という調べに合わせて (R, quoted as the English); lenatseach "指揮する" (L), "音楽を導く" (B, R) — A musical term of uncertain sense (footnoted), the word for "eighth"; Readable keeps the English quotation so the quotation count matches. The same term heads Psalms 6 and 12.
- [ ] [1CH 15:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/15/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · Chenaniah "ケナンヤ"; massa' "[歌を]上げること" (L), "音楽" (B, R) — The English reads massa' as the lifting up of song (uncertain, footnoted); Japanese follows it.
- [ ] [1CH 16:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/16/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'eshpar "なつめやしの菓子", as 2 Samuel 6:19 — The sense is uncertain (footnoted); Japanese keeps the reading of the English and the 2 Samuel parallel.
- [ ] [1CH 16:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/16/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · lehazkir "呼び求める" (L, B), "呼びかけて祈る" (R); lehodot "感謝する"; lehallel "賛美する" — the Levites' three tasks, kept for the later chapters — The English reads "invoke" (footnoted, uncertain); hodah 感謝する and hillel 賛美する are kept apart so the pair stays distinct in Chronicles, Ezra and Psalms.
- [ ] [1CH 16:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/16/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · behadrat qodesh "聖なる輝きのうちに" (L, B); Readable describes the LORD as "聖なる、栄光に満ちた" — The English reads God's holy splendor (uncertain, footnoted); "聖なる装い" (the worshipers' dress) is the other reading.
- [ ] [1CH 16:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/16/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · YHWH malakh "…は王として治められる" (L), "…こそ王である" (B), "…こそ王だ" (R) — The English reads "reigns" / "is King" (uncertain); the shout is quoted as in the English.
- [ ] [1CH 17:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/17/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · ketor ha'adam hamma'alah "高い位の人の身分に従って" (L), "身分の高い者のように" (B), "偉大な人であるかのように" (R) — The English reading of a difficult phrase (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CH 18:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/18/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · lehatsiv yado "自分の手を立てる" (L), "自分の記念碑を立てる" (B), "自分の力を示す記念碑を立てる" (R) — Chronicles has "set up his hand", a monument (uncertain, footnoted), where 2 Samuel has "restore".
- [ ] [1CH 18:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/18/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · Abimelech "アビメレク", Shavsha "シャウシャ" — The names as Chronicles writes them (the first footnoted); the Japanese does not harmonize them with 2 Samuel.
- [ ] [1CH 26:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/26/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · Jehohanan "ヨハナン", the usual Japanese form of the name; Eliehoenai "エルエホエナイ". — Japanese usage does not keep the long Yeho- form of this name apart from Johanan.
- [ ] [1CH 26:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/26/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Parbar transliterated "パルバル" at every level; Readable "パルバルと呼ばれる場所" as the English. — The meaning of the word is uncertain (the footnote explains it); a transliteration does not guess.
- [ ] [1CH 26:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/26/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · Jerijah "イエリヤ", with the initial イエ that keeps it apart from Elijah エリヤ (as イエシェル, イエレド in 1 Chronicles 2 and 4); Jazer of Gilead "ギレアドのヤゼル". — The plain form エリヤ is the prophet Elijah's name; the footnote ties this man to Jeriah of 23:19 and 24:23, which should take the same form.
- [ ] [1CH 27:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/27/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · Jashobeam "ヤショブアム", Zabdiel "ザブディエル"; the same man as Josheb-basshebeth (2 Samuel 23:8) by the footnote, so the name is not changed to that form. — The Chronicles spelling is kept, as the 1 Chronicles 1 notes do for names spelled differently from Samuel.
- [ ] [1CH 27:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/27/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Shamhuth the Izrahite "イズラ人シャムフト", in the Chronicles spelling; the footnote links him with Shammoth of 11:27. — Each list keeps its own spelling of the name.
- [ ] [1CH 28:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/28/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · baruach "霊のうちに" (L, the Hebrew word), "心に描いていた" (B, R), as the meaning decision reads it. — The footnote gives the other reading (by the Spirit).
- [ ] [1CH 28:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/28/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · mizlagot "フォーク" (L, B), "肉をさすフォーク" (R), as EXO 27:3; mizraqot "鉢" (L), "振りかけるための鉢" (B), "血を振りかけるための鉢" (R), as 1 Kings 7:50; qesot "水差し", as EXO 25:29; kefor … — The vessel words of Exodus and 1 Kings; kefor, a covered bowl, is kept apart from the basins.
- [ ] [1CH 28:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/28/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · merkavah "車" (L, B), "神の乗り物" (R); keruvim "ケルビム", Readable "翼のある天の生き物" as 1 Kings 6; "[翼を]" supplied at Literal as the English. — The cherubim as God's chariot (the footnote); "戦車" would suggest war.
- [ ] [1CH 29:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/29/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · shoham "しまめのう" (as EXO 28); pukh "アンチモンの石" (L), "黒いアンチモンの石" (B), "黒い石" (R); shayish "雪花石膏" (L, B), "白く美しい石" (R), as the English alabaster. — Stone names follow the English reading; the footnote marks antimony as uncertain.
- [ ] [1CH 29:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/29/#v7) L/B/R · setting_alternative · adarkonim "ダリク" (L); at B and R {{units:約84キログラム|一万ダリク}} (a daric about 8.4 g), the weight as the English gives it rather than a wage value; the talents as v. … — The English treats the darics as a weight of gold; Japanese does the same, metric.
- [ ] [1CH 29:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ch/29/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · netsach "勝利" every level, as the English; tif'eret "栄光", hod "威厳", gedullah "偉大さ", gevurah "力". — The footnote gives the other sense (splendor, endurance).

## 歴代誌下 (40)

- [ ] [2CH 8:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/8/#v16) B/R · word_choice · "基礎が据えられた日から完成の時まで" (B), "土台が据えられた日から、完成する日まで" (R), for "until the day of the foundation and until its finishing". — The meaning decision reads it as from start to finish.
- [ ] [2CH 9:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/9/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · Court titles and the closing idiom as 1 Kings 10:5: "家臣" (L, B), "家来" (R); "侍従" (L, B), "召し使い" (R); "酌人" (L, B), "飲み物係" (R); "息もなくなった" (L), "息をのむばかり" (B), … — Same scene as the parallel; the meaning decision follows 1 Kings 10:5 for the offerings.
- [ ] [2CH 9:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/9/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · mesillot "階段" every level, as the English "steps"; kinnorot "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R), nevalim "琴". — The meaning decision reads a raised way or stair, uncertain.
- [ ] [2CH 9:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/9/#v12) B/R · word_choice · "持って来た物にまさって" (B), "持って来た物よりも、もっと多くの物" (R) for "besides what she had brought". — The meaning decision reads it as more than her own gifts (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 9:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/9/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "タルシシュへ行く船" / "タルシシュの船" (L, B), R "タルシシュへ航海する船" / "大きな貿易船"; 象牙, 猿 (R さる), くじゃく, as 1 Kings 10:22. — Chronicles has ships going to Tarshish; the meaning decision keeps the difference (uncertain).
- [ ] [2CH 10:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/10/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしはおまえたちのくびきを重くし" for Chronicles' "I will make your yoke heavy", where 1 Kings 12:14 has "my father made your yoke heavy"; Rehoboam rough (おまえたち). — The text as read; the meaning decision is uncertain, with a footnote.
- [ ] [2CH 11:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/11/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · se'irim "やぎの悪霊" (L), "やぎの姿をした悪霊" (B, R), as LEV 17:7; bamot "高き所" (L, B), "丘の上の礼拝所" (R); 'agalim "子牛" (L, B), "若い雄牛の像" (R), as 1 Kings 12:28. — The settled forms; the meaning decision is uncertain about the se'irim.
- [ ] [2CH 11:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/11/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Mahalath "マハラト" (as GEN 28:9), Jerimoth "エリモト", Abihail "アビハイル", Eliab "エリアブ"; L lists the two names side by side as the Hebrew, B and R make Abihail the … — The meaning decision reads Abihail as Mahalath's mother (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 11:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/11/#v23) B/R · supplied_words · "彼らのために" (B), "息子たちのために" (R) supplied with the wives, as the English; L leaves the object open. — The meaning decision reads the wives as sought for his sons (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 12:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/12/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · Lubim "リビア人", Sukkiim "スキ人" (new; a people otherwise unknown), Kushim "クシュ人" (as 2 Kings 19:9 "クシュ"). — Peoples of the Egyptian army; Sukkiim is uncertain (meaning decision).
- [ ] [2CH 12:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/12/#v7) B/R · word_choice · kim'at liflétah "いくらかの救い" (B), "少しだけでも、彼らを救ってやろう" (R); L "わずかな救い". — The meaning decision reads a limited rescue (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 12:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/12/#v12) R · word_choice · "まだ良いこともいくらか残っていました" (R), as the English "were still found"; L, B "良いことがあった". — The meaning decision is uncertain whether these are good deeds or good conditions; the Japanese stays as open as the Hebrew.
- [ ] [2CH 12:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/12/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · The sources formula as 9:29 ("…に記されているではないか？" L, "…に記されている" B, "…に書かれています" R; divrei "言葉" L, "記録" B, R); lehityachesh "系図に記載するために" (L), "系図の記録" (B), "家系の記録" … — The meaning decision reads it as genealogical records within the writings (uncertain, with a footnote).

- [ ] [2CH 14:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/14/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "この地はまだわたしたちの前にある" (L), "まだわたしたちのもの" (B, R); the repeated "we have sought" kept as "求めた…求めると" at every level. — The meaning decisions: the land is still open to us; the verb is repeated for emphasis (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 14:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/14/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "ツェファタの谷" (new), as the English and the Hebrew as written. — The meaning decision keeps the Hebrew name (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 14:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/14/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "強い者と力のない者との間で助けることのできる方は、あなたのほかには" (L), "弱い者が強い者に立ち向かうとき…あなたのほかには" (B), "…あなただけです" (R). — The meaning decision reads it as helping the weak against the strong (uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 15:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/15/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "預言者オデドの預言" for "the prophecy, Oded the prophet", as the English; shiqqutsim "忌まわしいもの" (L), "忌まわしい偶像" (B), "汚らわしい偶像" (R), as 1 Kings 11:5. — The text as it stands (meaning decision, uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [2CH 15:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/15/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · As 1 Kings 15:13 at each level: gevirah "王母" (L, B), "王の母としての位" (R); mifletset "忌まわしいもの" (L), "忌まわしい像" (B), "ひどく汚らわしい偶像" (R); nachal Qidron "キドロンの涸れ谷" (L), … — Same account as the parallel; the meaning decisions are uncertain about the mother and the object.
- [ ] [2CH 18:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/18/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "身を支えて" (L), "身を起こして" (B), "体を支えて" (R): Chronicles' active verb, where 1 Kings 22:35 has "支えられて". — The meaning decision reads the form as active; the Japanese keeps it apart from Kings.
- [ ] [2CH 22:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/22/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · Jehoshabeath "ヨシャブアト", written from Chronicles' own spelling, apart from 2 Kings 11:2 "ヨシェバ" (Jehosheba); chadar hammittot "寝具の部屋" (L), "寝室" (B, R), as 2 Kings. — One form per Hebrew spelling, as Abel-maim beside Abel-beth-maacah; a reviewer may prefer the Kings form for the same woman.
- [ ] [2CH 23:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/23/#v9) L/B · word_choice · maginnot / shelatim "小盾と、盾" (L), "二種類の盾" (B), as the English. — The two words are both shields; their difference is uncertain.
- [ ] [2CH 23:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/23/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "出て来させ" (L), "呼び出して" (B), "送り出して" (R) for the verb "brought out", whose sense here is uncertain; otherwise the Japanese of 2 Kings 11:15. — The meaning decision footnotes the uncertainty.
- [ ] [2CH 24:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ch/24/#v27) L · word_choice · hinnam ketuvim worded as the regnal source formula settled for Kings at Literal ("…記されているではないか？"), a rhetorical question that affirms; B and R state it. — 1KI-consistency fixes this form for hinnam ketuvim; the question form asserts, as the meaning decision's statement does.

- [ ] [EZR 4:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezr/4/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · Osnappar "オスナパル"; 'avar naharah "川向こう" (L), "川向こうの州" (B), "ユーフラテス川の西の州" (R), as NEH 2:7; ukh'enet "そして今" (L), "など" (B, R). — Settled in Nehemiah; "and now" here means "and so on" (m3).
