## ネヘミヤ記 (27)

- [ ] [NEH 1:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/1/#v3) L/B/R · register · The men from Judah report to Nehemiah, a court official, in です・ます. — They speak to the king's cupbearer; the polite form also matches their grief.
- [ ] [NEH 2:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/2/#v6) B · register · Narration keeps plain forms for the king ("良しとし" L, B); B keeps Nehemiah's humble "申し上げた" for his own words to the king, as 2:1 "差し上げた". — Respect forms in narration are for God only; a humble form for the narrator's own act is not a respect form for the king.
- [ ] [NEH 2:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/2/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · Gate and spring names: sha'ar haggay "谷の門" (as 2 Chronicles 26:9), 'ein hattannin "竜の泉", sha'ar ha'ashpot "糞の門" (L, B), "ごみ捨ての門" (R). — Names translated, as the English does; "糞" is not a Jōyō kanji, so R gives the sense (the gate to the refuse dump).
- [ ] [NEH 2:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/2/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · vayechazzequ yedeihem lattovah "自分たちの手を強くした" (L), "取りかかる備えをした" (B), "準備を整え、喜んで…取りかかりました" (R). — The idiom kept at L; B and R follow the English sense (the meaning decision is uncertain).
- [ ] [NEH 3:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "did not bring their neck" kept as "首を差し出さなかった" (L, B); R says the sense; 'adoneihem "主人たち" (L, B), "工事を指揮する人たち" (R). — The meaning decision is uncertain who "their lords" are; R follows the English reading (the men directing the work).
- [ ] [NEH 3:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · lekhisse' pachat 'ever hannahar "川向こうの総督の座に属する" (L), "川向こうの州の総督の管轄下にあった" (B), "…総督が治めていました" (R); Melatiah "メラトヤ", Jadon "ヤドン", Meronothite "メロノト人". — The phrase is obscure (meaning decision uncertain); B and R follow the English reading.
- [ ] [NEH 3:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · Ezer "エゼル", Jeshua "イエシュア" (as Ezra 2:2 and 1 Chronicles 24:11); 'aliyyat hannesheq "武器庫への上り坂"; hammiqtsoa' "曲がり角" (L, B), "城壁が曲がっている所" (R), as 2 Chronicles … — The Angle keeps the Chronicles form; R explains it.
- [ ] [NEH 3:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · hakkikkar "周辺の地域" (L, B), "エルサレムの周りの地域" (R). — The plain around Jerusalem (meaning decision uncertain); as the English.
- [ ] [NEH 3:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · habbayit ha'elyon "王の上の家" (L), "王の上の宮殿" (B, R); chatsar hammattarah "監視の庭" (L, B), "見張りの兵士たちの庭" (R); Palal "パラル", Uzai "ウザイ", Pedaiah "ペダヤ", Parosh "パロシュ". — The verb is understood in the Hebrew: L brackets it, B and R write it, as the English. The English B and R run 3:25 into 3:26 with a parenthesis; Japanese closes 3:25 with a full sentence and makes 3:26 a sentence of its own.
- [ ] [NEH 3:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v26) B/R · word_order · B and R make the temple servants the subject of the stretch "…までを受け持った" instead of the English parenthesis; L keeps the note "住んでいた" with the extent after it. — Japanese cannot hang a parenthesis between the verb of 3:25 and its extent in 3:26; the meaning decision leaves both readings open.
- [ ] [NEH 3:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · sha'ar hassusim "馬の門"; me'al "上手では" (L), "上の方では" (B), "北の方では" (R). — R follows the English "North of"; the meaning decision is uncertain.
- [ ] [NEH 3:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · sha'ar hammifqad "召集の門" (L), "検査の門" (B, R), as the English Muster Gate (L) / Inspection Gate (B, R); 'aliyyat happinnah "隅の屋上の部屋" (L), "隅の上の部屋" (B), … — The gate's purpose is uncertain; Japanese follows the English choice for each level.
- [ ] [NEH 3:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/3/#v37) L/B/R · supplied_words · The object of hikh'isu supplied as "[あなたを]" (L), "あなたを" (B, R), as the English. — The meaning decision reads God as the one provoked (uncertain).
- [ ] [NEH 4:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/4/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · 'eser pe'amim "十度も" (L), "何度も" (B), "くり返し" (R); the warning, obscure in the Hebrew, follows the English at each level. — The meaning decision on the warning is uncertain.
- [ ] [NEH 4:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/4/#v10) L/B/R · word_order · The English runs 4:10 into 4:11 ("behind all the house of Judah / who were building"); Japanese closes 4:10 with "後ろに[いた]" / "控えていた" and lets 4:11 say who … — A relative clause cannot follow its noun across a verse in Japanese; the meaning decision on 4:11 calls the link uncertain.
- [ ] [NEH 4:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/4/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ish shilcho hammayim, obscure, "おのおの自分の武器を[持って]水[へ行った]" (L), "水をくみに行くときも…武器を手放さなかった" (B, R). — The text is uncertain (meaning decision); B and R follow the English reading, L keeps the bare words with brackets.
- [ ] [NEH 5:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/5/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · me'at hakkesef "百分の一" (L), "利息" (B, R); tirosh, yitshar "新しいぶどう酒、新しい油" (L), "新しいぶどう酒、オリーブ油" (B), "ぶどう酒、オリーブ油" (R), as 2 Chronicles 31:5. — The hundredth is probably a monthly rate (meaning decision uncertain); B and R give its sense, as the English.
- [ ] [NEH 6:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · Kephirim "ケフィリム" (transcribed, as the English), biq'at Ono "オノの平野" (L, B), "オノの谷" (R, as the English "Ono Valley"). — The meaning decision allows "one of the villages"; the name is kept as the English does.
- [ ] [NEH 6:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v9) L/B/R · register · vechazzeq et-yadai, a short prayer: "[神よ、]わたしの手を強めてください" (L), "神よ、わたしの手を強めてください" (B), R in its own quotation "「神よ、わたしを強くしてください！」", as the English. — The meaning decision reads it as a prayer (uncertain); です・ます to God as the other short prayers.
- [ ] [NEH 6:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · 'atsur "閉じこもっていた" every level. — The reason is not given (meaning decision uncertain); Japanese leaves it open as the English does.
- [ ] [NEH 6:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "go into the temple and live" "神殿に入って生きられる" (L), "…生きていられる" (B), "自分の命を守るために神殿に入ってよい" (R). — Two senses are possible (meaning decision uncertain); each level follows its English.
- [ ] [NEH 6:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · Noadiah hannevi'ah "女預言者ノアドヤ" (L), "預言者ノアドヤ" (B, R), as 2 Kings 22:14 (Huldah). — One rule for nevi'ah in the Old Testament Japanese (2KI-consistency): Literal marks the feminine form, Balanced and Readable say "預言者".
- [ ] [NEH 6:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/6/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · vayyippelu me'od be'eineihem "自分たちの目に大いに落ちた" (L), "すっかり自信を失った" (B), "いばる気持ちもなくなりました" (R). — The idiom kept at L, its sense at B and R; the reading "were afraid" is uncertain (meaning decision) and kept as the English.
- [ ] [NEH 7:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/7/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ad chom hashshemesh "日が熱くなるまで" (L), "日が高く昇って暑くなるまで" (B, R). — The sense is uncertain (meaning decision); B and R follow the English "until the sun is hot" with what it implies.
- [ ] [NEH 7:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/7/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "ヒズキヤ" for the short spelling Chizqiyyah, as 10:18 and 1 Chronicles 3:23; Ezra 2:16 has the long spelling, "エヒズキヤ". — Men other than King Hezekiah are named by the Hebrew spelling; this Hezekiah is not the king (m1).
- [ ] [NEH 12:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/12/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · Joiakim "ヨヤキム", Joiada "ヨヤダ" — ヨヤダ as NEH-1 (3:6); ヨヤキム shares its Japanese with King Jehoiakim, as Japanese usage does.
- [ ] [NEH 13:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/neh/13/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · yehudit "ユダの言葉" at every level — The English keeps 'the language of Judah' at every level, as 2 Kings 18:26 does; ヘブライ語 would name it in a way the text does not.

## エステル記 (10)

- [ ] [EST 1:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/1/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · bahat "斑岩" (L, B), "赤い石" (R); shesh "大理石"; dar "真珠貝"; sochared "色石" (L), "宝石" (B), "高価な石" (R), following the English readings — The stones are not certainly known (see the meaning decision); Japanese names them as the English does.
- [ ] [EST 2:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/2/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · hanachah "免除" (L), "税の免除" (B, R), as the English; the word may instead mean a holiday — The meaning decision is uncertain; Japanese follows its first reading.
- [ ] [EST 4:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/4/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · mimmaqom acher "別の所から" (L), "ほかの所から" (B), "どこかほかの所から" (R), with nothing added about who will send it — The meaning decision leaves the source unnamed; Japanese keeps the silence of the text.
- [ ] [EST 5:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/5/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ets "木" (L), "柱" (B, R) where the English says gallows; talah "かける" — As 2:23: the pole is named for what it is, the use is left to the verb.
- [ ] [EST 7:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/7/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "the distress is not equal to the damage of the king" "その苦しみは、王の損失に値しない" (L), "王さまを煩わせるほどのことではありません" (B, R) — The phrase is obscure (see the meaning decision); Balanced and Readable follow its reading.
- [ ] [EST 8:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/8/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · rekhesh ha'achashteranim bene harammakhim "王室の早馬、雌馬の子ら" (L), "王の御用のために育てられた足の速い馬" (B), "王の馬小屋の速い馬" (R) — The Persian words are not certainly known (see the meaning decision); Japanese follows the English readings.
- [ ] [EST 8:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/8/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · The triple verb and "little ones and women" are worded as in Haman's decree (3:13) at L and B; taf venashim read as the attackers' families, as the English — The meaning decision notes the same words turned around; the reading of who the women and children are is uncertain.
- [ ] [EST 8:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/8/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · yom tov "良い日" (L), "祝日" (B), "お祝いの日" (R), the same in 9:19, 9:22; mityahadim "ユダヤ人となった" (L), "ユダヤ人になった" (B, R) — The meaning decision leaves open whether they converted or joined the people; Japanese keeps the Hebrew's wording.
- [ ] [EST 9:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/9/#v25) B/R · supplied_words · "エステルが" (B, R) for the Hebrew "when she came", as the English — The meaning decision reads the subject as Esther.
- [ ] [EST 9:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/est/9/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · iggeret happurim hazzot hashshenit "このプリムの第二の書簡" (L), "プリムについての第二の書簡" (B), "プリムについて二通目の手紙" (R) — Which letter is meant is uncertain (see the meaning decision); Japanese keeps the plain reading.

## ヨブ記 (13)

- [ ] [JOB 7:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/7/#v6) L/B · word_choice · 'ereg "機織りの杼" (L, B), described at Readable. — The traditional word for the weaver's shuttle; it is not Jōyō, so Readable describes the tool instead.
- [ ] [JOB 30:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/30/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · malluach "塩草" (L), "塩辛い草" (B); retamim "えにしだ" (L, B), "荒れ地の低い木" (R, as the English "desert shrubs"). — No common Japanese name fits the desert saltbush; L and B name it by its salt taste. えにしだ is the usual Japanese for the broom (retem).
- [ ] [JOB 30:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/30/#v24) L · supplied_words · L supplies "[打ち]" to make the English Literal's reading (no one strikes at a ruined man) say something in Japanese. — The Hebrew line is obscure (m1, uncertain); without the bracket the Japanese would be meaningless. B and R follow the reading that a ruined man reaches out for help.
- [ ] [JOB 31:35](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/31/#v35) L/B/R · word_choice · tavi "署名" (every level); 'ish rivi "訴え手" (L), "わたしを訴える者" (B, R), left as 者, not 方. — The opponent is most likely God (m3, uncertain); Japanese does not decide it by using the respect word 方.
- [ ] [JOB 33:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/33/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · The one who is gracious and speaks is left unnamed, with plain verbs (あわれんで言う), not respect forms. — He may be God or the angel (m1, uncertain); light respect forms would decide it for God. No 「」, as the English.
- [ ] [JOB 38:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/38/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · Kimah "すばる", Kesil "オリオン座", as in 9:9. — The star names used for the same pair in 9:9.
- [ ] [JOB 38:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/38/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · Mazzaroth "マザロト" (L, transliterated as the English L keeps the name), "星座" (B), "星の群れ" (R); Ayish "熊座" at every level, as 9:9. — The star group is not known (meaning decisions m1, m2); Balanced and Readable follow the English.
- [ ] [JOB 40:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/40/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · behemot as the name "ベヘモット" at every level, with "という大きな獣" at R, as the English R "the great beast". — What animal is meant is uncertain (m1, footnoted); a name keeps the Hebrew open, where 河馬 or 象 would decide it. hinneh-na' "さあ…見なさい" (L), as a call to look.
- [ ] [JOB 40:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/40/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · L keeps the jussive "…剣を近づけるがよい"; B and R "…だけが…近づくことができる", as the English (m2, uncertain). God's own acts plain: "神が造った" (R). — The line is hard (footnoted); B and R follow the English reading. In God's own speech his acts take no respect form, as at 39:17.
- [ ] [JOB 40:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/40/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · liwyatan "レビヤタン" (L, B), "海の怪物レビヤタン" (R), as 3:8; in chapter 41 the creature is "それ". — The JOB-1 form; R names what it is once, where the English R keeps only the name, since a young reader would not know it.
- [ ] [JOB 41:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/41/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · 'elim "力ある者たち" (L), "勇士たち" (B), "いちばん強い者たち" (R), as the English. — The word may also mean "gods" (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese follows the meaning decision and does not write 神々.
- [ ] [JOB 42:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/42/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · The LORD's words are given in the wording of 40:7 (わたしはあなたに尋ねる。あなたはわたしに知らせなさい / わたしが問う。あなたはわたしに答えなさい). — Job quotes the LORD's challenge (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the same Japanese lets the reader hear the echo of 38:3 and 40:7.
- [ ] [JOB 42:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/job/42/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · ma'as "退け" (L), "取り消し" (B, R); nicham "悔い改める" at every level; 'afar va'efer "ちりと灰" as 30:19. — Both verbs are uncertain (m1, m2, footnoted). 悔い改める says that Job turns from what he said; it does not say he did the wrongs the friends charged (m4), and the footnotes carry the other readings.

## 詩編 (596)

- [ ] [PSA 2:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/2/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "その方" and "お怒りになる" for "he", which may be the son or the LORD (m3) — その方 and a light respect form fit both readings, so the Japanese keeps the ambiguity the meaning decision records; 彼 would pick the son and could not be used of God.
- [ ] [PSA 3:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/3/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · Selah "セラ" at every level, kept as it is at the end of the verse's last line, straight after the closing punctuation (no space, no brackets); for the whole … — Its meaning is no longer known (m3), so it is transliterated, as every other language keeps it; Japanese has no space between words, so it follows the 。 or 」 directly.
- [ ] [PSA 4:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/4/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · lamnatseach "指揮する者のために" (L), "音楽を導く者のために" (B), "音楽を導く人のために" (R); binginot "弦楽器に合わせて" (L, B), "弦楽器で演奏する" (R); for every psalm title — 1 Chronicles 15:21 settled lenatseach as 指揮する (L) and 音楽を導く (B, R), and the title uses the same verb as a noun phrase; who the director was is uncertain (m2), so no office name such as 聖歌隊長 is used. The title pieces are separated by 。 as the English separates them.
- [ ] [PSA 5:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/5/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'el-hannechilot "笛に合わせて" (L, B), "笛で演奏する" (R) — As the English reads it, an instrument; whether it is an instrument or a tune is uncertain (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 5:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/5/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · 'e'erokh-lekha "あなたの前に[それを]整え" (L), "すべてをあなたの前に整えて" (B), Readable as the English ("願いごとを…申し上げ") — The object (a prayer or a sacrifice) is uncertain (m1), so Literal and Balanced say only "set out"; Readable follows the English Readable's requests, with the humble 申し上げる for the speaker's own act.
- [ ] [PSA 6:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/6/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-hasheminit "シェミニトに合わせて" (L, B), "「第八」という調べに合わせる" (R), as 1 Chronicles 15:21 — The term is a musical direction whose sense is uncertain (m3); Literal and Balanced transliterate it as the English does, and Readable translates it in quotation marks as the English Readable does, in the form 1 Chronicles settled.
- [ ] [PSA 7:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/7/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · shiggayon "シガヨン" (L, B), "歌" (R); Kush ben-Yemini "ベニヤミン人クシュ" (L), "ベニヤミン族の人クシュ" (B), "ベニヤミン族のクシュ" (R); for the Psalter, miktam "ミクタム" and maskil "マスキル" alike — The kind of song is unknown (m2), so it is transliterated where the English transliterates it, and Readable says 歌 as the English Readable does. Cush is not otherwise known (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 7:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/7/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · God as the subject of the weapons (その方 L, 神 B, R), with respect forms; shuv "立ち返る" (L, B), "悪から離れる" (R) — m1 reads God readying his weapons against the one who will not repent (uncertain); the respect forms make the subject God in Japanese, where the Hebrew has only "he".
- [ ] [PSA 8:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/8/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-haggittit "ギティトに合わせて" (L, B), "「ギティト」に合わせる" (R, in quotation marks as the English Readable); tune and instrument names follow the English level in this way … — The word may name an instrument or tune from Gath (m3); it is transliterated, and Readable marks it as a name as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [PSA 8:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/8/#v2) L · word_choice · 'asher tenah hodekha "あなたは、その威光を天の上に置かれた" (L), as the English Literal reads the hard clause — The construction is uncertain (m4); Literal takes it as a statement of what God has done, Balanced and Readable as the English do.
- [ ] [PSA 8:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/8/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · me'elohim "神より", not Hebrews 2:7's "天使たちよりも"; "栄光と誉れの冠をかぶらせ" (B) as Hebrews 2:7 B — Hebrews quotes the Greek translation ("angels"); the meaning decision keeps "God" with a footnote on heavenly beings, so the Japanese follows the Hebrew there and shares Hebrews' words only for the crown, where they agree.
- [ ] [PSA 9:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/9/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-mut labben "ムト・ラベンに合わせて" (L, B), "「ムト・ラベン」という調べに合わせる" (R) — Probably the name of a tune ("death of the son", m3, uncertain); transliterated as the English keeps it, with Readable naming it a tune as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [PSA 9:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/9/#v7) L · word_order · Literal keeps the broken, emphatic sentence with a dash (敵は――) and the closing "彼らの[記憶]は" — m1 reads a broken sentence (uncertain); Balanced and Readable say it smoothly as the English does.
- [ ] [PSA 9:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/9/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · higgayon "ヒガヨン" at every level, before セラ, as the English keeps it — A musical direction whose sense is uncertain (m2), transliterated like Selah.
- [ ] [PSA 10:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/10/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · botsea' berekh "[自分を]祝福し" (L), "自分を祝福する" (B), "自分をほめる" (R), as the English reads it — The verb may mean blessing himself or (as a euphemism) cursing (m1, uncertain); the Japanese follows the English reading and does not choose 呪う.
- [ ] [PSA 10:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/10/#v6) L · word_order · "代々にわたって、わたしは災いに遭わない" (L) for the hard line — The Hebrew line is uncertain (m2); Literal follows the English Literal's reading.
- [ ] [PSA 10:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/10/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · chelekhah "弱い者" (L), "助けのない人" (B), R as the English; also 10:10, 10:14 — A word found only in this psalm (m2, uncertain); the English helpless is followed at each level.
- [ ] [PSA 12:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/12/#v7) L/B · word_sense · "地にある炉" (L), "土の炉" (B) — m2: a furnace on the ground or one of clay; each level follows its English reading.
- [ ] [PSA 16:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/16/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · miktam "ミクタム" (L, B), "歌" (R), as the Psalter's titles; El "神よ"; the request "守ってください" — The kind of psalm is unknown (m2), so Literal and Balanced transliterate it as the English does; requests to God are 〜てください in the Psalter.
- [ ] [PSA 17:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/17/#v14) L/B · word_order · The rescue of 17:13 runs on into 17:14 at Literal ("人々から…") as the English Literal; Balanced repeats the request (救い出してください), and the rest is set as new … — Japanese cannot hang a long 〜から phrase after a closed request; repeating the verb keeps 17:14's content in its own verse.
- [ ] [PSA 18:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/18/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · mehullal "ほめたたえられるべき方" (L), "ほめたたえられるにふさわしい" (B), "賛美を受けるにふさわしい" (R), as 2 Samuel 22:4 — The same Hebrew as 2 Samuel 22:4 keeps its Japanese; for a reviewer: the Psalter elsewhere keeps hallel (賛美する) apart from barekh (ほめたたえる).
- [ ] [PSA 18:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/18/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · 'anvatekha "あなたのへりくだり" (L), "身をかがめてくださった" (B, R), where 2 Samuel 22:36 reads "your answer" (応え) — God stooping down to help (m1); Literal keeps the noun.
- [ ] [PSA 19:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/19/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · qav "綱" (L, B), "知らせ" (R as the English); "その言葉は世界の果てにまで" as Romans 10:18, which quotes the verse — The Hebrew has a measuring line (m1) where Paul's Greek has "their voice", so Literal and Balanced keep the line; the second half reads as Romans in Japanese (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 19:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/19/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · piqqudim "命令" (L, B), "命じられること" (R); mitsvah "戒め" (L, B), "命令" (R, as Deuteronomy) — Settled here for the Psalter's law words beside torah 教え, 'edut 証し / さとし, mishpatim 掟 and chuqqim 定め; "諭し" is avoided because Balanced already uses さとし for 'edut.
- [ ] [PSA 20:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/20/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · hammelekh "王" read as God, with "答えてくださる" — The English reads "save! May the King answer us" with King for God (m1); the respect form makes that clear in Japanese, and Readable names God as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [PSA 21:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/21/#v9) L/B/R · register · The king is addressed with plain verbs (見つけ出す, 捕らえる), not the respect forms used for God — 21:9-13 are read as spoken to the king (m1, uncertain); the plain verbs keep the reading of the English without deciding more than it does.
- [ ] [PSA 22:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/22/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-'ayyelet hashshachar "アイェレト・ハシャハルに合わせて" (L, B), "「夜明けの雌鹿」という調べに合わせる" (R), as the Psalter's tune names — Literal and Balanced transliterate as the English does; Readable translates the probable tune name in quotation marks as the English Readable (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 22:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/22/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · The opening cry in the words of the Japanese of Matthew 27:46 and Mark 15:34 at each level ("わたしの神よ、わたしの神よ、なぜわたしを見捨てたのか？", R "どうしてわたしをひとりぼっちにしたのか？"), with a … — The Hebrew says what the Gospels translate (m1); a reader who hears Jesus' cry should find the same words in the psalm, so the Psalter's respect form (見捨てられた) gives way to the Gospel wording.
- [ ] [PSA 22:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/22/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · ka'ari "獅子のように" with the English, not "刺し貫いた" — The meaning decision follows the Hebrew text as handed down (m1, uncertain, with a footnote); Japanese supplies the verb in brackets at Literal as the English does.
- [ ] [PSA 22:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/22/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · 'anitani "あなたはわたしに答えてくださった" as a past act (the turning point); re'emim "野牛" — The meaning decision reads the verb as "you have answered me" (m1, uncertain); 野牛 as Numbers and Deuteronomy.
- [ ] [PSA 23:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/23/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · nafshi yeshovev "わたしの魂を…連れ戻し" (L), "わたしに命を取り戻させ" (B), "もう一度元気にしてくださる" (R) — The meaning decision (m1, uncertain) reads it as giving new life, while the verb can also mean bringing back a straying sheep; Literal keeps the verb's picture (連れ戻す), Balanced and Readable say the sense, as the English does.
- [ ] [PSA 23:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/23/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · tsalmavet "深い闇" (L), "深い闇に閉ざされた" (B), "とても暗くて危ない" (R), not "死の陰" — The meaning decision (m1, footnoted) reads one word, deep darkness, as the English does; the two-word reading, shadow of death, is the footnote's alternative and the familiar church phrase.
- [ ] [PSA 23:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/23/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · yirdefuni "追いかける" / "追いかけて来る" at every level; veshavti "戻って[住む]" (L), "帰って来て" (B), "戻って住む" (R) — The Hebrew says goodness and chesed pursue him (m1), a stronger picture than following; for the verb the Hebrew text reads "I will return" (m3, footnoted), so Literal and Balanced say return, and Readable joins return and live, as the English. The psalm speaks of God in the third person again …
- [ ] [PSA 24:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/24/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · nafshi "わたしの魂" (L), "神ご自身の命" (B), "神の名" (R) — The Hebrew text has "my soul", the LORD speaking of himself (m2, uncertain, footnoted): Literal keeps it, Balanced and Readable say what it means, as the English (r-en-1); the people are 人たち with no gender span (r-en-2).
- [ ] [PSA 24:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/24/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · dor "世代" (L), "このような人たち" (B, R); "[これが]ヤコブ[である]" (L), "これがヤコブだ" (B), "本当のヤコブの家族" (R) — The generation is a kind of people (m3), and those who seek God are the true Jacob (m2, uncertain, footnoted), as the English reads it.
- [ ] [PSA 24:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/24/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · Selah "セラ" at every level, straight after the closing 。, as the Psalter's convention — Its meaning is no longer known (m4); it is kept as it is at the end of the verse's last line, as from Psalm 3.
- [ ] [PSA 25:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/25/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'emet "まこと" (L, B), "真実" (R) — The word is God's faithfulness and his reliable word (m1, uncertain); the Psalter's まこと covers both at Literal and Balanced, and Readable says 真実 as the English "truth".
- [ ] [PSA 25:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/25/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · hirchivu "広がった" (L), "ますます大きくなった" (B, R) — The text reads "they have made wide", that is, the troubles have increased (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the other reading, "relieve the troubles of my heart", is the footnote's.
- [ ] [PSA 25:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/25/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · tom vayosher "潔白とまっすぐさ" (L), "潔白と正しさ" (B), "正直で、正しく生きること" (R) — The pair are pictured as guards (m1, uncertain); 潔白 keeps tom apart from chesed (誠実) and from tamim's 無垢 (Psalm 15:2).
- [ ] [PSA 27:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · bezot "それでも" (L), "なお" (B), not translated as a word (R) — "In this" is read as even so, in spite of this (m2, uncertain), as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 27:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · no'am "麗しさ" (L, B), "どれほど良い方で、美しい方か" (R); levaqqer "尋ね求める" (L, B), "どうすればよいかを…尋ねる" (R) — No'am is God's pleasantness and kindness (m1); the last verb is read as seeking God's answer, with reflecting as the other reading (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 27:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v8) L/B/R · register · God's words quoted by the heart, "わたしの顔を求めなさい" (L, B), "わたしのところに来なさい" (R), with God's command form 〜なさい — The quoted words are God's own, repeated by the heart (m1, uncertain, footnoted); God's commands to people are 〜なさい in the Psalter. To seek the face is to come into God's presence (m2), which Readable says as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 27:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · ki "まことに…見捨てた" (L), "たとえ…見捨てても" (B, R) — Literal follows the Hebrew perfect; Balanced and Readable read it as the worst that could happen (m1, uncertain), as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 27:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · vifeach chamas "暴虐を吐く[者]" (L), "暴虐の息を吐く者たち" (B), "わたしを傷つけようとしている" (R) — The word is read as breathing out violence (m3, uncertain; it may also mean a witness), as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 27:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/27/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · lule' "もし…信じていなかったなら――" (L, broken off), "…わたしはどうなっていただろうか？" (B), "そうでなかったら、わたしはあきらめていただろう" (R) — The sentence is left unfinished in the Hebrew (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Literal breaks off with ――, Balanced and Readable supply the end as the English does (r-en-1). Tuv YHWH 恵み深さ as 25:7.
- [ ] [PSA 28:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/28/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · lamo "彼らの" (L, B), "ご自分の民" (R) — The Hebrew text has "for them" (m1, uncertain, footnoted; other witnesses read "for his people"), and Readable supplies the people as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 29:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/29/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · benei 'elim "神々の子らよ" (L), "天の者たちよ" (B), "天に住むすべての者たちよ" (R) — The phrase is read as the heavenly beings around God's throne (m2, uncertain, footnoted); Literal keeps the Hebrew words, Balanced and Readable say who they are, as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 29:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/29/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · behadrat-qodesh "聖なる輝きのうちに" (L), "聖なる輝きの中で" (B, R) — Read as holy splendor (m1, uncertain, footnoted; it may also mean holy attire), as the English at every level.
- [ ] [PSA 29:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/29/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ayyalot "雌鹿" (L, B), "鹿" (R) — Read as female deer (m1, uncertain, footnoted; with other vowels "oaks", which would make the line "shakes the oaks"), as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 30:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/30/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · mizmor shir chanukkat habbayit "賛歌。家の奉献の歌。" (L), "賛歌。家の奉献のための歌。" (B), "家が神にささげられたときのための歌。" (R); ledavid at the end "ダビデによる" — The title is its own verse, in the Hebrew order and with the English's pieces; the house is probably the temple (m2, uncertain), so Japanese keeps 家 as the English keeps "the house". mizmor 賛歌 and shir 歌 as the Psalter's titles; Readable has one 歌, as the English Readable one "song".
- [ ] [PSA 30:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/30/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · rafa' "癒やされた" (L), "癒やしてくださった" (B), "いやし、もう一度元気にしてくださった" (R) — Healed means made well (m1, uncertain: from illness or from danger); 癒やす / いやす as the Psalter's rafa'.
- [ ] [PSA 30:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/30/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · chayyim birtsono "その恵みのうちに命" (L, B), "その方のやさしさは命を与える" (R); ratson "恵み" (L, B), "やさしさ" (R) — Life is read as life itself (m1, uncertain, footnoted; it may be "a lifetime", against the moment of anger); ratson as the Psalter's convention.
- [ ] [PSA 30:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/30/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの山を力強く立たせられた" (L), "山のように揺るぎなくしてくださった" (B), "山のように強くなった気がした" (R) — The hard Hebrew is read as "made me secure" (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Literal keeps the Hebrew words, Balanced and Readable the English simile.
- [ ] [PSA 30:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/30/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · kavod "[わたしの]栄光" (L), "わたしのすべて" (B), "心を込めて" (R) — Glory is read as the inner self, the whole being (m1, uncertain, footnoted), as the English; Literal keeps the word with the supplied わたしの.
- [ ] [PSA 31:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · lamnatseach "指揮する者のために" (L), "音楽を導く者のために" (B), "音楽を導く人のために" (R) — As the Psalter's titles; the sense of the term is uncertain (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 31:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · sal'i "わたしの岩山" (L, B), "わたしの岩" (R); "あなたこそ" (B) for the emphatic 'attah; the verbs as confidence, "導き…てくださる" — Sela' 岩山 (L, B) as the Psalter's refuge words; the pronoun is emphatic (m1); the verbs are read as confidence rather than request (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 31:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · sane'ti "わたしは…憎む" (every level); havlei-shav' "むなしい偶像" (L), "役に立たない偶像" (B), "助けにならない偶像" (R) — The Hebrew text has "I hate" (m1, uncertain, footnoted; some witnesses read "you hate"); the empty vanities are worthless idols (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 31:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · ba'avoni "わたしの咎のために" (L), "わたしの咎が" (B), "わたしの罪のせいで" (R) — The Hebrew text has "my iniquity" (m1, uncertain, footnoted; the Greek reads "my affliction"); 'avon 咎 (L, B), 罪 (R).
- [ ] [PSA 31:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · cherpah "そしり" (L), "物笑いの種" (B), "ばかにする" (R); me'od "はなはだしく" (L), "とりわけ" (B), "なおさら" (R) — A reproach is an object of scorn (m1); me'od is read as "above all" (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 31:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · magor missaviv "周りはすべて恐怖！" (L), "どこもかしこも恐怖だ！" (B), "どこへ行っても、わたしはこわい" (R), set off with ―― as the English sets it off with dashes — The phrase is Jeremiah's (m1) and is read as an outcry (m2, uncertain; it may be what the enemies whisper); Readable makes it the psalmist's own fear, as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 31:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · rukhsei 'ish "人々の謀りごと" (L), "人の企み" (B), "傷つけようと計画する人たち" (R), with no gender span; sukkah "仮庵" (L), "隠れ家" (B), "安全な場所" (R), as 27:5 — The plots are schemes (m1, uncertain); 'ish is people in general, so no span; the shelter is a booth, a covered place (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 31:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/31/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ir matsor "包囲された町" (L, B), "敵に囲まれた町" (R) — Read as a city under siege, hemmed in by enemies (m1, uncertain; it may be a figure for his distress); barukh as the Psalter's convention.
- [ ] [PSA 32:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · maskil "マスキル" (L, B), "教えるための歌" (R) — As the Psalter's titles: the sense is probably a skillful or instructive song (m2, uncertain), transliterated at Literal and Balanced, explained at Readable as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 32:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · leshaddi "わたしの潤い" (L), "わたしの中の命" (B), "わたしの力" (R) — The word is read as sap, the moisture and vigor of life (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 32:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · le'et metso' "見いだされる時に" (L), "あなたが見いだされる時に" (B), "まだあなたを見つけることができるうちに" (R); "その人" with no gender span — Read as while God may be found (m2, uncertain), as the English; the English Literal span on them becomes その人.
- [ ] [PSA 32:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · ronnei fallet "救いの喜びの叫び" (L), "救われた喜びの歌声" (B), "喜びの声" (R) — Shouts of deliverance are songs of joy for rescue (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 32:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v8) L/B/R · register · God speaking, plain: "わたし…あなた", 〜よう / 〜ない — The speaker is read as God (m1, uncertain, footnoted; it may be the psalmist teaching); as the Psalter's God speaking, plain with あなた. There are no quotation marks, as in the English.
- [ ] [PSA 32:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/32/#v9) L/B/R · register · 'al-tihyu "〜になるな" (L), "〜であってはならない" (B), "〜であってはいけない" (R) — Spoken to all who listen (m1), so the Psalter's prohibitions to people apply; the harness and the last clause are uncertain (m2, m3).
- [ ] [PSA 33:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/33/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · kinnor "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R); nevel 'asor "十[弦]の琴" (L), "十弦の琴" (B), "十本の弦のある琴" (R) — The instrument names as 1 Samuel 10:5 and 1 Chronicles; "of ten" is read as ten strings (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 33:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/33/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · tseva'am "その万象" (L), "天に連なるすべての星" (B), "すべての星" (R) — Their host is read as the stars (m3, uncertain), as the English; the line recalls Genesis 1 (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 33:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/33/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · kanned "山のように" (every level); tehomot "深淵" (L), "大海の深み" (B), "深い海" (R) — The Hebrew vowels read "like a heap" (m1, uncertain, footnoted; the ancient versions read "as in a wineskin"); the storehouses show the waters under God's control (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 34:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/34/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · hibbitu "仰ぎ見て"; naharu "輝いた" (L), "喜びに輝く" (B), "喜びで顔が明るくなる" (R) — The Hebrew has "they looked" and "their faces" (m1, with a variant "look to him" in some witnesses, uncertain); Literal keeps "they", Balanced and Readable say "those who look" as the English; radiant means shining with joy (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 34:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/34/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · ra'ah "悪" (L), "自分の悪" (B), "自分がした悪" (R) — The evil may be their own wickedness or the disaster it brings (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the bare word, Balanced and Readable follow the English (their own evil).
- [ ] [PSA 35:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · segor "斧" (every level), uncertain — The word is a weapon, probably a battle-axe (m1, uncertain, footnoted in English); 斧 follows the English.
- [ ] [PSA 35:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · shachat rishtam "彼らの網の穴" (L), "穴に網を隠した" (B, R) — A hunter's trap of pit and net (m1); the Hebrew order is hard and may have been disturbed (m2, uncertain); Balanced and Readable follow the English sense. shachat here is a trap, 穴 (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 35:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · shekhol "子を失った悲しみ" (L), "子を失った者のように" (B), "子どもを亡くした親のように" (R) — The word is the loss of children (m1, uncertain), the image of utter desolation.
- [ ] [PSA 35:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · tefillati 'al-cheqi tashuv "わたしの祈りはわたしの胸に帰って来た" (L), "頭を胸に垂れて祈った" (B), "頭を下げて…祈った" (R) — The idiom is uncertain (m3, footnoted): most likely he prayed with his head bowed on his chest; Literal keeps the image, Balanced and Readable the likely sense as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 35:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · nekhim "打つ者たち" (L), "襲う者たち" (B), "人を襲う者たち" (R); velo yada'ti "わたしは知らなかった" (L), "わたしが知らないうちに" (B), "わたしの知らない人たち" (R) — Both words are uncertain (m1, m2): nekhim is read as attackers, and "I did not know" as without my knowing (B) or people I did not know (R), each level as its English.
- [ ] [PSA 35:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/35/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · chanfei la'agei ma'og "菓子のために嘲る、神を敬わない者たち" (L), "ひと口の食べ物のために人を嘲る、神を敬わない道化者" (B), "食事にありつくために人をばかにする、神を敬わない者たち" (R) — The phrase is obscure (m1, uncertain, footnoted): read as godless jesters who mock for a bite of food, as the English at each level.
- [ ] [PSA 36:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/36/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · ne'um-pesha' "背きの託宣" (L); libbi "わたしの心" at every level — ne'um is a solemn utterance (m2), here what rebellion says; the Masoretic "my heart" is kept (m1, uncertain; other witnesses read "his heart").
- [ ] [PSA 36:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/36/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "自分の咎を見いだして憎むことが[できないほどである]" (L), "自分の咎に気づいて、それを憎むことがない" (B) — The Hebrew clause is hard (m1, uncertain, footnoted); it is read as "too much to see his guilt and hate it", the bracket at Literal supplying what the Hebrew leaves out. 'avon 咎 (L, B), 罪 (R), as PSA-3.
- [ ] [PSA 37:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · re'eh 'emunah "真実を食め" (L), "真実を糧としなさい" (B), "真実をあなたの食べ物としなさい" (R) — The phrase is uncertain (m1, footnoted): feed securely on what is reliable, or "cultivate faithfulness"; the English reading is followed, with 'emunah 真実 (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 37:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · rasha'im rabbim "多くの悪しき者たち" (every level) — "Many" may go with the wicked or with their wealth (m1, uncertain); the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [PSA 37:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · kiqar karim "牧場の輝き" (L), "野に咲く花" (B), "すぐにしおれる野の花" (R) — The phrase is uncertain (m1, footnoted): the splendor of the pastures, flowers that soon fade (or "the fat of lambs"); the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [PSA 37:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "その方" (L, B), "神" (R) for the Hebrew "his" — "His" refers to the LORD (m1, uncertain); Japanese avoids 彼 for God, so その方 (L, B), and Readable names God as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 37:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "その方はその人の道を喜ばれる" (L), "…喜ばれるときである" (B), "…喜ばれるとき" (R) — It is uncertain who delights in whose way (m1, footnoted); the English reading (the LORD is pleased with the person's way) is followed, as a separate clause at Literal and a condition at Balanced and Readable. geber 人, with no gender span for the English {{gender:...}} (L).
- [ ] [PSA 37:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · le'olam nishmaru "彼らはとこしえに守られる" (every level) — The Hebrew as written is followed (m2, uncertain, footnoted); the acrostic suggests a lost line here.
- [ ] [PSA 37:35](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v35) L/B/R · word_choice · ke'ezrach ra'anan "生い茂る土着の[木]" (L), "生まれた土地に根を張った、葉の茂る木" (B), "自分の土地で育った緑の茂った木" (R) — The word is uncertain (m1, footnoted): a tree rooted in its own soil (the Greek reads "cedars of Lebanon"); the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [PSA 37:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · vayya'avor "彼は過ぎ去り" (L), "その者は過ぎ去り" (B); vehinneh "なんと" (L), dropped (B, R) — The Hebrew "he passed away" is kept (m1, uncertain; other witnesses read "I passed by"); hinneh is never 見よ (PSA-1): なんと at Literal, dropped at Balanced and Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 37:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/37/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · 'acharit "未来" (L), "先の望み" (B), "良い未来" (R); 'ish shalom "平和の人" (L), "平和に生きる人" (B, R) — The future is a good outcome, and perhaps descendants (m1, uncertain); 'ish shalom is one who lives in peace and wholeness (m2). 人 with no gender span for the English {{gender:the person|the man}} (L).
- [ ] [PSA 38:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/38/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · lehazkir "思い起こしていただくために" (L), "記念のために" (B), "思い出していただくために" (R); also for Psalm 70:1 — The term is uncertain (m2): to call to mind before God, perhaps at the memorial offering; the humble form いただく shows that it is God who is asked to remember. 1CH 16:4's lehazkir 呼び求める is the Levites' task in a sentence, not a title, so the title follows the English sense.
- [ ] [PSA 38:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/38/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · kesalai "わたしの腰" (L), "わたしの脇腹" (B), "わたしの体" (R); niqleh "燃えるような[痛み]" (L), "燃えるような熱" (B) — The kesalim are the muscles of the back and sides (m1); the burning is inflammation or fever (m2, uncertain). The last clause repeats 38:4 (m3) and keeps its wording.
- [ ] [PSA 38:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/38/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · chayyim "生き生きとして" (L), "命にあふれ" (B), "元気で" (R) — The Hebrew "living" is read as alive and well (m1, uncertain, footnoted; a small change gives "without cause", as 35:19).
- [ ] [PSA 39:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/39/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · hecheshesheti mittov "善いことからさえ口を閉ざした" (L), "良いことさえ口にせず" (B, R) — The phrase is uncertain (m1, footnoted): silent even from good words, or silent with no good result; the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [PSA 39:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/39/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · hevel "息" at every level ("息にすぎない" L, B, "息のようなものだ" R), as ECC; Selah "セラ" (uncertain) — hevel is breath, vapor, what passes at once (m2); Ecclesiastes' 息 (not 空しい) is kept for the same word, also in 39:7, 12. Selah is written セラ after the last 。 as the Psalter (m4, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 39:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/39/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · nig'ekha "あなたの打撃" (L), "わたしを打たれるあなたの手" (B); tigrat yadekha "あなたの手の攻め" (L), "あなたの手の激しさ" (B) — nega' is a blow, a stroke (m1); tigrah is attack or strife (m2, uncertain); Balanced follows the English "force".
- [ ] [PSA 39:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/39/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · hasha' "目をそらしてください" (L), "目を離してください" (B), "見つめるのを、やめてください" (R) — "Look away" asks God to turn his searching gaze away (m1, uncertain); Readable adds "in anger" as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 40:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · bor sha'on "騒ぎの穴" (L), "とどろく滅びの穴" (B), "水がごうごうと鳴る深い穴" (R) — sha'on is the din of rushing water, or ruin (m1, uncertain); the pit and the mire mean being near to death (m2). bor is 穴 (L, B) as the Psalter; Readable follows the English "deep, noisy pit" rather than 死の穴, since the picture is spelled out. sela' 岩 here, not 岩山, because the point is firm footing.
- [ ] [PSA 40:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · rehavim "高ぶる者たち"; satei khazav "偽りに迷い出る者たち" (L), "偽りを追って迷い出る者たち" (B), "偽りの神々に向かう人たち" (R) — People who rely on themselves or on lies (m3, uncertain); Readable says false gods as the English Readable.
- [ ] [PSA 40:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · nifle'otekha "奇しいみわざ" (L), "不思議なみわざ" (B), "驚くようなこと" (R), as 9:2 — Deeds only God can do (m1); the Psalter's wording of 9:2 is kept. 'en 'arokh elekha "あなたに並ぶものはない" follows the English (m2, uncertain; also "none can set them out before you").
- [ ] [PSA 40:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · 'aznayim karita li "耳を、あなたはわたしのために掘られた" (L), "わたしの耳を開いてくださった" (B), "聞いて従えるようにしてくださった" (R); not HEB 10:5's "体を備えてくださった" — The Hebrew says ears were dug, i.e. God made me able to hear and obey (m1, uncertain); Hebrews 10:5 quotes the Greek "a body you prepared for me" (m3, footnoted), so the Japanese follows the Hebrew here while sharing Hebrews' words for the offerings (いけにえと供え物, 焼き尽くすささげ物, 罪のための[ささげ物] L, 罪を償うささげ物 B).
- [ ] [PSA 40:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · katuv 'alai "わたしについて書かれている" (L, as HEB 10:7), "わたしがなすべきことは…記されている" (B), "わたしがすべきことが書いてある" (R) — "Written concerning me" may be "written for me to do" (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Literal keeps the words Hebrews uses, Balanced and Readable the English sense.
- [ ] [PSA 40:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · lo-tikhla' "押しとどめることはなさらない" (L), "差し控えることはなさらない" (B), "いつまでも…優しくしてくださる" (R) — The verb answers 40:10 "I do not hold back my lips" (m1); Literal uses the same 押しとどめる so the play shows. It may be a statement of trust or a prayer (m2, uncertain); the English statement is followed. rachamim あわれみ (L), 深いあわれみ (B) as PSA-3.
- [ ] [PSA 40:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "見ることができない" (L), "何も見えない" (B), "どこへ行けばよいのかわからない" (R); libbi 'azavani "わたしの心はわたしを見捨てた" (L), "気力を失ってしまった" (B), "勇気をなくしてしまった" (R) — Not able to see means overwhelmed (m2, uncertain); my heart has forsaken me means my courage is gone (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 40:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · yigdal YHWH "DNは大いなる方！" (L), "DNはなんと偉大な方か！" (B), "DNは偉大な方だ！" (R), as 35:27; for Psalm 70:5 — Praise or a wish (m1, uncertain), as the English an exclamation; the clause "those who love your salvation" comes before the quotation in Japanese for natural order. Psalm 70:5 has Elohim for YHWH: its drafter writes 神は大いなる方！ and keeps the rest.
- [ ] [PSA 40:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/40/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ani ve'evyon "苦しむ者、乏しい者" (L), "貧しく、乏しい者" (B), "貧しく、困っている" (R); 'Adonai yachashov li "主がわたしを心に留めてくださるように" (L, B); for Psalm 70:6 — The Psalter's 'ani / 'evyon (PSA-1; B by the English "poor"); "will think of me" is read as a wish (m2, uncertain), 'Adonai 主 with no span (m3). Psalm 70:6 reads Elohim chushah li (神よ、わたしのところに急いで来てください) and YHWH 'al te'achar (DNよ、遅れないでください): its drafter keeps the rest.
- [ ] [PSA 41:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/41/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "その寝床のすべてを、あなたは変えられた" (L), "病の床を、すっかり健やかなものに変えてくださる" (B), "もう一度元気にしてくださる" (R) — The idiom is read as restoring the sick to health (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the image, Balanced and Readable say what it means, as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 41:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/41/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · yitlachashu "ささやき合い" (L), "ひそひそと話し合い" (B), "ひそひそと話す" (R) — Low talk, perhaps spells (m1, uncertain); the Japanese keeps the plain sense of whispering.
- [ ] [PSA 41:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/41/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · devar-beliyya'al "よこしまなもの" (L), "何か死に至るもの" (B), "何かひどいもの" (R) — A deadly disease or a curse (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the vague "worthless, wicked thing" (beliyya'al ならず者 elsewhere is for people).
- [ ] [PSA 41:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/41/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "かかとを高く上げた" (L), "わたしに逆らってかかとを上げた" (B), "わたしに背いた" (R) — John 13:18 quotes this line (m4); Balanced and Readable follow the John Japanese at each level (B "わたしに逆らってかかとを上げた", R "わたしに背いた"), Literal keeps the Hebrew "made great the heel" (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 41:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/41/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · va'ashallemah "報いよう" (L), "報いることができる" (B), "仕返しができる" (R, as the English R "pay them back") — Read as justice rather than private revenge (m2, uncertain); the Japanese follows the English level's strength.
- [ ] [PSA 42:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "コラの子らのマスキル" (L), "コラの子孫のマスキル" (B), "コラの一族の、教えと悟りを与える歌" (R) — The Psalter's title forms (PSA-1): benei Qorach コラの子ら / コラの子孫 / コラの一族, maskil kept as マスキル at L and B (m3, uncertain), explained at R as the English R. Psalms 42 and 43 are one poem (m5) and share one wording for the refrain (42:6, 42:12, 43:5).
- [ ] [PSA 42:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · ta'arog "あえぐ" (L, B), "探し求める" / "求めている" (R); 'ayyal "鹿" (L, R), "雌鹿" (B, as the English B) — The verb is a thirsty crying out (m1); the deer may be a doe (m2, uncertain); nafshi is the whole self (m3), "わたしの魂" at L, "わたしは全身で" at B. Worded afresh, avoiding the familiar church phrasing of this verse.
- [ ] [PSA 42:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "神の顔の前に出る" (L), "神の顔の前に立てる" (B) — The reading "appear before the face of God" (m2, uncertain, footnoted) is the one the English follows; Readable says it as being with God at his house, as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 42:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの魂を注ぎ出す" (L), "心を注ぎ出す" (B), "心は張り裂けそうになる" (R); "彼らを導いて…練り歩いた" (L) — Pouring out the soul is letting out all one's grief (m1); the procession words are uncertain (m2) and kept general.
- [ ] [PSA 42:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · yeshu'ot panav "その顔の救いのゆえに" (L), "その方の顔から来る救いのゆえに" (B), "わたしと共にいて…救ってくださる" (R) — The Masoretic "his face" here, against "my face and my God" in 42:12 and 43:5 (m3, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [PSA 42:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · Hermonim "ヘルモンの[峰々]" (L), "ヘルモンの峰々" (B), "ヘルモン山" (R); Mits'ar "ミツァル" — ヘルモン as in the earlier books; Mizar is a hill whose place is unknown (m2, uncertain), transcribed from the Hebrew; Readable adds "far away in the north" as the English R (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 42:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · tehom "淵" (L), "深い淵" (B), "深い水" (R); tsinnorekha "あなたの滝" — The great deep waters (m1); the rare word is read as waterfalls (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 42:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · chasdo "その誠実" (L), "変わらぬいつくしみ" (B, R); yetsavveh "命じられ" (L), "送ってくださり" (B) — The Psalter's hesed forms (PSA-1); the verb is read as a statement, not a wish (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 42:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/42/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの骨が砕かれるようにして" (L), "骨まで砕くようなあざけり" (B), "骨が折れたかのように…痛む" (R) — Pain that goes to the bone (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 44:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/44/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "コラの子らによる" (L), "コラの子孫による" (B), "コラの一族による" (R); maskil "マスキル" (L, B), "教えと悟りを与える歌" (R) — The Psalter's title forms (PSA-1), as Psalm 42:1; maskil uncertain (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 44:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/44/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "彼らを植えられた" / "彼らを広げられた" (L), with "わたしたちの先祖" (B) and "わたしたちの民" (R) supplied as the English — God's power settled Israel in the land (m1); sending them out is read as letting them spread like branches (m2, uncertain). goyim 諸国の民, 'ummim もろもろの民 (L, B) by the Psalter's forms.
- [ ] [PSA 44:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/44/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · nodeh "感謝する" (L), "感謝をささげる" (B), "賛美する" (R, as the English R praise); Selah "セラ" — hodah 感謝する by the Psalter's praise words, the English R's "praise" followed at R; Selah as PSA-1 (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 44:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/44/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · tannim "ジャッカル" (L, B), "野犬" (R); tsalmavet "死の陰" (L), "死のような闇" (B, R) — A place of jackals is a ruin (m1, uncertain); tsalmavet by context (PSA-1): darkness like death (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 45:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-shoshannim "ショシャニムに合わせて" (L, B), "「ゆり」という調べに合わせる" (R); shir yedidot "愛の歌" (every level) — Tune names as the Psalter's convention (PSA-1): transliterated where the English level transliterates, translated in 「」 where it translates (m3, uncertain). A wedding song for the king (m5).
- [ ] [PSA 45:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · rachash "わき立つ" (L, B), "いっぱいだ" (R); ma'asai "わたしの作品" (L), "わたしの歌" (B), "この歌" (R) — The heart bubbles over (m1, uncertain); the poet speaks of his poem (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 45:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'anvah-tsedeq "へりくだりと義" (L, B); "右の手が…教えるように" (L, B), "力強い腕は…成し遂げる" (R) — The pair is hard (m2, uncertain) and read as two causes, as the English; 'anvah as in 18:36 (PSA-2). The right hand teaching awesome deeds is the king's strength showing him great victories (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 45:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · Word for word as Hebrews 1:8 at each level ("神よ、あなたの王座は世々限りなく[続き]…") — Hebrews quotes these verses (m2) and the Hebrew agrees with its Greek, so the Hebrews Japanese is followed; the king is addressed as God (m1, uncertain, footnoted), kept as "神よ" as in Hebrews.
- [ ] [PSA 45:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · As Hebrews 1:9 ("あなたは義を愛し…", "神、あなたの神は", "喜びの油を注"), but resha' "悪" (not Hebrews' "不法") and the respect form "注がれた" for God — The Hebrews Japanese is followed where the Hebrew agrees (m2 of 45:7); resha' is the Psalter's word for wickedness, and God's act takes the Psalter's light respect form where Hebrews' quotation is plain. "God, your God" (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 45:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · mor "没薬", 'ahalot "沈香" (as JHN 19:39), qetsi'ot "桂皮" (L, B); "よい香りの香料" (R); minni "弦の音" (L, B), "音楽" (R) — Costly perfumes (m1), in the forms of the earlier books; strings read as stringed instruments (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 45:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · bat-Tsor "ティルスの娘" (L, B), "ティルスの人々" (R); Tsor "ティルス" as in the earlier books — The daughter of Tyre is read as the people of Tyre (m1, uncertain); entreating the face is seeking favor (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 45:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · lakh "あなたのもとへ" (L, B), "王よ、あなたのところへ" (R) — "To you" is the king (m2, uncertain), named at Readable as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 45:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/45/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · kol-ha'arets "全地" (L, B), "国じゅう" (R); sarim "君" (L, B), "指導者" (R) — "The land" may be Israel or the whole earth (m2, uncertain); Readable follows the English R's "everywhere in the land".

- [ ] [PSA 46:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/46/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'Elyon "いと高き方" (L, B), "すべてのものの上におられる神" (R); mishkenei "住まい" — The Psalter's form for Elyon (PSA-1); God's dwelling place (m2, uncertain).

Desolations (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 46:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/46/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · 'agalot "車" (L), "戦いの車" (B, R) — Wagons, here read as wagons of war (m1, uncertain, footnoted), kept apart from rekhev 戦車.
- [ ] [PSA 47:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/47/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · Present "従わせられる" (L), "置かれる" (B); past at R as the English R — What God did for Israel, the tense uncertain (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 47:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/47/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · maskil "マスキル" (L), "巧みな歌" (B), "よくできた賛美の歌" (R) — A skillful or teaching song (m1, uncertain); Balanced and Readable translate it as the English do here.
- [ ] [PSA 47:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/47/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · 'am 'Elohei 'Avraham "アブラハムの神の民[として]" (L), "…の民となる" (B), "…の民に加わる" (R); maginnei-'erets "地の盾" (L, B), "世界の支配者たち" (R) — What the nobles become (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the shields of the earth are its rulers (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 48:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "その聖なる山で" closes 48:2 at every level — The verse division is uncertain (m2); the English reading, with the holy mountain at the end of 48:2, is kept. Worthy of all praise (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 48:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · yarketei tsafon "ツァフォンの果て" (L), "まことのツァフォンのはるかな高み" (B), "本当の神の山" (R); qiryat melekh rav "大いなる王の都" (L), "偉大な王の都" (B, R) as MAT 5:35 — Zaphon as ISA 14:13 (ISA-1 notes), here the true mountain of God (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Jesus quotes "the city of the great King" (m2), and the Matthew Japanese is followed at each level.
- [ ] [PSA 48:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · hinneh "ああ" (L), "見なさい" (B), dropped (R) — The Psalter never renders hinneh 見よ (PSA-1); rulers allied against Zion (m2), advancing together (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 48:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · yekhoneneha "堅く立てられる" (L), "堅く立たせてくださる" (B), "守ってくださる" (R); Selah "セラ" — Make firm, keep standing (m2); Selah as PSA-1 (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 48:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · benot Yehudah "ユダの娘たち" (L), "ユダの町々" (B, R); mishpatekha "あなたの裁き" (L), "正しく裁かれる" (B) — The daughters of Judah read as its towns (m1, uncertain); your just verdicts and acts (m2). The wish takes 〜がよい / 〜がいい, as a call to third persons.
- [ ] [PSA 48:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · shitu libbekhem "心を留め" (L), "よく見" (B, R); pasgu "めぐり歩け" (L) — Pay close attention (m1); the rare verb pissgu read as walking among them (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 48:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/48/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-mut "死に至るまで" (L), "わたしたちが死ぬまで" (B), "生きているかぎり" (R) — Until death, as long as we live (m2, uncertain, footnoted); other readings take the word with the next psalm's title.
- [ ] [PSA 49:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v3) L/B/R · setting_alternative · No gender span: benei adam / benei ish "人の子らも、人間の子らも" (L), "身分の低い者も高い者も" (B); 'evyon "乏しい者" (L, B) — Japanese 人 and 子ら already include women (PSA-1), so the English gender spans of 49:3, 10, 13, 17-21 have no Japanese span; the pair is read as people of low and high rank (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 49:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · 'avon 'aqevai "わたしのかかとの咎" (L), "わたしにつきまとう者たちの悪" (B), "ずるい人たち" (R) — Those at my heels, people who cheat me (m1, uncertain, footnoted).

The sentence runs on to 49:10 (m3); each verse closes in Japanese.
- [ ] [PSA 49:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · qirbam "彼らの内なる[思い]" (L), "思い込み" (B), "思っている" (R) — The Masoretic "their inward part" read as what they think (m1, uncertain, footnoted); other witnesses read "their graves".
- [ ] [PSA 49:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · kesel "愚かさ" (L), "愚かにも自分を信じる" (B), "自分は大丈夫だと思い込んでいる" (R) — Foolish confidence (m1, uncertain); followers approve their words (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 49:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · She'ol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R, also where the English R says "grave"); the last line "それには高い住まいもない" (L), "壮大な住まいから遠く離れる" (B) — The Psalter's Sheol forms (PSA-1, m4); the last line is hard (m3, uncertain, footnoted) and follows the English reading at each level.
- [ ] [PSA 49:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/49/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · yiqqacheni "わたしを迎え取ってくださる" (L, B), "わたしを迎えて、ご自分と共にいさせてくださる" (R); padah "贖われる" (L), "代価を払い" (B, R) — What no one else can do (m1), with the word of 49:8; "take" as God took Enoch and Elijah (m2, uncertain), worded with くださる so it does not read as a passive.

- [ ] [PSA 50:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/50/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · ziz sadai "野にうごめくもの" (L, B), "野にいる生き物" (R); 'immadi "わたしと共に[ある]" (L), "わたしの手の内にある" (B) — The small creatures that swarm (m1, uncertain); "with me" is in my keeping, mine (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 50:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/50/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · vatirets "気が合い" (L), "喜んで仲間になり" (B), "それに加わる" (R); mena'afim "姦淫する者たち" (L, B), "夫や妻を裏切る人たち" (R) — "You were pleased with him" as the Masoretic text (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the commandments broken (m2), na'af as the earlier books.
- [ ] [PSA 50:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/50/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · dofi "汚名" (L), "名を汚す" (B), "うそを言いふらす" (R); ben-'immekha "あなたの母の子" — A blemish, slander (m1, uncertain); your mother's son is your own brother (m3), kept as the image.

The two charges are answered (m2) in the words of 50:14-15.

The verb is kept a statement (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 51:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/51/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · damim "血[の罪]" (L), "わたしが流した血の罪" (B), "わたしが人を死なせた罪" (R) — The plural 'bloods' is the guilt of shedding blood (m1, uncertain, with its footnote), here David's guilt for Uriah's death; Literal brackets what Japanese must supply.

The negative takes 〜ことはなさらない for God.
- [ ] [PSA 52:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/52/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · maskil "マスキル" (L, B), "教えと悟りを与える歌" (R), as the Psalter conventions — The meaning of maskil is uncertain (m3); Literal and Balanced keep the term, Readable follows its English.

- [ ] [PSA 52:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/52/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · Selah "セラ" at every level, after the closing 。 — Its meaning is unknown (m3), kept as a transliteration as in the rest of the Psalter.

The terror without a cause (m2) is uncertain.
- [ ] [PSA 54:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/54/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · zarim "よそ者たち" (L, B), "知らない人たち" (R); 'aritsim "横暴な者たち" (L), "暴虐な者たち" (B), "ひどい人たち" (R) — The text read is 'strangers' (m1, uncertain and footnoted; some manuscripts and Psalm 86:14 read 'arrogant'). nafshi is 'my life' at B, R as their English (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 54:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/54/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · hinneh "まことに" (L), "しかし" (B), "でも" (R), not 見よ; Adonai "主" with no span; besomkhei "支える者たちの中に[おられる]" (L), "支えてくださる方" (B) — hinneh marks the turn to confidence (m3). 'Among those who uphold' means the one who upholds (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the Hebrew, Balanced and Readable say it as their English.
- [ ] [PSA 54:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/54/#v9) L · supplied_words · The subject of hitsilani is left unstated (L), "その方は" (B), "神は" (R) — The subject may be God or God's name of verse 8 (m1, uncertain); Japanese can leave it out at Literal, and the respect form shows that God is meant.
- [ ] [PSA 55:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/55/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · 'oyev "敵"; 'aqat rasha' "悪しき者の圧迫" (L), "悪しき者が重くのしかかる" (B); yamitu 'aven "災いを降らせ" (L), "浴びせ" (B) — 'aqah is crushing pressure (m1, uncertain); to drop trouble is to bring harm down (m2, uncertain). rasha' 悪しき者 (L, B), 悪い人たち (R) as the Psalter conventions.
- [ ] [PSA 55:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/55/#v11) B/R · supplied_words · The subject of yesovevuha supplied as "暴虐と争い" (B), "暴力と争い" (R), as the English; Literal "彼ら" — 'They' are violence and strife of verse 10 (m1, uncertain), pictured as watchmen on the walls.
- [ ] [PSA 55:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/55/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · padah "贖われた" (L), "贖い" (B), "救い" (R); beshalom "平安のうちに" (L), "無事に" (B, R); 'immadi "わたしと共に" (L), "わたしに向かって" (B), "わたしと戦っていた" (R) — 'With me' is read as 'against me' (m3, uncertain), as the English Balanced and Readable; Literal keeps the Hebrew word. padah 贖う (L) as the Psalter conventions.
- [ ] [PSA 55:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/55/#v20) L/B/R · word_order · Selah "セラ" moved to the end of the verse, as the English; ya'anem "低くされる" (L, B), "倒される" (R); chalifot "変わること" — In the Hebrew Selah stands in the middle of the verse; the Psalter writes it at the end of the verse's last line. 'Humble them' and 'no changes' are uncertain (m1, m3).
- [ ] [PSA 55:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/55/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · hashlekh "投げかけよ" (L, as 1PE 5:7 L), "ゆだねなさい" (B), "まかせなさい" (R); mot "揺るがされる" (L), "つまずき倒れる" (B), "倒される" (R); the negatives "お許しにならない", "なさらない" — 1 Peter 5:7 echoes this line (m3); Literal shares its verb. The psalmist's command to the hearer is 〜せよ (L), 〜しなさい (B, R) as the Psalter conventions. yehav is what is given one to carry (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 56:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/56/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · sha'af "踏みにじる" (L), "踏みつける" (B, R); 'enosh "人" (L), "人々" (B, R) — The verb may mean to hound or pant after (m1, uncertain); 'enosh is a weak human being (m2), set against God in the refrain.
- [ ] [PSA 56:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/56/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · marom "高ぶって" (L), "高慢にも" (B), "高ぶった人たち" (R); shorerai "わたしの敵" — marom is read as 'from on high, arrogantly' (m1, uncertain and footnoted); some take it as an address to God, the Most High.
- [ ] [PSA 56:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/56/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · pallet-lamo as a question expecting no "逃れがあろうか？" (L), "逃れられるだろうか？" (B), "逃げてよいのだろうか？" (R); 'ammim "もろもろの民" (L, B), "国々の民" (R) — The question form is uncertain (m1, footnoted); the peoples are the nations hostile to God's people (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 57:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/57/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-tashchet "「滅ぼすな」" (L), "「滅ぼすな」という調べに合わせて" (B), "…に合わせる" (R), with the English quotation count; me'arah "洞穴" — A tune name probably (m3, uncertain), translated in brackets at every level as the English quotes it, in the Psalter's form for tune names. The cave recalls 1 Samuel 22 and 24 (m5).
- [ ] [PSA 57:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/57/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · Elohim 'elyon "いと高き神" (L, B), "すべてのものの上におられる神" (R); gomer "成し遂げてくださる" — Elyon as the Psalter conventions; 'who completes for me' is read as carrying his purpose through (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 57:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/57/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · leva'im "獅子たち" (L, B), "ライオンのような敵" (R); lohatim "むさぼる者たち" (L); benei 'adam "人の子ら" (L) with no gender span — lohatim is read as 'devourers' (m2, uncertain); the root can also mean 'aflame'. 人の子ら is inclusive in Japanese, so the English span has no counterpart.
- [ ] [PSA 57:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/57/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · kevodi "わたしの栄光" (L), "わたしの魂" (B), "わたしの心" (R); nevel "琴", kinnor "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R) — 'My glory' is read as the inner self (m1, uncertain), which Balanced says as its English. nevel and kinnor as 1CH and PSA-3.
- [ ] [PSA 58:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/58/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · 'elem read as 'elim "神々よ" (L, B), "神々のようにふるまう支配者たちよ" (R), addressed as "あなたがた" (L, B), "あなたたち" (R); tsedeq "義" (L), "正しいこと" (B, R); meisharim "公正に" (L), "公平に" … — The word is read as 'gods', the mighty ones who rule (m1, uncertain and footnoted); Readable makes it explicit as its English. The questions expect 'no' (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 58:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/58/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · peten "コブラ" at every level; the run-on sentence (m3) closed at 58:5 and 58:6 begins "それは" (L) / "そのコブラは" (B, R) — peten is a poisonous snake, perhaps the cobra (m1, uncertain), which snake charmers handled (m2). A Japanese relative clause cannot follow its noun, so 58:6 restates the subject.
- [ ] [PSA 58:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/58/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · yidrokh chitstsav "彼が自分の矢をつがえるとき" (L, singular as the Hebrew), "彼らが" (B, R as their English); yitmolalu "断ち切られたように" (L), "折られる" (B) — The one who takes aim is read as the wicked (m2, uncertain); 'as if cut off' may mean blunted (m3, uncertain). 彼 here is a man, never God.
- [ ] [PSA 58:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/58/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · shabblul "かたつむり" (L, B), "なめくじ" (R, its English 'slug'); nefel 'eshet "女の死産の子" (L), "死んで生まれた子" (B) — The snail that seems to melt as it leaves its slime trail (m1, uncertain); the last clause is a wish (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 58:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/58/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · sirotekhem 'atad "あなたがたの鍋が茨を感じる" (L), "…茨の火を感じる" (B); yis'arennu "吹き払われる" (L, B), "吹き飛ばされる" (R); God as "その方" / "神" — A hard verse read as sudden judgment, before a pot over thorns grows hot (m1, uncertain and footnoted; m2 uncertain); the verb is the sweep of a storm wind (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 59:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/59/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · hinneh "まことに" (L), "見てください" (B), "聞いてください" (R); mi shomea' as the enemies' own words in 「」 with "と[言う]" (L), "と言う" (B), "と思っている" (R) — 'Who hears?' is read as their own words (m2, uncertain), supplied with a verb of saying as the English. yabbi'un is to spew or bubble out (m1).

- [ ] [PSA 59:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/59/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · kalleh "滅ぼし尽くしてください" (L), "滅ぼしてください" (B), "終わらせてください" (R); veyede'u "彼らが知るように" (L), "人々は…知る" (B, R); Ya'aqov "ヤコブ" (L, B), "イスラエル" (R, as its English) — 'Let them know' is read as 'people will know' (m1, uncertain); the tension with 59:12 'do not kill them' is noted in m3.
- [ ] [PSA 60:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/60/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · shushan 'edut "シュシャン・エドトに合わせて" (L, B), "「契約のゆり」という調べに合わせる" (R); lelammed "教えるためのもの" — The tune name is uncertain (m3), transliterated where the English transliterates and translated in brackets where the English Readable quotes it, as PSA-1's ショシャニム / 「ゆり」. The title runs on into 60:2 (m1); Japanese closes it and makes 60:2 the time clause.
- [ ] [PSA 60:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/60/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · yere'eikha "あなたを恐れる者たち" (L), "畏れる者たち" (B), "おそれ敬う人たち" (R); nes "旗"; lehitnoses mippenei qoshet "[そこへ]逃れるように、弓の前から" (L) — The fear of God as the Psalter conventions. The banner is a flag to gather around (m1); 'to flee from the bow' is uncertain (m2, footnoted). お与えになった avoids 与えられた, which could read as passive.
- [ ] [PSA 60:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/60/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · yedideikha "あなたの愛する者たち" (L), "あなたが愛しておられる者たち" (B, R); yeminkha "右の手" (L, B), "大きな力" (R) — 60:7-14 is repeated in Psalm 108:7-14 (m1); the drafter of 108 should copy these lines and change only where 108's Hebrew differs. 'Answer me' is the reading followed (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 60:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/60/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · beqodsho "その聖において" (L), "その聖なる所から" (B, R); Shekhem "シケム", Sukkot "スコト" (as 1 Kings and Genesis); God's speech plain with わたし, opening here and closing in 60:10 — 'In his holiness' may mean from his holy place or by his holy nature (m2, uncertain); Literal keeps the Hebrew. God's words (m4) run to 60:10, as the English.

Philistia's shout is read as hailing the victor (m4, uncertain and footnoted); God's commands are 〜なさい as the Psalter conventions.
- [ ] [PSA 60:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/60/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ir matsor "堅固な町" (L), "城壁のある町" (B), "エドムの強い町" (R); nachani "導いてくれたか" (L, the Hebrew perfect), "導いてくれるだろうか" (B, R) — The king speaks (m3); 'has led me' means 'who could lead me' (m4), which Balanced and Readable say. The city is Edom's stronghold (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 61:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/61/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · miqtseh ha'arets "地の果てから"; tsur yarum mimmenni "わたしより高い岩" (L), "わたしには登れないほど高い岩" (B, R) — The end of the earth is read as far from God's house (m1, uncertain); the high rock is a safe place he cannot reach alone (m2, uncertain). tsur 岩 as the Psalter conventions.
- [ ] [PSA 62:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/62/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 'al-yedutun "エドトンに合わせて" (L), "エドトンの調べに合わせて" (B), "エドトンの歌い方で" (R) — Jeduthun keeps the 1CH form エドトン (as 39:1). With 'al the title names a manner of performance linked to him (m3, uncertain); the Psalter's に合わせて pattern for music terms is used, with B and R following the English "According to" / "In the style of".
- [ ] [PSA 62:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/62/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · teratsechu "打ち砕こうとするのか" (L), "打ち倒そうとする" (B), "攻め続ける…押して倒そうとする" (R); the wall and fence picture the victim about to fall — The verb and its vowels are uncertain (m1, footnoted): "batter" or "murder"; the English reading is followed. The leaning wall is the threatened man, not the attackers (m2, uncertain). Plural you あなたがた (L, B), あなたたち (R).
- [ ] [PSA 62:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/62/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · Selah "セラ" at every level, at the end of the verse (also 62:9 and in Psalms 66, 67, 68) — The meaning of the word is no longer known (m4); it is kept as it is, as the Psalter conventions (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 62:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/62/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · bene-'adam / bene 'ish "人の子ら" / "人間の子ら" (L), "身分の低い者" / "身分の高い者" (B), "普通の人々" / "えらい人々" (R) — The pair is read as low and high in rank (m2, uncertain), as the English B and R; Literal keeps two different words for the two Hebrew phrases. hevel "息" at every level (Psalter convention, 39:6).
- [ ] [PSA 63:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/63/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · ken "そのように" (L, B), not rendered (R) — "So" may mean "in the same way" (as I long now, so I looked before) (m1, uncertain); Readable leaves it out, as the English R. Seeing God is meeting him in worship (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 63:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/63/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · hannishba' bo "その方にかけて誓う者" (L), "その方の名によって誓う者" (B), "神の名によって約束をする人" (R) — "Him" is read as God (m2, uncertain), as the English; the king (m1) is David, named in the third person.

- [ ] [PSA 64:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/64/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · tamnu chefes mechuppas, read as the plotters' boast in quotes: "わたしたちは、よく練り上げた企てを仕上げた！" (L), "完璧な計画だ。よく練り上げてある！" (B, R) — The text and its sense are uncertain (m1, footnoted); the English reading is followed. The deep inner being and heart are hidden thoughts (m2); 人 needs no gender span.
- [ ] [PSA 64:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/64/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · vayyakhshiluhu "彼をつまずかせ" (L), "彼らをつまずかせて倒す" (B, R); yitnodedu "[頭を]振る" (L), "頭を振る" (B), "あきれて首を振る" (R) — The clause is hard (m1, uncertain): their own words bring them down; the head-shaking is scorn (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 65:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/65/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · dumiyyah tehillah "沈黙が賛美[である]" (L), "沈黙は賛美である" (B), "静かに待つときも、賛美する" (R) — The Hebrew vowels read "silence"; the Greek reads "praise is fitting" (m1, uncertain, footnoted). The English reading, quiet trust as worship, is followed. neder 誓願 (L, B), 約束 (R) as the Pentateuch notes; the vow is kept with an offering (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 65:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/65/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · motsa'e-voqer va'erev "朝と夕べの出て行く所" (L), "日の昇る所も日の沈む所も" (B), "日の昇る所から日の沈む所まで" (R) — The phrase is read as east and west, the whole world (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the Hebrew picture.
- [ ] [PSA 65:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/65/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · peleg 'Elohim "神の流れ" (L), "神の水路" (B), "空にある神の川" (R) — The source of the rain (m2, uncertain); peleg 流れ (L), 水路 (B) as palgei mayim in 1:3; Readable explains, as the English R.

- [ ] [PSA 66:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/66/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · simu khavod tehillato "その方への賛美を、栄光とせよ" (L), "栄光に満ちた賛美を、その方にささげよ" (B, R by sense) — The phrase is read as giving God glorious praise (m2, uncertain); zimmer ほめ歌う (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 66:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/66/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · sham nismechah-bo "そこで、わたしたちはその方を喜ぼう" (L, B), "あのとき彼らがそこで喜んだように、わたしたちも…" (R) — The singers join the crossing of the Red Sea and the Jordan (m1, m2, uncertain); Readable spells it out as the English R.

Calls to praise 〜せよ (L, B), 〜しよう (R, God's people).

What the women say follows in 68:13 in quotes (m3).

The women's song in quotes, as the English (m1, uncertain).

The chariots are God's heavenly army (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 68:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/68/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · lishkon "住まわれるために" (L, B), "住まわれる" (R) — That God might live there (m4, uncertain); the English R makes it a statement.
- [ ] [PSA 68:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/68/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · ya'amos-lanu "わたしたちのために[重荷を]担われる" (L), "わたしたちの重荷を担ってくださる" (B), "わたしたちの重い荷を担ってくださる" (R) — Bearing the people's burden (m1, uncertain; it may be "loads us with good"). barukh as the Psalter conventions; 'Adonai 主, ha'el 神 (m2).

The dogs picture total defeat (m3).

"Guilt on their guilt" holds every sin against them (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 70:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/70/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · lehazkir "思い起こしていただくために" (L), "記念のために" (B), "思い出していただくために" (R), as the title of 38:1; ledavid alone "ダビデによる" — The same title word as Psalm 38:1 keeps the same Japanese (m3, uncertain); "Of David" without mizmor is ダビデによる, as in the Psalter's other titles. Psalm 70 repeats 40:14-18 (m4), and its Japanese is copied from Psalm 40, changed only where the Hebrew differs.

L follows the English line order, with the subject after the quotation.

The hard line is rendered as the English reads it.

- [ ] [PSA 71:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/71/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · mofet "不思議なしるし" (L), "人々が驚くしるし" (B); R a clause; machaseh "避け所" (L, B), "逃げ込める…場所" (R) — A portent is a sign that makes people stop and look (m1, uncertain): as a warning or as a wonder; Japanese keeps it open, as the English does.

A causative stays plain for God (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 71:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/71/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · boshu / chaferu as done: past "恥を見…うろたえた" (L, B), "恥をかき、面目を失った" (R); tehgeh "口ずさむ" (L), "語り続ける" (B), "話そう" (R) — The enemies' shame is spoken of as already done (m1, uncertain); hagah is speaking over and over (m2), 口ずさむ at L as 1:2.
- [ ] [PSA 72:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/72/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · lishlomoh "ソロモンによる" at every level — "Of Solomon" may mean for or by Solomon (m2, uncertain); the Psalter's title form ダビデによる / アサフによる keeps the same openness.

B and R name the king rather than repeat 彼, which reads stiffly in Japanese.

- [ ] [PSA 72:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/72/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · tsiyyim "荒れ野に住む者たち" (L), "砂漠の遊牧の民" (B), "砂漠に住む人たち" (R); "lick the dust" kept at L, B, explained at R — The peoples of the wilderness (m1, uncertain); licking the dust is bowing to the ground in defeat (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 72:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/72/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · The unnamed "one" who gives, prays and blesses as passives (L, B: "与えられ", "ささげられ", "祝福される") and as "人々" (R) — The king lives; others give, pray and bless (m1, uncertain); Japanese passives leave the doer unnamed, as the Hebrew does.

God is その方 in the third person, never 彼 (PSA-1).
