- [ ] [PSA 74:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/74/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · Livyatan "レビヤタン" (L, B), "海の怪物レビヤタン" (R), as JOB and ISA; le'am letsiyyim "民の、荒れ野に住む者たち" (L), "砂漠の生き物たち" (B), "砂漠の野の獣たち" (R) — Leviathan is a many-headed sea monster (m1); "to the people, to the desert-dwellers" is read as the creatures of the desert (m2, uncertain), as the English B and R.
- [ ] [PSA 74:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/74/#v16) B/R · word_choice · ma'or "光" (L), "月" (B, R) — The light is a light in the sky, the moon (m1, uncertain), as the English B and R; compare Genesis 1:14-18 (m2).

- [ ] [PSA 75:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/75/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · God's speech opens with 「 in 75:3 and closes 」 in 75:6, as the English; God speaks plainly, "わたし" — Verses 3-6 are read as God speaking (m1, uncertain, with a footnote in the English); his words are plain, as God's speech in the Psalter (PSA-1). mesharim "まっすぐに" (L), "公平に" (B, R): what is level and fair (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 75:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/75/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ammudeha "その柱" (L, B), "その土台" (R); Selah "セラ" — The pillars are what holds the world up (m2), R "foundations" as the English R; Selah as PSA-1 (m3, uncertain).

The sentence runs on (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 75:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/75/#v10) B/R · supplied_words · "それを" (B), "神がなさったことを" (R) supplied as the English — Declaring is telling what God has done (m2); "I will declare" is the Hebrew reading (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 75:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/75/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · The speaker "わたし" with plain verbs (the psalmist); qeren "角" (L, B), "力" (R) — "I will cut off" is read as the psalmist, as God's servant (m1, uncertain), so the verb is not God's and carries no respect form; horns are strength and pride (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 76:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/76/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · Shalem "サレム" (L, B, as GEN 14:18), "エルサレム" (R); sukko / me'onato "隠れ場" / "すみか" (L, B), a lion's den (R) — Salem is Jerusalem (m1), named at R as the English R; lair and den are a lion's shelter (m2, uncertain), so sukkah is not 仮庵 here. 'aryeh ライオン (R) as PSA-1.

R uses 〜てくださった for God's saving act.

The verb usually rendered 感謝する is made たたえる here, since rage cannot give thanks.
- [ ] [PSA 76:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/76/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · nidru veshallemu "誓願を立て、[それを]果たせ" (L), "…果たしなさい" (B), "約束をしたら、それを守りなさい" (R); the jussive "let them bring" "携えて来るがよい" (L), "…来るように" (B, R); lammora' "恐るべき方" … — Vowing and paying is promising an offering and giving it (m1); those around him are the surrounding peoples (m2, uncertain), R "国々" as the English R; the Fearsome One is a title (m3). The psalmist's commands 〜せよ / 〜しなさい (PSA-1).

A causative stays plain for God (PSA-1).

'Elyon いと高き方 (L, B), すべてのものの上におられる神 (R).

"Turned and tested" is tested again (m1).

Japanese therefore gives the sense the name …

- [ ] [PSA 88:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/88/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · 'afunah "途方に暮れている" (L, B), "どうしたらよいのかわからない" (R); go'ea' minno'ar "若いころから死にかけている" (L), "若いころから…死に瀕してきた" (B) — I am helpless is at a loss, without way out (m1, uncertain); dying from youth is a lifelong affliction (m2).

God's words end here (m2).

The negative for God avoids 〜られない (PSA-1).

Emptiness is futility (m3).

L runs on into 89:52 (m4).

The title keeps the Psalter's form for Moses (PSA-1 conventions); Psalm 90 is drafted afresh, not after a familiar Japanese wording.

'avon 咎 (L, B), 罪 (R) as in the Psalter.

The repeated pronoun hu' is kept at L (その方が、その方こそが).

The mother bird's wings (m1) are spelled out in R, as in 17:8 and 63:8.

- [ ] [PSA 91:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/91/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "あなた" for God in the first line and for the hearer in the second, as the Hebrew's 'attah and the second-person verb; the second line has plain verbs (住まいとした), … — The speaker changes suddenly (m1, uncertain, footnote): the first line is a cry to God, the second turns back to the one who trusts. Japanese keeps the Hebrew's shift; the plain verb of the second line shows it is not God who is addressed there.

The three-fold line (m3) is kept at L and B.

The respect verbs keep their object 彼らを, so they do not read as passives.
- [ ] [PSA 95:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/95/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · mechqerei-'arets "地の深い所" (L, B); to'afot harim "山々の頂" (L, B) — Depths and peaks (m1, m2, uncertain) are the lowest and the highest places (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 95:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/95/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · hayyom 'im-beqolo tishma'u "今日、あなたがたがその方の声に聞き従うなら！" (L), "ああ…聞き従ってくれたなら！" (B), "…どんなによいだろう！" (R); "その方の声" for HEB 3:7's 彼の声 — The line is a wish, 'if only' (m2, uncertain, footnote), not the protasis of 95:8; L keeps the bare 'if' with ！, B and R spell out the wish. Hebrews 3:7 quotes it (m3); its 今日 and 聞く are kept, but the Psalter never uses 彼 for God, so その方の声.
- [ ] [PSA 96:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/96/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · First line as 1CH 16:29c, second as 1CH 16:30a; behadrat-qodesh "聖なる輝きのうちに" — The Hebrew joins 1 Chronicles 16:29c and 16:30a, and both keep their Japanese. The splendor of holiness may be God's or the worshipers' attire (m1, uncertain, footnote); tremble is shaking with awe (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 96:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/96/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · YHWH malakh as 1CH 16:31 and 93:1; 'af-tikkon tevel bal-timmot as 1CH 16:30b; yadin 'ammim bemesharim "公平をもって…お裁きになる" (L), "公平にお裁きになる" (B, R) — The psalm says 'say among the nations' where 1 Chronicles 16:31 has 'let them say', and adds the line on judging, so the frame and the last line are new; the cry and the firm world keep the Chronicles Japanese (m1, uncertain; m2). To judge with uprightness is to rule fairly (m3); お裁きになる, not 裁かれる, …
- [ ] [PSA 97:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/97/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · YHWH malakh as 93:1 and 96:10 (L "王として治められる", B "こそ王である！", R "こそ王だ！"); 'iyyim "島々" (L, B), "遠くの島々や海辺に住む人々" (R) — The kingship cry keeps one Japanese form through Psalms 93-99 (m1, uncertain); the coastlands are the lands across the sea (m2), which R spells out as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 97:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/97/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · benot Yehudah "ユダの娘たち" (L), "ユダの町々" (B, R), as 48:12; Zion "シオンの人々" (R) where the English R says Jerusalem — The daughters of Judah are its towns (m1, uncertain), as in 48:12 (m2); the Psalter keeps シオン also where the English R names Jerusalem (69:36).
- [ ] [PSA 97:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/97/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · zarua' "蒔かれ" (L, B), "まいてくださる" (R) — The Hebrew reads 'sown' (m1, uncertain, footnote; some read 'dawns'); light is life and blessing (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 99:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/99/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · YHWH malakh as 93:1, 96:10, 97:1; yoshev keruvim "ケルビムの上に座しておられる" (L, B as 80:2, 1SA 4:4, 2KI 19:15), "翼のあるケルビムの上の王座に座っておられる" (R) — The kingship cry keeps one form through Psalms 93-99 (m1, uncertain); enthroned on the cherubim is above the ark (m2), in the form already used in 80:2 and Samuel-Kings; R adds the wings as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 99:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/99/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · ve'oz melekh mishpat 'ahev "王の力は公正を愛する" (L), "公正を愛する力ある王よ" (B, R); 'attah "あなたこそ"; mishpat utsedaqah "公正と義" (L, B) — The strength of the King loves justice is read as the mighty King who loves justice (m1, uncertain, footnote); L keeps the Hebrew's words. The repeated 'you' is emphasis (m2); Jacob is the people of Israel (m3). R uses 愛しておられる, not 愛される, which reads as a passive.
- [ ] [PSA 99:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/99/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · 'El nose' "赦す神" (L, B), "ゆるしてくださった" (R); noqem 'al-'alilotam "彼らの行いに報いる" (L), "悪い行いを罰する" (B), "罰もお与えになった" (R) — Forgiving is lifting away sin (m1), with the Psalter's salach forms (赦す L, B, ゆるす R); avenging on their deeds is punishing their wrongs (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 100:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/100/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · mizmor letodah "感謝の賛歌" (L), "感謝をささげるための賛歌" (B), "…歌" (R); hari'u "喜び叫べ" (L), "喜びの声を上げよ" (B, R), as 98:4 — For thanksgiving is for giving thanks (m2, uncertain); the shout greets a king (m3), with the words of 98:4. Psalm 100 is drafted afresh, not after a familiar Japanese wording; the call to the earth is 〜せよ at every level.
- [ ] [PSA 101:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/101/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · chesed umishpat "誠実と公正" (L, B), "誠実さと、公平に行うこと" (R), not the Psalter's 変わらぬいつくしみ for God — The loyal love may be what God shows or what the king will practice (m4, uncertain); 誠実 is the Psalter's word for chesed between people and its L word for God's, so it keeps both readings. The title ledavid mizmor is ダビデによる。賛歌。 as 24:1, since the English separates the two words.
- [ ] [PSA 101:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/101/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · derekh tamim "無垢な道" (L), "非の打ちどころのない道" (B), "欠点のない生き方" (R); tom-levavi "心の潔白" (L), "潔白な心" (B), "正直" (R) — The Psalter's tamim and tom forms (15:2, 26:1); give heed is act wisely (m1); the question is a longing for God (m2, uncertain); integrity within the house is honesty in private too (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 101:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/101/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · devar-beliyya'al "よこしまなもの" (L, as 41:9), "卑しいもの" (B), "悪いもの" (R); 'asoh-setim "道をそれる者たちの行い" (L), "背き去る者たちの行い" (B) — A worthless thing is anything base (m1); setting before the eyes is looking with approval (m2); the doing of those who turn aside is uncertain (m3), and the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [PSA 101:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/101/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · levav 'iqqesh "ねじれた心" (L), "曲がった心" (B), "ゆがんだ考え" (R); lo' 'eda' "知らない" (L), "関わらない" (B, R) — A perverse heart is a twisted mind (m1, uncertain); to know here is to have to do with (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 102:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/102/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · keqed "炉のように" (L), "かまどのように" (B, R) — The hearth is a place of burning (m1, uncertain); days in smoke and burned bones are life wasting away (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 102:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/102/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · qe'at midbar "荒れ野のみみずく" (L, B), "荒れ野にいる野鳥" (R); kos choravot "廃墟のふくろう" (L), "廃墟にいる小さなふくろう" (B) — Two birds of lonely places (m1, uncertain); two owl words keep them apart, as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 102:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/102/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · cherfuni "ののしり" (as through Psalm 69); bi nishba'u "わたしを[呪いに]使って誓う" (L), "わたしの名を呪いの言葉にする" (B, R) — Those who mock me are those who make a fool of me (m1, uncertain); swearing by me is using me as a curse (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 102:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/102/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · teracham "あわれまれる" (L), "あわれんでくださる" (B); lechenenah "恵みを示す" (L, B), "やさしくしてくださる" (R); mo'ed "定めの時" (L), "あなたが定められた時" (B) — Zion lies in ruins (m1, uncertain); the appointed time is the time God has set (m2). The Hebrew's 'her' is rendered シオン, since Japanese does not use 彼女 for a city.
- [ ] [PSA 102:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/102/#v21) L/B/R · setting_alternative · benei temutah "死の子ら" (L), "死に定められた者たち" (B), "死刑を言い渡された人たち" (R), with no gender span — Sons of death are those doomed to die (m1); 子ら is inclusive, so the English gender span (children / sons) is not needed. The prisoners may be the exiles (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 103:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/103/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'edyekh "おまえの飾り" (L), "おまえの生涯" (B), "わたしの人生" (R) — The word usually means ornament; here it is read as life or desire (m1, uncertain). L keeps the word, B and R give the sense as the English does. Eagle: 鷲 (L, B), わし (R; not a Joyo kanji), as Exodus 19:4.
- [ ] [PSA 103:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/103/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · yitsrenu "わたしたちの成り立ち" (L), "どのように造られたか" (B, R); 'afar "ちり" — Our form is how we are made (m2, uncertain), an echo of Genesis 2:7 (m1); ちり as Genesis 3:19 and Psalm 90:3.
- [ ] [PSA 104:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "風をご自分の使いとなさり" (winds made messengers), not Hebrews 1:7's "天使たちを風とし" — The meaning decision reads the Hebrew as 'makes the winds his messengers' (m1, uncertain, footnote); Hebrews 1:7 quotes the Greek, which makes the angels winds (m2). The Japanese follows the Hebrew and keeps Hebrews' 仕える者 and 炎/火.
- [ ] [PSA 104:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · The waters as subject: "山々を上り、谷を下り" — The meaning decision reads the waters going up the mountains and down the valleys (m1, uncertain, footnote); the other reading, 'the mountains rose, the valleys sank', is left to the footnote. The place is the seas (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 104:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v13) L/B/R · register · "その方…ご自分の高殿" and "あなたのわざ" in one verse, as the Hebrew — The verse speaks of God and to God (m3); Japanese keeps both persons. The fruit of your works is what God's work brings (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 104:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · la'avodat ha'adam "人の労働のために" (L), "人が耕して育てる" (B), "人々が畑で育てる" (R); lechem "パン" (L), "食べ物" (B, R) — Plants for people to work (m1, uncertain); bread is food (m2). The run-on sentence of 104:13-15 is closed in each verse (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 104:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · 'arzei Levanon "レバノンの杉" — The cedars of Lebanon (m2), 杉 as in Kings. The trees of the LORD are the great trees he planted (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 104:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · chasidah "コウノトリ"; beroshim "糸杉" — The stork (m1) as Leviticus 11:19; berosh 糸杉 as Kings and Isaiah (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 104:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · Livyatan "レビヤタン" (L, B), "海の怪物レビヤタン" (R) — A great sea creature (m1), in the form of Job and Psalm 74:14. To play in it is in the sea (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 104:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · ruchakha "霊" (L, B), "命の息" (R); yibbare'un "創造され" (L), "造られ" (B) — Your spirit is your life-giving breath (m1, uncertain); bara' 創造する (L), 造る (B) as Psalm 51:12. The same ruach is 'their breath' in 104:29, which Japanese says as 息.
- [ ] [PSA 104:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/104/#v34) L/B · word_choice · sichi "わたしの思い" (L), "わたしの思いめぐらすこと" (B) — My musing is my song or my thought (m1, uncertain); 'I' is emphatic, 'as for me' (m2), which L keeps as このわたしは.
- [ ] [PSA 105:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · nafsho "首" — Neck is the throat, the life (m2, uncertain), as the English has it.
- [ ] [PSA 105:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · devaro "彼の言葉" (Joseph's); 'imrat YHWH "主の仰せ" (L, B, the Psalm 119 table for 'imrah); tseraf "試した" (L, R), "練り清めた" (B) — His word is read as Joseph's word (m1, uncertain); tested is refined, as metal in fire (m2), which B and R bring out.
- [ ] [PSA 105:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · le'sor "縛る" — The form read is 'to bind' (m1, uncertain, footnote); the ancient versions read 'to instruct', which the footnote gives. Elders are senior officials (m3), so B, R 相談役.
- [ ] [PSA 105:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · divrei 'ototaw "その方のしるしの言葉" (L), "その方が告げられたしるし" (B, R); "彼らは" as subject — The words of his signs are the signs he had spoken (m1, uncertain); the form read is 'they set' (m2, uncertain), Moses and Aaron. mofetim 不思議 (L, B), 驚くべきこと (R).
- [ ] [PSA 105:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · velo'-maru "逆らわなかった" (L, B), "モーセとアロンは…背かなかった" (R) — They are Moses and Aaron, who obeyed (m2, uncertain, footnote); marah 逆らう (L, B), 背く (R) in the Psalter's forms. Darkness 闇 (L, B), 暗やみ (R) as Exodus 10.
- [ ] [PSA 105:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · 'arov "[虫の]群れ" (L), "あぶの群れ" (B, R); kinnim "ぶよ" — Swarm and gnats as Exodus 8 and Psalm 78:45 (m2, m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 105:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v34) L/B · word_choice · yeleq "若いいなご" — Young locusts, hoppers (m2, uncertain); 'arbeh いなご as Exodus 10. R says only locusts, as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 105:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/105/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · bishvataw "その方の部族"; koshel "つまずく者" (L), "よろめく者" (B), "弱って歩けない人" (R) — His tribes are God's tribes (m2, uncertain); to stumble is to be feeble, fall behind (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 106:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · razon "やせ衰え" (L), "やせ衰えさせる病" (B), "病気にして、やせ細らせた" (R); nafsham "彼らの魂" (L) — Leanness is a wasting disease (m2, uncertain); their soul is themselves (m3), kept at L only.
- [ ] [PSA 106:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · ulehappil "倒し" (L), "倒され" (B) — The form read is 'to make fall' (m2, uncertain), a warning that looks ahead to the exile (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 106:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · Ba'al Pe'or "ペオルのバアル" (L, B), "ペオルの神バアル" (R); zivchei metim "死者へのいけにえ" (L), "死んだ神々にささげられた肉" (R) — Names and verbs as Numbers 25:3; sacrifices of the dead are offerings to lifeless gods (m2, uncertain), which R says as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 106:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · vayefallel "裁きを行い" (L), "間に入り" (B), "行動を起こし" (R) — Intervened, carried out judgment (m2, uncertain); Phinehas ピネハス as Numbers 25.
- [ ] [PSA 106:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · mei Merivah "メリバの水" (L, B), "メリバの泉" (R) — The waters of Meribah, Numbers 20 (m1), as Psalm 81:8; 'him' is God (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 106:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · rucho "その方の霊" (L, B), "神の霊" (R), not 聖霊 — His spirit is read as God's Spirit (m1, uncertain, footnote); Japanese has no capital letter and writes 霊, as Psalm 51:13 あなたの聖なる霊; the other reading, Moses' spirit, is left to the footnote.
- [ ] [PSA 106:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/106/#v43) L/B/R · word_choice · ba'atsatam "自分たちの計りごとによって" (L), "自分の思いのままに" (B); vayyamokku "低くされた" (L), "沈んでいった" (B) — Their counsel is their own plans (m2); sank low is were brought down (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 107:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/107/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · miyyam "海から" (L), "南から" (B, R) — The sea is read here as the south, completing the four directions (m2, uncertain, with the English footnote); L keeps the Hebrew word.
- [ ] [PSA 107:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/107/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · shechitotam "その穴" (L), "滅びの穴" (B); R "死なないように守ってくださった" — Their pits are the grave (m2, uncertain); the Psalter's forms for shachat as the pit of death (穴 L, 滅びの穴 B). rafa' 癒やす (L, B), いやす (R).
- [ ] [PSA 107:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/107/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · yishtoqu "それらが静まった" (L), "すべてが静まる" (B), "何もかも穏やかになる" (R); mechoz "港" — Who grew still, the waves or the sailors, is uncertain (m1); L 'they' as それら leaves it open, B and R say everything, as the English. The harbor is a haven by the shore (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 107:39](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/107/#v39) L/B/R · word_choice · yashochu "かがめられる" (L), "打ちひしがれる" (B), "押しつぶされてしまう" (R); 'otser ra'ah "圧迫と災い" (L), "ひどい支配と災い" (B) — A turn of fortune (m1, uncertain); the oppression of evil is crushing harm (m2), set out as a list as the English does.
- [ ] [PSA 108:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/108/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · As 57:8 (nakhon "揺るがない" L, "定まった" B, "落ち着いて、しっかりしている" R), without its repeated half-line; 'af-kevodi "わたしの栄光も[共に]" (L), "わたしの魂をこめて" (B), "わたしのすべてをもって" (R) — 108:2 takes 57:8 without the repetition of 'my heart is steadfast' and draws in kevodi from 57:9, where the Psalter has わたしの栄光 (L), わたしの魂 (B), わたしの心 (R). Here my glory is the inner self (m3, uncertain); R 'with all that I am' as the English R.
- [ ] [PSA 109:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/109/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · yistenuni "わたしを訴える"; wa'ani tefillah "わたしは祈り[そのもの]" (L), "わたしは祈り続ける" (B, R) — To accuse is to act as an accuser at law (m1), so 訴える, not サタン; 'I am prayer' is giving oneself to prayer (m2, uncertain). L keeps the bare noun with a bracket, B and R say the sense as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 109:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/109/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · hafqed "任命してください" (L), "立ててください" (B); satan "告発する者" (L, B), R "彼を訴える" — A request to God, so 〜てください (PSA-1); satan here is an opponent at law (m3, uncertain, footnote), so a common noun, not サタン. The one prayed against is 彼, one enemy or each of them (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 109:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/109/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · yetse' rasha' "有罪とされて出て行く" (L), "有罪の判決が下る" (B); tefillato "彼の祈り" — To come out guilty is to leave the court condemned (m2); his prayer may be his plea in court (m1, uncertain); Japanese keeps 祈り as the English.
- [ ] [PSA 109:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/109/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · nowa' yanu'u "さまよいにさまよって" (L); mecharvoteihem "自分たちの廃墟を離れて" (L), "荒れ果てた家を離れて" (B), "廃墟となった家から遠く離れて" (R) — Wander and wander is always on the move (m1); 'seek from their ruins' is read as searching for food away from home (m2, uncertain, footnote); food is supplied as in the English.
- [ ] [PSA 109:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/109/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · Perfect verbs "臨んだ", "遠ざかった" (L), "降りかかった", "遠く離れた" (B), R "今…降りかかっている", "今…来ない" — It came and it was far say what has happened to him (m1, uncertain, footnote); curse and blessing return to the one who speaks them (m2). Japanese keeps the Hebrew past at L and B, and R makes the present result explicit as the English R does.

Plural you あなたがた (L, B); R leaves it out.

- [ ] [PSA 129:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/129/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · The harvest greeting in one quotation at every level, as the English; plural you "あなたがた" (L, B), "あなたたち" (R) — Whether "we bless you" is the reply or the passers-by continuing is uncertain (m2); the English keeps both in one quotation, so Japanese does too.

Jaar is probably Kiriath-jearim (キルヤト・エアリム in 1SA), but the name is kept as the text has it (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 132:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/132/#v6) R · supplied_words · "神の箱" (R) for "it" — As the English R; m1 reads "it" as the ark (uncertain); 神の箱 is the 1SA form.

- [ ] [PSA 132:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/132/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · yatsits "輝く" (L), "光り輝く" (B), "明るく輝く" (R) — m1: shine or blossom (uncertain); "shine" as the English.

- [ ] [PSA 133:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/133/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · hashemen hattov "良い油" (L), "頭に注がれた貴い油" (B), "頭に注がれた特別な油" (R); pi middotaw "衣のえり" (every level) — The anointing of the high priest Aaron (m1); the opening of the robes is read as the collar (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 134:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/134/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · qodesh "聖所" (L, B), "聖なる所" (R); u-varkhu as 134:1 — The holy place is the sanctuary (m2, uncertain); lifting hands is the posture of prayer (m1), said at R.

- [ ] [PSA 135:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/135/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · zammeru "ほめ歌を歌え" (L, B), "ほめ歌を歌おう" (R); na'im "快い" (L), "喜ばしい" (B), "楽しい" (R) — Psalter forms for zimmer; what is pleasant is read as singing his praise (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 135:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/135/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Word for word as 115:8 at each level: yihyu "同じようになる" as a statement — The Hebrew of 135:18 is the same as 115:8; statement or wish is uncertain (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 136:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/136/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · beshiflenu "わたしたちが低くされていたとき" (L, B), "弱く、どうすることもできなかったとき" (R); zakhar "心に留めてくださった" (L), "覚えていてくださった" (B), "思い出してくださった" (R) — The singers' own story (m1); remembering is acting for them (m3); the low state is uncertain (m2).

What the right hand forgets is uncertain (m2, footnote); B and R supply playing, as the English does.

"なさった" avoids the passive-looking "された".

- [ ] [PSA 139:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/139/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · yeshufeni "押しつぶし" (L), "覆い尽くし" (B), "飲み込んでしまい" (R) — The verb is read as in the Hebrew text, 'crush' (m1, uncertain, footnote; many read 'cover'); B and R follow the English level's picture.

- [ ] [PSA 139:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/139/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · nora'ot nifleti "恐るべきほどに、不思議に造られている" (L), "畏れを覚えるほど驚くべきもの" (B), "驚くほど、すばらしく造ってくださった" (R) — The Hebrew text 'I am made wonderful' is followed (m1, uncertain, footnote; the old versions read 'you are wonderful'); R makes God the subject as the English R does (r-en-2).

- [ ] [PSA 140:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/140/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · Title as 13:1: "指揮する者のために。ダビデの賛歌。" (L), "音楽を導く者のために。ダビデの賛歌。" (B), "音楽を導く人のために。ダビデの歌。" (R) — Psalter title forms (PSA-1); the director is uncertain (m2).

B and R supply 'people' as the English does (r-en-1).
- [ ] [PSA 141:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/141/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · 'atsamenu "わたしたちの骨" (Hebrew text, m1, uncertain); she'ol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R) — Psalter rule for Sheol; the bones are kept as the Hebrew has them, though some read 'their bones'.
- [ ] [PSA 141:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/141/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · bemakhmoraw "自分の網" (L, B), "自分たちが仕掛けたわな" (R); yachad "共に" (L), "みな一緒に" (B), "みな" (R) — Each one's own nets (m1); together read as all at once (m2, uncertain). Imprecation R 〜ようにしてください (Psalter rule).
- [ ] [PSA 142:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/142/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · maskil ledavid "ダビデのマスキル" (L, B), "ダビデによる、教えるための歌" (R, English R 'A song to teach'); bamme'arah "洞穴"; tefillah "祈り" — Psalter title forms (PSA-1, PSA-6 52:1, PSA-8 78:1); maskil is uncertain (m2); the cave as 57:1 (1 Samuel 22, 24; m3). - [ ] [PSA 142:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/142/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · habbet / re'eh as requests to God: "見て、ご覧ください" (L), "見て、確かめてください" (B), "見てください" (R) — The two verbs are read as commands to God (m2, uncertain); the Psalter puts requests to God as 〜てください, never 見よ. manos "逃れ場" (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 142:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/142/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · yakhtiru "取り囲む" (L), "周りに集まる" (B, R); tigmol "豊かに報いてくださる" (L), "良くしてくださる" (B, R) as 13:6 — Gather around (m2, uncertain); gamal as PSA-1 13:6. masger "獄" (L, B), R "閉じ込められているこの場所" (m1, the trap of his distress).
- [ ] [PSA 143:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/143/#v6) L/B/R · supplied_words · "[慕う]" (L), "日照りに…待ち望んでいる" (B), "雨を必要とする" (R) supplied — The Hebrew has no verb (m2); B and R supply the drought and the rain as the English does (r-en-1). Selah セラ (uncertain, m3).

- [ ] [PSA 143:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/143/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · ruchakha tovah "あなたの良い霊" (L, R), "恵み深いあなたの霊" (B), not "聖霊" — God's own Spirit guiding him (m1, uncertain); as 139:7 the Psalter keeps "あなたの霊". retsonekha "みこころ" (L) as 103:21; "お喜びになる" / "お望みになる" avoid passive-looking forms.

- [ ] [PSA 144:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/144/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · banenu / benotenu "息子たち" / "娘たち" — Sons and daughters are set side by side, so 息子たち is used rather than the inclusive 子ら; no gender span, as the English has none.
- [ ] [PSA 144:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/144/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · perets / yotse't "破れ" / "出て行くこと" (L), "城壁が破られること" / "捕らわれて連れ出されること" (B, R) — No enemy breaking in, no one led away captive (m2, uncertain, footnote); B and R supply walls and exile as the English does (r-en-1). me-suballim "重く[子を]はらみ" (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 145:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/145/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · divrei nifle'otekha "奇しいみわざの事柄" (L), "不思議なみわざの物語" (B), "驚くべきこと" (R); 'asichah "思いめぐらす" (L, B), "よく考える" (R) — nifle'ot as 26:7 and siach as Psalm 119 (PSA-3, PSA-13); the 'matters' are the works and the words that tell them (m2, uncertain); the Hebrew text 'I will meditate' is kept (m4, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 145:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/145/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · nor'otekha "恐るべきわざ" (L), "畏るべきみわざ" (B), "おそれるべきこと" (R); gedullatekha singular "偉大さ" — nora' as 76:8 (PSA-8); 'your greatness' follows the form to be read (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 145:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/145/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · malkhut kol-'olamim "とこしえにわたる王国" (L), "永遠に続く王国" (B), "決して終わらない" (R); no line added for the missing nun — The Hebrew text has no nun line here (m1, uncertain, footnote); the Japanese follows it and adds nothing.
- [ ] [PSA 145:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/145/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · pote'ach 'et-yadekha "手を開かれ" (L, B), "手を大きく開かれると" (R), as 104:28; umasbia' … ratson "望みで満ち足らせて" (L), "望むものを与えて、満ち足らせて" (B) — The hand is worded as 104:28 ("あなたが手を開かれると"); the rest differs in the Hebrew (God satisfies every living thing with favour or desire, m1, uncertain), so it is drafted for this verse.
- [ ] [PSA 146:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/146/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · she'ein lo teshu'ah "救いをもたない" (L), "救う力のない" (B), "だれも…救うことはできない" (R) — Read as one who cannot save (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 147:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/147/#v1) L/B/R · word_order · ki tov zammerah … ki na'im "まことに、良いことだ、…ことは。まことに、[それは]快く" (L); B and R drop the two ki — As 92:2 (PSA-10), tov is put first at L; the ki may give reasons or mean 'how' (m2, uncertain), and what is pleasant is the singing (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 147:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/147/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · nidchei Yisra'el "イスラエルの追い散らされた者たち" (L), "散らされたイスラエルの人々" (B), "…追い出されたイスラエルの人々" (R); yekhannes "集めてくださる" — The banished of Isaiah 56:8 (m1, m3); the return from exile is the likely setting (m2, uncertain). "集めてくださる" avoids the passive-looking "集められる".
- [ ] [PSA 147:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/147/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · moneh mispar "数を数え" (L), "数を定め" (B), "いくつあるかを…知っておられる" (R) — Setting or knowing how many (m2, uncertain), as in Isaiah 40:26 (m1); "名で呼んでおられる" / "お与えになる" avoid passive-looking 呼ばれる / 与えられる.
- [ ] [PSA 147:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/147/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · chatsir "草" (Hebrew text) — The Hebrew text is followed (m2, uncertain); some witnesses add a line about plants for people's work.
- [ ] [PSA 147:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/147/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · Jerusalem / Zion addressed with "おまえ" (L, B); shabbechi "たたえよ", halleli "賛美せよ"; R "エルサレムの人々よ … たたえよう" — The city is addressed おまえ, as 116:19 and 122:2 (PSA-13, PSA-14); shibbach and hallel kept apart (PSA-7). R calls God's people with 〜しよう and supplies 'people of' as the English R (r-en-1). 147:12-20 is kept with 147:1-11 as one psalm (m1).
- [ ] [PSA 148:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/148/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · choq "定め" (L, B), "きまり" (R); velo' ya'avor "過ぎ去ることがない" (L, B), "決して変わらない" (R) — Psalter word for choq (PSA-1, PSA-13); 'it' is read as the decree (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 148:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/148/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · 'esh "火" (L, B), "稲妻" (R); qitor "煙" (L), "霧" (B, R) — Fire is lightning (m1, r-en-1); qitor is a thick mist or cloud (m2, uncertain); the wind doing his word echoes 147:15-18 (m3).
- [ ] [PSA 148:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/148/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · vayyarem qeren "角を高く上げてくださった" (L, B), "強くしてくださった" (R); tehillah "賛美" (L), "賛美となる" (B), "賛美する理由がある" (R) — qeren 角 (L, B), 力 (R) by the Psalter rule (PSA-1), here the strength given (m1, uncertain); praise for his faithful read as a reason for them to praise (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 149:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/149/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · bekhavod "栄光のうちに" (L), "その栄光の中で" (B), "神が与えてくださる誉れ" (R) — The honour God has given them (m1, uncertain); kavod is 栄光, R 誉れ where the English R says honor (PSA-1).
- [ ] [PSA 149:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/149/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · mishpat katuv "書き記された裁き" (L, B), "神が書き記された裁き" (R); hadar "誉れ" — The sentence God has decreed (m1, uncertain); the task itself is the honour (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 150:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/150/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · beqodsho "その聖所" (L), "その方の聖なる所" (B), "神の聖なる場所" (R); birqia' 'uzzo "その力の大空" (L), "その力を表す広い大空" (B), "その力を示す大空" (R) — The holy place may be in heaven or on earth (m3, uncertain); raqia' 大空 as 19:2 (PSA-2), the dome of the sky (m4).
- [ ] [PSA 150:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/150/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · shofar "角笛" (every level); nevel "琴", kinnor "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R) — Psalter instrument names (PSA-5 47:6, PSA-8 71:22); the temple's stringed instruments (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 150:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/150/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · tof "タンバリン"; minnim "弦" (L, B), "弦楽器" (R); 'ugav "笛" — tof as 149:3 (m1); minnim are strings (m2); 'ugav a wind instrument (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [PSA 150:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/150/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · tsiltselei-shama' "響きのシンバル" (L), "澄んだ音のシンバル" (B); tsiltselei teru'ah "歓呼のシンバル" (L), "叫びのように鳴り響くシンバル" (B) — Two kinds or two ways of playing the cymbals (m1, uncertain); teru'ah is the shout of joy (m2).
- [ ] [PSA 150:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/psa/150/#v6) L/B/R · register · tehallel "…はみな、{{divine_name:主|ヤハ}}を賛美せよ" (L, B), vocative "…生き物よ、…賛美せよ！" (R) — The third-person jussive is put as 〜は…せよ, as 118:2 (PSA-13), so the book ends on a call rather than a weaker 〜ように; R calls all creatures, so 〜せよ (PSA-1). kol hanneshamah is everything that breathes (m1, uncertain).

## 箴言 (12)

- [ ] [PRO 8:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/8/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · qanani "わたしを得ておられた" (L), "わたしを生み出された" (B), "わたしを造られた" (R), as the English levels read it; God in the third person "その方" with light respect forms through 8:22-31 — The verb is uncertain (m1, footnoted); each level follows its English reading. God's acts take 〜られた as the book's convention.
- [ ] [PRO 8:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/8/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · amon "名匠" (L), "腕の良い職人" (B), "熟練した職人" (R) — Follows the reading 'master worker' that the English takes; the word is uncertain (m1) and the English footnote gives the other readings.
- [ ] [PRO 14:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/14/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · "自分の造り主" (L) keeps the Hebrew's open "his Maker"; Balanced and Readable "その人を造られた方 / 神" as the English, with respect forms for God — m2: the Maker may be the oppressor's or the poor man's; Literal leaves it open as the Hebrew does.
- [ ] [PRO 15:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/15/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · Sheol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R); Abaddon "アバドン" (L, B, as Job 26:6 and 28:22) — The book's forms for Sheol (1:12); Balanced keeps the name, as Job does, where the English Balanced translates it ("Destruction"); m1 gives its meaning.
- [ ] [PRO 16:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/16/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · kipper "贖われる" (L), "償われる" (B), "取り除かれる" (R); 'avon "咎" (L, B), "罪" (R); sur mera' "悪から離れる" (L), "遠ざかる" (B), as JOB 28:28 — m1: wrong is atoned for by loyal love and faithfulness; Literal keeps the cultic word, Balanced and Readable say how the wrong is put right without implying a sacrifice.
- [ ] [PRO 17:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/17/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · naval "愚か者" (L, B), "愚かな人" (R), with kesil (also 17:21) — m1: the nabal is the boorish fool; the book keeps one Japanese family for its fools (PRO-1), so the two Hebrew words are not distinguished.
- [ ] [PRO 21:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/21/#v4) L/B · word_choice · nir "ともしび" kept as an image (L, B), as in 20:20, 27; Readable "明かりにして生きている" with the English Readable — The reading is uncertain (m2, footnote); the drafts follow the English reading 'lamp'.
- [ ] [PRO 21:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/21/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · tsaddiq read as God (m1): "正しい方" with the respect forms used for God (心を留められ, 倒される), not "正しい者" — With the meaning decision; the honorific verbs show the reader that God is meant. The reading is uncertain (footnote).
- [ ] [PRO 22:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/22/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · shalishim read 'thirty' (m1): "三十[の格言]" (L), "三十の格言" (B), "三十のことわざ" (R), with mashal's book forms — The supplied noun follows the book's word for a saying (格言 L, B; ことわざ R). The reading is uncertain (footnote).
- [ ] [PRO 29:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/29/#v10) L · word_choice · "その命を求める" (L), as open as the Hebrew; "その命を気づかう" (B) and Readable "守ろうとする" as the English — The English reads "seek his life" as caring for the blameless (meaning m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the bare verb.
- [ ] [PRO 30:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/30/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · hammassa' "託宣" (L, B), as massa' in 2 Kings 9:25; Readable "大切な知らせ" for the English Readable "message"; not the place name "マサ" — The meaning decision reads massa' as "the oracle", a weighty message (m1), not as the tribe or land of Massa; Japanese follows it.
- [ ] [PRO 31:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/pro/31/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · Lemuel "レムエル" (a personal name); massa' "託宣" (L, B), "大切な知らせ" (R), as 30:1; not "マサの王" — The meaning decisions read massa' as "an oracle" (m1) and Lemuel as a personal name (m3), so Massa is not a place here; the forms match Agur's heading in 30:1.

## コヘレトの言葉 (5)

- [ ] [ECC 2:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ecc/2/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · shiddah ve-shiddot "側女と側女たち" (L), "多くの側女" (B), "大勢の女たちのいる後宮" (R) — m2 (uncertain): many women for the king's pleasure; the levels follow the English concubine(s) / harem. The word occurs only here, and older readings (musical instruments, cups) are left to the meaning footnote.
- [ ] [ECC 3:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ecc/3/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · ha'olam "永遠" (L), "永遠を思う思い" (B), "いつまでも続くものを求める思い" (R) — m2 (uncertain): a sense of time beyond the present, of the whole that people cannot see. Literal keeps the bare noun; Balanced and Readable say what is put in the heart, as the English B and R do. The noun is read by some as "the world" or "darkness"; that is left to the meaning footnote.
- [ ] [ECC 8:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ecc/8/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · ruach here "風" at every level, as the English "wind", not "霊" (3:19, 12:7) — m1 (uncertain): the wind, and also the breath of life; the English chose wind, and Japanese follows it, leaving the second sense to the meaning footnote.
- [ ] [ECC 11:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ecc/11/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · ha-ruach here "霊" (L), "命の霊" (B, R), as in 3:19, where the English says "the breath (of life)" — m1 (uncertain): the breath of life that comes to an unborn child; 息 is kept for hevel through the book, so the life-breath is 霊 here as in 3:19 and 12:7, with "命の" added at Balanced and Readable as the English adds "of life".
- [ ] [ECC 12:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ecc/12/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · shaqed "アーモンドの木", chagav "ばった", 'aviyyonah "ふうちょうぼくの実" (L, B), "欲望" (R, as the English R "desire") — m1-m3 (uncertain): pictures of old age; the plants and the insect are named plainly, and Readable says what the caper-berry stands for, as the English R does. 人 / 人はみな with no gender span.

## 雅歌 (18)

- [ ] [SNG 2:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/2/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · chavatselet "クロッカス"; shoshannah "ゆり" (kana, as 1KI 7:19); Sharon "シャロン" (R "シャロン平野") — The English has crocus, with the uncertainty in the meaning decision; the older "シャロンのばら" is not followed. R names the plain as the English R does.
- [ ] [SNG 2:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/2/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · tappuach "りんごの木" (and "りんご" 2:5, 7:9, 8:5) — The English says apple tree, with the uncertainty in the meaning decision.
- [ ] [SNG 2:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/2/#v16) L/B/R · supplied_words · haro'eh "[群れを]飼う" (L), "群れを飼っている" (B, R); the same in 6:3 — Japanese 飼う needs an object, so the flock is supplied; the meaning decision leaves open whether he feeds himself among the lilies, which Japanese cannot keep in one verb.
- [ ] [SNG 2:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/2/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · sov "向きを変えて"; Bether "バテル" — 向きを変えて keeps both readings of "turn" (come back, or turn away). The Hebrew is bater, and バテル keeps it apart from Bethel ベテル.
- [ ] [SNG 4:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/4/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · letalpiyyot "石を段々に積んで" (L), "幾段にも石を積んで" (B), "石を積み重ねて" (R) — As the meaning decision (in rows of stone), which is uncertain.
- [ ] [SNG 4:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/4/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · achoti kallah "わたしの妹、花嫁よ" (L), "わたしの妹、わたしの花嫁よ" (B), "妹のように大切な花嫁よ" (R), through 4:9-5:2 — Japanese must choose older or younger; 妹 has long been a word of love for a woman in Japanese poetry (いも), so it reads as endearment, not kinship. R follows the English R "dear as a sister".
- [ ] [SNG 5:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/5/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · The unnamed voice in neutral 〜なさい; dodim "愛する者たちよ" (L), "愛し合う者たちよ" (B), "愛し合う人たちよ" (R) — The meaning decisions leave open who speaks and whether dodim means the lovers or love; Japanese names no one and does not narrow "lovers" to the couple.
- [ ] [SNG 5:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/5/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · migdelot merqachim "香油の塔" (L), "香りのよい草の茂み" (B) — L keeps the Hebrew "towers of perfumes"; B follows the English B "mounds of sweet herbs" (the text variant noted in the meaning decision).
- [ ] [SNG 6:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/6/#v2) L/B/R · supplied_words · lir'ot "[群れを]飼い" (L), "群れを飼い" (B, R), as 2:16 — Japanese 飼う needs an object; the same choice as 2:16 and 6:3.
- [ ] [SNG 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · Tirzah "ティルツァ" (as 1KI 14:17); ayummah kannidgalot "旗を掲げた[軍勢]のように畏れを抱かせる" (L, B), the same in 6:10 — 畏れ is awe, not fear; the image of an army under banners is kept, with its uncertainty in the meaning decision.
- [ ] [SNG 6:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/6/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · Ammi-nadib "アミナディブ"; nafshi "わたしの魂" (L), "わたしの心" (B), "わたしの恋しい思い" (R) — One of the hardest verses; the name is kept as the English has it (apart from Amminadab アミナダブ of MAT 1:4), and the verb stays as close to "set me" as Japanese allows.
- [ ] [SNG 7:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/7/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · hashulammit "シュラムの女" (every level); Mahanaim "マハナイム" (as GEN 32:3, 2SA 2:8) — The meaning decision gives "the woman from Shulem" first, with a name like Solomon's as the alternative in the footnote; Japanese uses the gentilic form readers know.
- [ ] [SNG 7:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/7/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · ahavah as an address "愛よ" — He calls her "love" itself, as the English does; the footnoted meaning decision keeps the uncertainty.
- [ ] [SNG 7:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/7/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · kefarim "村々" (L, B), "村" (R) — As the English; the alternative is in the meaning decision's footnote.
- [ ] [SNG 8:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/8/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · telammedeni "母はわたしを教えるだろう" (L), "母はわたしに教えてくれるだろう" (B, R) — As the English, which reads "she"; the alternative "you would teach me" is in the meaning decision's footnote.
- [ ] [SNG 8:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/8/#v6) L · setting_alternative · "{{divine_name:主|ヤハ}}の炎" at L only, as the English L; B "激しい炎", R "激しく燃える火" with no span — The same span as EXO 15:2 for the short form Yah; the English B and R read "a mighty flame", so no divine name appears there.
- [ ] [SNG 8:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/8/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · shalom "平安" — Inner peace and well-being in his eyes, so 平安 by the style guide's rule; the reading "one who brings peace" stays in the meaning decision's footnote.
- [ ] [SNG 8:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/sng/8/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · berach "逃げて" (L), "急いで" (B, R) — As the English levels; whether toward her or away is left open, as the meaning decision says. The animal words as 2:9 and 2:17.

## イザヤ書 (84)

- [ ] [ISA 2:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/2/#v9) L/B/R · register · A request to God in an oracle is "〜てほしい / 〜ないでほしい" (as Job and Lamentations), never "〜てください" or an imperative; forgive "赦す" (L, B), "ゆるす" (R). — The prophet asks God not to pardon (m2); the plain request keeps the verse in the poetry register without commanding God.
- [ ] [ISA 3:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/3/#v18) L/B/R · word_order · The list of finery (3:18-23) follows the verb, which Japanese puts in 3:18; each later verse adds its items with "、" and 3:23 closes with "……である。". Many of the … — Holding the verb back for six verses would leave every verse but the last without a predicate; the items keep the Hebrew order.
- [ ] [ISA 4:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/4/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · tsemach YHWH "the branch of the LORD": "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の若枝" (L, B), "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}が育ててくださる若枝" (R); tsemach is "若枝" through the prophets … — The meaning decision leaves open whether the branch is a coming king (m1); "若枝" is a plant word that can carry either reading, and one word lets the later messianic Branch passages be heard together.
- [ ] [ISA 4:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/4/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · ruach mishpat / ruach ba'er: "裁きの霊", "焼き尽くす霊" (L, B), Readable spelling out the act (裁き, 火のように焼き尽くす) as the English Readable. — The meaning decision leaves open whether God's Spirit or his power is meant (m3); "霊" keeps the Hebrew word and both readings, without the NT title "聖霊".
- [ ] [ISA 7:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/7/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · ben Tav'al "タベエルの子" (L, B), "タベエルの息子" (R), the Ezra 4:7 form of the name. — The man is unknown (m3); the conventional form of the name is kept, as in Ezra, rather than imitating the vowel pun of the Hebrew text.
- [ ] [ISA 7:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/isa/7/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · ha'almah: "そのおとめ" (L, B), "一人のおとめ" (R, as the English Readable's indefinite "a young woman"). Jeremiah proclaims God's word to the king plainly …

- [ ] [LAM 2:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lam/2/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · sukko "his booth" is "その仮小屋" (L, B), and the temple it pictures is named at Readable, as the English Readable; za'am 'appo "the indignation of his anger" … — The temple is torn down as easily as a garden shelter (m1, uncertain); Readable names what the picture means.
- [ ] [LAM 4:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lam/4/#v6) B/R · word_choice · 'avon and chatta't as "罰" (B, R) where Literal has "咎" and "罪", following the English Balanced and Readable "punishment". — The words mean the punishment that iniquity brings (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the words themselves.
- [ ] [LAM 4:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lam/4/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · nezireha "her princes" is "その君たち" (L, B), "シオンの身分の高い人たち" (R), as the English "princes"; sappir "サファイア" (L), "ラピスラズリ" (B, as the English Balanced), "青い宝石" (R). — The word may mean Nazirites or nobles (m1, uncertain); the English reads nobles at every level, and Japanese follows.
- [ ] [LAM 4:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/lam/4/#v22) L/B/R · register · Zion is addressed as "あなた" (L, B), "あなたたち" (R), and Edom as "おまえ", "おまえたち", so the two daughters stand apart, though the Hebrew addresses both with one form. — Comfort to Zion and judgment on Edom in one verse (m1, m3); the pronouns mark the turn at Balanced and Readable.

- [ ] [EZK 1:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/1/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · chashmal: "琥珀" (L), "輝く金属" (B), "光り輝く金属" (R), as the English amber / gleaming metal / shining metal; the same at 1:27 and 8:2. — What the substance is, is uncertain (m1, footnoted); the Japanese follows each English level. "琥珀" is not Jōyō but is common and stays at Literal only.
- [ ] [EZK 1:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/1/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · tarshish "かんらん石" (L, B), "かんらん石という宝石" (R, as the English R "a gem called chrysolite"). — The stone is uncertain (m1, footnoted); かんらん石 is the form of Exodus 28:20 and Daniel 10:6 for this word.
- [ ] [EZK 1:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/1/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · gabbim "外輪" (L, B; 1 Kings 7:33), "車輪の外側の輪" (R); yir'ah "恐ろしさ" (L), "恐ろしいほど" (B), "見るのも恐ろしい" (R). — The rims inspired fear (m1, uncertain); Japanese says this with 恐ろしい at each level, as the English awesomeness / awe-inspiring / frightening.
- [ ] [EZK 2:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/2/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · ruach "霊" (every level), for the English capital "Spirit". — The LORD's Spirit giving Ezekiel strength (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the OT Japanese does not say 聖霊, and "霊" leaves the reading open as the meaning decision does.
- [ ] [EZK 2:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/2/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · sarvim vesallonim "いばらとあざみ" (L, B), "とげのある草やいばら" (R); 'aqrabbim "さそり" (kana, every level). — Prickly plants (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese uses the common pair for thorny weeds, as the English pairs briers and thorns. The people's hostility is the point (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 3:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/3/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · barukh kevod YHWH mimmeqomo "主の栄光は、その場所からほめたたえられよ" (L), "…ほめたたえられるように" (B), "主の栄光を、それがある場所からほめたたえよ" (R, as the English R "Praise"). — The text as it stands (m1, footnoted); Japanese follows the English reading at each level, with ほめたたえる for barukh as the Psalms.
- [ ] [EZK 3:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/3/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · mar bachamat ruchi "苦い思いで、わたしの霊の憤りのうちに" (L), "苦い思いと心の憤りを抱いて" (B), "苦々しく、腹立たしい思いで" (R). — Ezekiel's own bitterness and anger (m1, uncertain, footnoted); ruach here is his own spirit, so 霊 only at Literal and 心 at Balanced.
- [ ] [EZK 3:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/3/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · mikhshol "つまずき" (L, B), "つまずかせるもの" (R); tsaddiq "正しい者" (L), "正しい人" (B, R). — The LORD brings the fall that follows the turning (m1, uncertain, footnoted); "つまずき" is the plain word and leaves the sense as open as the Hebrew.
- [ ] [EZK 3:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/3/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · ne'elam "口がきけなくなる" (L, B), "話せないようにする" (R); 'ish mokhiach "戒める者" (L), "責める" (B), "叱る" (R). — Ezekiel will speak only when the LORD speaks (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the words also serve at 24:27 and 33:22.
- [ ] [EZK 4:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/4/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · machavat barzel "鉄の焼き板" (L), "鉄板" (B), "パンを焼く鉄の板" (R); 'ot "しるし" (every level). — The griddle of Leviticus 2:5 (鉄板); an unbreakable barrier (m1, uncertain). "しるし" is an act that shows what is coming (m3).
- [ ] [EZK 4:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/4/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "三百九十日" in kanji numerals at every level, where the English writes 390. — Japanese running text uses kanji numerals (style 5); the number itself is the Hebrew's (m1, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 5:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/5/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · shiqqutsim "忌むべきもの" (L), "忌まわしい偶像" (B), "汚らわしい偶像" (R); 'egra' "切り倒す" (every level). — Idols and the objects of idol worship (m2), as Jeremiah 7:30; the verb is uncertain (m3, footnoted), and "切り倒す" follows the English "cut down".
- [ ] [EZK 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · gillulim "汚らわしい偶像" (L), "偶像" (B, R), as the English filthy idols / idols, through the book; chammanim "香の台" (L, B), "香をたく台" (R). — A word of contempt for idols (m1, uncertain, footnoted), Ezekiel's own word for them; Literal shows the contempt as the English L does. The incense stands are uncertain (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 6:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/6/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · ye'shemu "罪を負い" (L), "罪に定められ" (B, R). — The altars are condemned (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the English held guilty / condemned.
- [ ] [EZK 6:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/6/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · zanah "淫行をする" (L), "淫行にふける" (B), "裏切る" / "多くの恋人を追いかける女のように" (R); nishbarti "わたしが打ち砕かれた" (L, B), "わたしの心を傷つけた" (R). — The Hosea table for unfaithfulness to the LORD (m2); the LORD's grief (m1, uncertain, footnoted) is said without a respect form, since God speaks of himself.
- [ ] [EZK 6:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/6/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · Diblah "ディブラ" (every level), as the Hebrew and the English; midbar "荒れ野". — The text may mean Riblah (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the Japanese keeps the written name as the English does.
- [ ] [EZK 7:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · ra'ah 'achat "ただ一つの災い" (L), "ほかに例のない災い" (B), "これまでにない災い" (R); hinneh "見なさい" (L, B, as the English "See"). — One disaster, unique (m1, uncertain, footnoted); never 見よ for hinneh.
- [ ] [EZK 7:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · qets / heqits "終わり" / "目を覚ました"; the play on words is not imitated. — The Hebrew plays on the sound of "end" and "awake" (m1, footnoted); Japanese keeps the sense and leaves the play to the note.
- [ ] [EZK 7:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · tsefirah "破滅" (every level, here and in 7:10); mehumah "騒乱" (L), "恐慌" (B), "うろたえる声" (R); hed "喜びの叫び". — The word is uncertain (m1, footnoted); "破滅" follows the English "doom". Panic instead of harvest songs (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 7:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · matteh "杖" (every level); zadon "高ぶり" (L, B), "おごり" (R). — Wickedness is ripe for judgment (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the images of the rod and the bud are kept at every level, as the English does.
- [ ] [EZK 7:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · hamas "暴虐" (L, B), "暴力" (R); hamehem / noah "富", "輝き" (L), "価値あるもの" (B, R). — Rare words (m2, uncertain, footnoted); the Japanese follows the English reading at each level.
- [ ] [EZK 7:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v13) L/B/R · setting_alternative · The English gender spans (their / his) become "自分" with no span; the hard verse (m2) follows the English reading at each level. — Japanese is already inclusive (style 7a); the verse is uncertain and footnoted.
- [ ] [EZK 7:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "all knees will go as water": "膝は水[のよう]になる" (L), "水のようになる" (B), "水のように力がなくなる" (R). — Weakness from terror (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese keeps the water image the English keeps.
- [ ] [EZK 7:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · tsevi 'edyo "その飾りの美しさ" (L), "美しい装身具" (B, R); to'evot / shiqqutsim "忌み嫌うべきもの" / "忌むべきもの" (L), "忌まわしい行いの像" / "忌まわしい偶像" (B), "汚らわしい偶像" (R). — Their gold and silver jewelry (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the two words for abominations in their book-wide forms. The Hebrew's singular "he" is kept at Literal only, as the English L.
- [ ] [EZK 7:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · tsefuni "わたしの秘められた[所]" (L), "わたしの大切な場所" (B, R). — Probably the temple (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese leaves it unnamed as the English does.
- [ ] [EZK 7:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/7/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · 'aseh haratoq "鎖を作れ！" (L, B), "捕虜をつなぐ鎖を用意しなさい！" (R). — Chains for captives (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the command is in the oracle forms (〜せよ L; B keeps the short imperative for its force).
- [ ] [EZK 8:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/8/#v2) L/B/R · setting_alternative · The figure of 1:26-27 is "その方" with no deity_pronoun span, and chashmal "琥珀" / "輝く金属" / "光り輝く金属" as 1:4, 1:27. — The figure is the one on the throne, who speaks as God (m1); Japanese has no capitals (style 7), and the vision's words are kept as in chapter 1 (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 8:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/8/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · semel haqqin'ah hammaqneh "ねたみを引き起こす、ねたみの像" (L), "ねたみの像、ねたみを引き起こす像" (B), "ねたみの像、つまり神にねたみを起こさせる偶像" (R). — An idol at the temple gate that roused the LORD's jealousy (m3, uncertain); "ねたみ" is the word of 5:13 for the LORD's passionate claim.
- [ ] [EZK 8:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/8/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "so that I go far from my sanctuary": "[わたしが]わたしの聖所から遠ざかるため" (L), "わたしをわたしの聖所から遠ざけるもの" (B), "わたしを自分の神殿から遠くへ追いやろうとしている" (R). — Who goes far is not stated (m2, uncertain, footnoted); the English reads the LORD, so does the Japanese, with the subject in brackets at Literal.
- [ ] [EZK 8:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/8/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · Jaazaniah son of Shaphan "シャファンの子ヤアザンヤ" (L, B), "シャファンの息子ヤアザンヤ" (R); miqteret "香炉" (L, B; 2 Chronicles 26:19), "香をたくための皿" (R). — Shaphan as 2 Kings 22 (シャファン); Jaazaniah as 2 Kings 25:23 (ヤアザンヤ), a different man (m2, uncertain). The English R "a pan for burning incense".
- [ ] [EZK 9:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/9/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · pequddot ha'ir: "都を罰する者たち" (L, R), "都を罰するために任じられた者たち" (B), as the English; Jerusalem as "都" in chapters 9-11. — The phrase is uncertain (m1, footnoted): those appointed to punish. "都" is the usual word for Jerusalem as the capital and keeps the city from being 彼女.
- [ ] [EZK 9:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/9/#v8) L/B/R · register · Ezekiel to God in prose: です・ます at every level with light respect forms ("滅ぼされるのですか", "滅ぼしてしまわれるのですか"), "ああ、{{divine_name:主なる神|主ヤハウェ}}よ"; Readable "ああ、そんな、" for … — notes/EZK-EZK-1.md (4:14). "とんでもありません", used at 4:14, answers a command; here the cry is alarm and grief (m1), so Readable uses a cry of dismay. she'erit "残りの者" (L, B), "残っている人々" (R), as Isaiah. "そんな" is the spontaneous cry of dismay ("no, it cannot be"), stronger than a comment on the event; kept …
- [ ] [EZK 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/10/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · 10:1 repeats 1:22, 26 in chapter 1's Japanese: raqia' "天蓋" (L, B), "丸天井" (R); "サファイアの石"; "王座の似姿の有様" (L), "王座のように見えるもの" (B, R); keruvim "ケルビム" (one "ケルブ") at … — The throne vision of chapter 1 returns (m2), so the same words let the reader see it is the same throne; the creatures are now named cherubim (m1, footnoted). Readable adds "深い青色の宝石" as the English R "a deep blue stone" (m3).
- [ ] [EZK 10:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/10/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · kol besaram "全身" (L, B), "体じゅう" (R); "周りじゅう目で満ちていた" as 1:18 (L, B). — Whose body is meant is uncertain (m1); Japanese, like the English, leaves it to the cherubim at L and B and says so at R as the English R does.
- [ ] [EZK 10:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/10/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · The name "旋回する車輪" (L, B), "回る車輪" (R), in 「 」 as the English quotation marks, and the same words as 10:2, 6. — A name given to the wheels (m1, uncertain); the Japanese keeps one wording for galgal throughout the chapter.
- [ ] [EZK 10:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/10/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "自分の顔の向いている方へまっすぐ進んだ" (L), "まっすぐ前へ進んだ" (B, R), as 1:9, 12. — The same motion as chapter 1 (m2); "their appearance and themselves" (m1, uncertain) is kept at L, B and left out at R as the English R does.
- [ ] [EZK 11:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · chosheve 'awen veyo'atsim 'atsat ra' "不正をたくらみ、悪い助言を与えている" (L), "悪をたくらみ、悪い助言を与えている" (B), "悪いことをたくらみ、…悪い助言を与えている" (R). — The counsel of the king's officials, probably to resist Babylon (m1, uncertain); 'etsah "助言" as 7:26.
- [ ] [EZK 11:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "家を建てる[時]は近くない" (L), "家を建てる時はまだ近くない" (B), "今は家を建てる時ではない" (R); sir "鍋" (R "料理の鍋"), basar "肉". — The saying is obscure (m1, footnoted): the leaders feel secure, the walls protecting them as a pot protects the choice meat (m2). The English single quotes inside God's words become 『 』.
- [ ] [EZK 11:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · challeikhem "あなたがたに殺された者" (L), "多くの人を殺し" (B, R), as the English. — Those killed through their rule (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the noun, B and R make it a verb as the English.
- [ ] [EZK 11:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · hotsi' "[わたしは]…連れ出す" (L), "わたしが…連れ出す" (B, R): the bare verb read as God's first person, as the English. — The verb form is uncertain (m2); the only ones who stay in the 'pot' are the dead (m1).
- [ ] [EZK 11:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · chuqqai "わたしの定め" (L, B), "わたしのきまり" (R); mishpatai "わたしの掟" (L, B), "わたしのおきて" (R), and the nations' mishpatim in the same word. — The Pentateuch forms (Leviticus 18-19, Deuteronomy 5; notes/EZK-EZK-3.md TORAH TERMS), which chapters 18 and 20 also use; 5:6-7 now uses them too. The LORD's judgments are set against those of the nations (m1). The relative clause "in whose statutes" is made a new sentence at every level.
- [ ] [EZK 11:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · 'anshe ge'ullatekha "あなたの贖いの[権利を持つ]人々" (L), "あなたを買い戻す権利を持つ親族" (B), left in "あなたの親族" at R as the English R; the people of Jerusalem speak plainly with no … — The kinsmen who had the duty to redeem a family's land (m1, footnoted), Ruth's go'el (買い戻す); "贖い" keeps the Hebrew word at Literal. The Jerusalemites' scorn for the exiles is plain speech (m2, m3).
- [ ] [EZK 11:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · miqdash me'at "しばらくの間、…聖所となった" (L, B), "しばらくの間、わたし自身が彼らの神殿となってきた" (R), as the English; "小さな聖所" left to the footnote. — me'at can mean 'a little' or 'for a little while' (m1, footnoted); the LORD is present with the exiles, not only in Jerusalem (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 11:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · lev 'echad "一つの心" (L, B), "心を一つにし" (R); lev ha'even / lev basar "石の心" / "肉の心" (L, B), "石のように固い心" / "柔らかく生きた心" (R), as the English R; ruach "霊". — The promise repeated in 36:26 (m2), so these words are to be kept there; Readable explains stone and flesh as hardness and responsiveness (m3). "one heart" follows the Hebrew text (m1, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 11:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/11/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "…の心に、その心が向かって行く" (L), "心が…に傾いている" (B), "…に心を向けている" (R); "bring their way on their heads" in the 9:10 Japanese. — The phrase is hard (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps both 'hearts' of the Hebrew. The idiom is the one of 9:10 (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 12:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · kele golah "捕囚の荷物" (L), "捕囚に出る人のように荷物をまとめ" (B), "捕虜として連れて行かれる人のように、荷物をまとめなさい" (R); galah "捕囚として出て行く / 移る" (L, B); 'ulai "もしかすると…かもしれない". — The few things a deportee could carry (m1); the act is a sign (m2). The 'though / for' of the last clause is uncertain (m3); Japanese takes it as concessive with the English.
- [ ] [EZK 12:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · mofet "しるし" at every level (as 'ot in 4:3), R "警告するためのしるし" as the English R; "顔を覆って、地を見ないように". — An act that points to what will happen (m1); covering the face shows grief and points to the king's fate (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 12:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · nasi' "君主" (L, B), "指導者" (R), as notes/EZK-EZK-1.md; hammassa' "託宣" (L, B), "お告げ" (R), as Isaiah; the Literal keeps the abrupt Hebrew with brackets. — The verse plays on massa' as 'oracle' and 'burden' (m1, footnoted), not imitated; the prince is Zedekiah (m2), not named in the text.
- [ ] [EZK 12:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · reshet / metsudah "網" / "わな" (kana; the same words as 17:20); "カルデア人の地バビロン" (L, B), "バビロニア人の地バビロン" (R), as 1:3; "それを見ることなく" without explaining the blinding. — Zedekiah was blinded before he was taken to Babylon (m1, footnoted, 2 Kings 25:7); the text keeps the riddle, as the English. The LORD himself captures the king (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 12:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · 'am ha'arets "国の民" (L, B), "国中の人々" (R), as 7:27; hamas "暴虐" (L, B), "暴力" (R); tesham "荒れ果てる". — Who 'the people of the land' are here is uncertain (m1); the book-wide forms are kept. The siege and its cause in one clause (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 12:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/12/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · mashal "ことわざ" at every level; "日々は長引き、すべての幻は消え去る" (L), "日々は過ぎて行き、どの幻も実現しない" (B), "時は過ぎて行くが、幻は一つも実現しない" (R). — A saying mocking prophecy (m1); 'al 'admat Yisra'el kept as "イスラエルの地について" (m2, uncertain); 12:23 and 18:2-3 use the same word.
- [ ] [EZK 13:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · peratsot "破れ口" (L, B), "城壁が崩れた所" (R); gader "石垣" (L, B), "壁" (R); yom YHWH "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の日" at every level. — Going up into the breaches is standing between the people and judgment (m1); the day of the LORD is here the coming judgment (m2). Who stands is uncertain (m3); B, R take it as the house of Israel, as the English.
- [ ] [EZK 13:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · The false prophets' formula ne'um YHWH in their mouths: 「{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}の言葉」 (L), 「{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}は告げる」 (B), 「{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}はこう言われる」 (R), … — The prophets claim the LORD's own formula (m1); at R they speak of the LORD with the respect form 言われる, as people speaking of God do (JER-1). They expect what they said to come true (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 13:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "Peace, when there is no peace" in the Jeremiah 6:14 Japanese ("平和" / 「平和だ！」 / "平和はない"); chayits "薄い壁" (L, B), "弱い壁" (R); tafel "しっくい" at every level. — The charge of Jeremiah 6:14 and 8:11 (m1); the whitewashed wall is weak plans made to look sound (m2). chayits is uncertain (m3).
- [ ] [EZK 13:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · geshem shotef "押し流す雨" (L), "大雨" (B), "激しい雨" (R); 'avne 'elgavish "雹の石" (L), "雹" (B), "ひょう" (R, kana); the hail addressed at L, B ("あなたがた、雹の石よ"). — The storm is addressed directly (m1, uncertain); the storm of God's judgment is the Babylonian attack (m2). R follows the English R, which does not address the hail.
- [ ] [EZK 13:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "そのただ中で滅びる" (L), "その下で滅びる" (B), "その下敷きになって死ぬ" (R), as the English. — Whether 'it' is the wall or the city it stands for is uncertain (m1); Japanese, like the English, keeps the wall.
- [ ] [EZK 13:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · kesatot "ひも" (L, B), "まじないのひも" (R); mispachot "覆い布" (L), "ベール" (B, R); nefashot "魂" (L), "命" (B, R); tsud "狩る" (L, B), "わなにかける" (R); hoy as 13:3. — Objects used in some kind of magic (m1, footnoted); souls are lives, living persons (m2). The last question is uncertain (m3); the Hebrew 'my hands' (m4) is read as 'the hands' with the English.
- [ ] [EZK 13:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · chillel "汚した" (L, B), "わたしの名をはずかしめた" (R); sha'ale se'orim / petote lechem "一握りの大麦" / "パンのかけら". — Treating the LORD's name as common by using it for gain (m3); the small fees for their services (m1, uncertain). R makes 'name' explicit as m3 says.
- [ ] [EZK 13:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/13/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · lefore chot "鳥のように" at every level; sham "そこで" at L only; hineni 'el "わたしは…に立ち向かう" as 13:8. — Both 'like birds' and 'there' are uncertain (m1, m2); B and R leave sham out as the English.
- [ ] [EZK 14:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v4) L/B/R · setting_alternative · 'ish 'ish "だれでも" (L), "だれか" (B), "イスラエルの人々" (R), and "その人" / "彼ら", with no gender spans where the English L, B have {{gender:anyone|any man}}, … — Japanese is already inclusive here (style §7a), so no span is needed; the answer is judgment, not an oracle (m2). The phrase 'according to the multitude of his idols' follows the text as read (m1).
- [ ] [EZK 14:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · litpos ... belibbam "その心において捕らえる" (L), "心を…つかむ" (B, R); nazoru "離れてしまった". — To grip their hearts and bring them back (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 14:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · natati panai "わたしの顔を向け" (L), "顔を向けて立ち向かい" (B), "立ち向かう" (R); 'ot "しるし", meshalim "ことわざ" (L, as 12:22), "もの笑いの種" (B), R as the English; hikhrati "断つ" (L, B), … — The words of the law against idolatry (Leviticus 20:3-6, m1); the verb form of 'make him a sign' is unusual (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 14:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · pittah "惑わす / 惑わされる" (L, B), "だます / だまされる" (R), the same verb for the prophet and the LORD. — The LORD gives a false prophet over to his own deception as judgment (m1, footnoted); the word means enticed, persuaded into what is false (m2). Keeping one verb lets the reader see the same act on both sides.
- [ ] [EZK 14:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · Names "ノア", "ダニエル", "ヨブ" (the NT and Daniel / Job forms); tsidqatam "正しさ" (L, B), "正しい生き方" (R, as the English R "the right way they lived"), with tsedaqah as … — Whether this is Daniel of the book of Daniel or Danel of ancient tradition is uncertain (m1, footnoted); the Hebrew name is written as Daniel's. Righteousness cannot be passed on (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 14:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/14/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · peletah "逃れた者たち" (L), "生き残る者たち" (B), "生き残る人々" (R), as Isaiah 4:2; darkam va'alilotam "道と行い" (L), "生き方と行い" (B, R); nicham "慰められる"; hinneh "見なさい" (L, as the … — The survivors will show by their conduct why the judgment came, and so comfort the exiles (m1, uncertain); ra'ah is the disaster (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 15:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/15/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · gefen "ぶどうの木" at every level (as 17:6 and Jeremiah 2:21); 'ets "木材" for the wood, kept apart from "木々" (trees). — The vine is a picture of Israel, here turned against it (m1); the branch among the forest trees is uncertain (m2), kept close at L.
- [ ] [EZK 15:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/15/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · hinneh "見なさい" (L), "いや" (B, R, as the English "No"); nachar "焦げる" (L, B), "黒焦げになる" (R). — A nation already partly destroyed (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 16:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · revavah "[一万倍にも]" (L only); ba'adi 'adayim "最も美しい飾りの[年ごろ]" (L), "美しさの盛り" (B), "美しい若い女性" (R); shadayim "乳房" (L, B), "胸" (R); 'erom ve'eryah "裸で、何もまとっていなかった". — Both phrases are uncertain (m1, m2, footnoted); breasts formed and hair grown mark womanhood (m3), named plainly at every level as the English (r-en-1), with the everyday "胸" at R.
- [ ] [EZK 16:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · damayikh "あなたの血" / "体についた血"; sukh shemen "油を塗った". — Blood still on her, which no one had washed away (m1, footnoted); scented oil, as a bride was prepared (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 16:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · riqmah "刺繍した布" (L), "刺繍を施した衣" (B), "ししゅうをした美しい服" (R); tachash "上等の革" (L, B), "最高の革" (R), as Exodus 25:5; shesh "亜麻布" (L), "上等の亜麻布" (B, R); meshi "高価な布" (L, B), … — The materials of the tabernacle and of royal clothing (m3); the identification of tachash and meshi is uncertain (m1, m2, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 16:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · zanah "淫行をする" (L), "淫行にふける" (B), "わたしを裏切る" (R, with the English R's simile "多くの恋人を持つ女のように"); taznut "淫行" (L, B); lo-yehi "それはその者のものとなった" (L), … — Jerusalem turned to other gods and nations, pictured as a wife's promiscuity (m1); the forms of Hosea and notes/EZK-EZK-1.md (6:9). The last words are uncertain (m2, footnoted). B and R say it as frankly as the English, without coarse words.
- [ ] [EZK 16:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · bamot telu'ot "色とりどりの高き所" (L, B), "丘の上の礼拝所を飾り立てた" (R); bamot as notes/EZK-EZK-1.md. — Shrines for other gods, decorated with cloth (m1); the closing words are uncertain (m2, footnoted) and follow the English.
- [ ] [EZK 16:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · tsalme zakhar "男の像" (L, B), "男の形をした偶像" (R); the idols as "それら", never 彼ら. — Idols in male form (m1, footnoted); gifts that belonged to the LORD turned into idols (m2). R follows the English R "worshiped them".
- [ ] [EZK 16:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · reach nichoach "なだめの香り" (L), "喜ばれる香りのささげ物" (B), R as the English R, as notes/EZK-EZK-1.md (6:13); vayyehi "そのとおりになった" (L), "実際に、そうなったのだ" (B), "そういうことをしたのだ" (R). — The language of sacrifice (m1); 'and so it was' means it really happened (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 16:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · gav "高台" (L), "壇" (B), "礼拝のための壇" (R); ramah "高い所" (L), "高い礼拝所" (B), "礼拝所" (R); rechov "広場". — Raised platforms for the worship of other gods (m1, footnoted); ramah is kept apart from bamot (高き所).
- [ ] [EZK 16:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v26) L/B/R · register · gidle basar "肉欲の盛んな" (L), "好色な" (B), "欲望に満ちた" (R); bene Mitsrayim "エジプトの子ら" (L), "エジプト人" (B, R). — The phrase means sexually potent (m2); the Literal "肉" is kept with "欲" so that it reads as Japanese, and B, R follow the English lustful / full of lust. The image is Judah's alliances with Egypt and its gods (m1).
- [ ] [EZK 16:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · benot Pelishtim "ペリシテ人の娘たち" (L), "ペリシテ人の町々" (B, R), as the English; zimmah "みだらな" (L, B), "恥ずべき" (R); choq "割り当て" (L), "定められた分け前" (B). — The daughters are the Philistine cities (m2); what was reduced is uncertain (m1); zimmah is shameful conduct, here sexual (m3).
- [ ] [EZK 16:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v36) L/B/R · word_choice · nechoshtekh "欲情" (L, B), "欲望" (R); 'ervatekh "裸" (L, B), "裸の体" (R); gillule to'avotayikh "忌み嫌うべき汚らわしい偶像" (L), "忌まわしい偶像" (B), "汚らわしい偶像" (R); the children "子ら" … — The word for 'lust' is uncertain (m1, footnoted); idols hateful to the LORD (m2). The English runs 16:36-37 into one sentence; B ends 16:36 with "それゆえ、" so that 16:37 can begin with the verdict, and R makes each charge its own sentence.
- [ ] [EZK 16:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v38) L/B/R · word_choice · mishpete no'afot veshofekhot dam "姦淫する女たちと血を流す女たちの裁き" (L), "姦淫と殺人を犯した女が裁かれるように" (B, R); qin'ah "ねたみ" (L, R), "ねたむ思い" (B); chemah "憤り" (L, B), "激しい怒り" (R). — The penalties of the Law (m1); qin'ah is the LORD's zeal for a covenant that is his alone (m2). R uses "ねたみ" here rather than EZK-1's "ねたむほどの愛" (5:13), which reads oddly beside a death sentence.
- [ ] [EZK 16:43](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v43) L/B/R · word_choice · "bring your way on your head" as 9:10 (L the perfect "返した"); zimmah "みだらなこと" (L), "みだらな行い" (B), "恥ずべきみだらな行い" (R); the closing question at L, B, a statement at … — The idiom as 9:10 (m1); the question expects 'yes' (m2, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 16:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v46) L/B/R · word_choice · "左" / "右" (L), "北" / "南" (B, R), as the English; benoteiha "その娘たち" (L, B), "その町々" (R); "サマリア" and "ソドム" named again rather than 彼女. — Left and right are north and south (m1, footnoted); the daughters are the towns around a city (m3). Older and younger is uncertain (m2), kept as 姉 / 妹 with the English.
- [ ] [EZK 16:47](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v47) L/B/R · word_choice · The sisters referred to as "姉妹たち" at every level, never 姉妹たち, since they are cities; kim'at qat "わずかなことであるかのように" (L), "それでも足りないかのように" (B), "それでは足りなかった" (R). — The phrase is uncertain (m1); Samaria and Sodom are cities, so the Japanese repeats 姉妹たち (or the names) rather than use 彼女 (notes/EZK-EZK-1.md); the same in 16:50-61.
- [ ] [EZK 16:50](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v50) L/B/R · word_choice · to'evah "忌み嫌うべきこと" (L), "忌まわしいこと" (B), "汚らわしいこと" (R), unnamed as in the Hebrew; vasir "取り除いた" (L, B), "滅ぼした" (R). — The abomination is not named here (m1, footnoted); the LORD's judgment on Sodom (m2, footnoted). B, R name Sodom and its towns and then use 彼ら for their people.
- [ ] [EZK 16:52](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v52) L/B/R · word_choice · se'i kelimmatekh "あなたの恥辱を負え" (L), "自分の恥を負わなければならない / 負いなさい" (B, R); pillalt "取りなした" (L), "弁護した" (B), "言い分を立ててやった" (R); the oracle commands "負え" / "恥じよ" (L), … — By her own worse sins she has, without meaning to, argued their case (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 16:53](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v53) L/B/R · word_choice · shav shevut "繁栄を元どおりにする" (L), "繁栄を回復する" (B), "再び幸せにする" (R), as Amos 9:14 and Jeremiah 29:14; the sisters "姉妹たち". — A promise even for Sodom and Samaria (m1, footnoted). The two cities are referred to as 姉妹たち, never 姉妹たち; a reviewer may prefer the names again.
- [ ] [EZK 16:54](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v54) L/B/R · word_choice · "姉妹たちの慰めとなる" (L, B), R as the English R "make your sisters feel better about themselves"; kelimmah "恥辱" (L), "恥" (B, R), as 16:52. — Her shame consoles her sisters (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 16:56](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v56) L/B/R · word_choice · shemu'ah "語りぐさ" (L), "もの笑いの種" (B), "名を口にしては、ばかにしていた" (R); ge'onayikh "高ぶり". — In her pride Jerusalem spoke of Sodom with scorn (m1, uncertain). The English L, B run the question on into 16:57; Japanese closes the question here and 16:57 begins with "それは" so each verse keeps its own words.
- [ ] [EZK 16:57](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v57) L/B/R · word_choice · benot 'Aram / benot Pelishtim "アラムの娘たち" / "ペリシテ人の娘たち" (L, B), "…の町々" (R), as 16:27; cherpah "そしり" (L, B), as 5:15; sha'at "さげすむ". — Aram follows the Hebrew text (m1, footnoted; some read Edom); the time of reproach means she is now the one scorned (m2, uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 16:61](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/16/#v61) L/B/R · word_choice · lo' mibberitekh "あなたの契約によるのではない" (L), "あなたの契約に基づくのではない" (B), "あなたが結んだ契約のためではない" (R). — Not because of anything in her covenant (m1, footnoted, uncertain); Samaria and Sodom joined to Jerusalem as daughters (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 21:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · shevet beni mo'eset kol 'ets "わたしの子の杖は、すべての木を侮る" (L, B), "…見下す" (R), kept as the words stand at every level (r-en-1); the same "侮る杖" in 21:18. — m1 (uncertain, footnoted): the sense is uncertain, so the Japanese keeps the Hebrew words.
- [ ] [EZK 21:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v18) L/B/R · word_order · ne'um stands on its own line as the English sets it: "――…の言葉――。" (L), "と…は告げる。" (B), "と…は言う。" (R), as JER 2:19. — Jeremiah convention for a ne'um line; m1 (uncertain, footnoted) for the verse.
- [ ] [EZK 21:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · tikkafel cherev shelishitah "二倍に、三倍になれ" (L), "二度、三度と打ちかかるように" (B), "何度も何度も" (R); chalal gadol "大いなる刺し殺された者" (L), "大殺戮" (B, R). — m2; m3 (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 21:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ivchat cherev "剣の切っ先" (L, B); me'uttah "握られている" (L), "抜かれている" (B), as the English. — m1, m2 (uncertain words; m1 footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 21:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · shevu'e shevu'ot "誓われた誓い" (L), "厳かな誓い" (B, R); mazkir 'avon "咎を思い起こさせる" (L), "罪を思い起こさせる" (B), "罪を思い出す" (R), the play with 21:29 ("思い起こす") kept at L and B. — m1 (uncertain, footnoted), m2.

- [ ] [EZK 21:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/21/#v34) L/B/R · register · "you" (fem. sg, the sword) "おまえ" (L, B); R follows the English R in the third person; chazon shav' "空しい幻" (L), "偽りの幻" (B); qesem kazav "偽りを占う" (L), "うその占い" … — m1 (uncertain, footnoted); EZK-2 forms for false visions.
