The phrase is obscure (m4, footnoted): Literal keeps the seal image, Balanced and Readable give the sense the English gives.
- [ ] [EZK 28:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/28/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · The nine stones in the Exodus 28:17-20 Japanese: odem "赤めのう", pitdah "トパーズ", yahalom "エメラルド", tarshish "かんらん石" (also EZK 1:16), shoham "しまめのう" (GEN 2:12), … — The stones are nine of the twelve on the high priest's breastpiece (m2, uncertain): the same Japanese names let a reader hear the link. The jeweler's words are uncertain (m3, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 28:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/28/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · keruv "ケルブ" (every level), as EZK 10 and the ケルビム of GEN 3:24; hassokekh "覆い守る" (L, the covering wings of EXO 25:20 and the guarding of GEN 3:24), "守護の" (B), … — A winged being who guards God's presence (m1); the reading "you were a cherub" and the sense of mimshach are uncertain (m2, m3, footnoted). The same words return in 28:16.
- [ ] [EZK 28:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/28/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · har qodesh 'elohim "神の聖なる山"; 'avne 'esh "火の石" (L, B), "火のように輝く石" (R), also 28:16. — The holy mountain and the fiery stones are images of God's dwelling (m4, uncertain); Readable says how they look, as the English R "fiery stones".
- [ ] [EZK 28:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/28/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · 'avonekha "咎" (L), "罪" (B, R), as the Ezekiel convention; miqdashekha "聖所" (L, B), "聖なる場所" (R). — Tyre's temples or the holy place of the poem (m1, uncertain): the general word leaves both readings open.
- [ ] [EZK 29:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/29/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · lo' te'asef velo' tiqqavets "集められず、拾い上げられない" (L), "葬るために集められることも、拾い上げられることもない" (B), "だれもおまえの死体を拾い上げず、葬りもしない" (R). — Left unburied (m1); the burial sense is the likely one (m2, uncertain), made plain at B and R as the English.
- [ ] [EZK 29:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/29/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · he'emadta lahem kol-motnayim "彼らのすべての腰を[こわばって]立たせた" (L), "彼らの腰をことごとくくじいた" (B), "彼らは腰を痛めた" (R). — The Hebrew is uncertain (m2, footnoted); Literal keeps the verb "made stand", B and R the sense of an injured back, as the English.
- [ ] [EZK 29:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/29/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · atsmiach qeren "角を生えさせる" (L, B), "再び強くする" (R); pitchon peh "口を開くこと" (L), "あなたの口を開く" (B), "自由に語れるようにする" (R); Ezekiel is "あなた". — New strength for Israel (m1, uncertain) and Ezekiel free to speak again (m2; 3:26-27, 24:27, 33:22); Readable gives the sense, as the English R.
- [ ] [EZK 30:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · chalchalah "もだえ苦しみ" (L, B), "恐れて震える" (R); hamonah "その豊かさ" (L), "その富" (B, R). — hamon may mean wealth or crowds (m2, uncertain): Literal keeps a word open to both, B and R follow the English "wealth".
- [ ] [EZK 30:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · Cush "クシュ", Put "プト" (GEN 10:6), Lud "ルド" (ISA 66:19), Kub "クブ" (new); ha'erev "混じり合った民" (L), "異国の民" (B), "外国人" (R). — Egypt's allies and hired soldiers (m1, m2, uncertain); Kub is unknown (m3, footnoted), so the Hebrew name is kept.
- [ ] [EZK 30:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · Sin "シン" (L, B), "ペルシウム" (R, as the English R); ma'oz "砦" (L), "要塞" (B, R); chemah "憤り" (L, B), "激しい怒り" (R), as EZK 5:13. — Pelusium on the northeastern border (m1, uncertain); the place name follows the English at each level.
- [ ] [EZK 30:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · chul tachul "激しくもだえ苦しみ" (L), "ひどくもだえ苦しみ" (B) for the doubled verb; tsare yomam "昼に敵が[臨む]" (L), "真昼に敵に襲われる" (B). — m2: the doubled verb as intensity; the Memphis clause is uncertain (m1, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 30:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · Aven "アベン" (L, B), "オン" (R, as the English R); Pi-beseth "ピ・ベセト" (every level); hennah "それら[の町々]" (L), "その町々そのもの" (B), "その町々の人々" (R), never 彼女たち. — On (Heliopolis) and Bubastis (m1, footnoted); the feminine "they" is the cities or their women (m2, uncertain), read as the cities with the English.
- [ ] [EZK 30:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/30/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · Tahpanhes "タフパンヘス" (JER 2:16, 43:7); motot "くびきの横木" (L, B; JER 28:10), "エジプトがほかの国々を押さえつけていた力" (R); benoteha "その娘たち" (L), "その娘の町々" (B), "その村々の人々" (R). — The yoke bars are the power by which Egypt held others (m3, uncertain); the daughters are the dependent towns, as the English B, R.
- [ ] [EZK 31:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'Ashshur "アッシリア"; 'erez "杉", Levanon "レバノン"; tsammeret "梢" (L, B), "てっぺん" (R), as EZK 17:3; 'avotim "雲" (every level). — The great king and empire pictured as a cedar (m2); the name and "among the clouds" are uncertain readings (m1 footnoted, m3), kept as the English has them.
- [ ] [EZK 31:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v5) L/B · word_order · Japanese keeps the verb in the first line ("高くそびえた") and gives the comparison "野のすべての木にまさって" its own second line, so each line holds its Hebrew colon. — The English splits "grew taller / than all the trees of the field"; Japanese puts the comparison before the verb, so the second line is phrased to stand after it. m1: "as it sent them out" (uncertain).
- [ ] [EZK 31:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · gan 'elohim "神の園" (as 28:13); beroshim "糸杉" (the OT form, ISA 14:8, though the English says junipers); 'armonim "プラタナス" (GEN 30:37). — The garden of God is Eden (m1); the trees keep the names other books give them (m2 uncertain for the plane tree).
- [ ] [EZK 31:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · gavahta "おまえが丈高くなり" (L) keeps the Hebrew's "you", then "それが" for the tree; B and R read the tree throughout, as the English B, R. — A change of person in the Hebrew (m1, uncertain); Literal shows it, the other levels smooth it as the English does.
- [ ] [EZK 31:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · She'ol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R) even where the English R says "the grave", as the OT notes (ISA 14:9); also 31:16-17, 32:21, 32:27. — Sheol is the world of the dead (m1), not a single tomb; the Japanese keeps the place apart from qever 墓 (32:22-26). The syntax of "I caused mourning; I covered the deep" is uncertain (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 31:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/31/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · challe cherev "剣で殺された者たち" (L, B), "戦いで殺された者たち" (R), through 32:20-32; zero'o "それの腕であった者たち" (L), "それの力となっていた者たち" (B), "それの味方" (R). — Those who died violent deaths (m2); "its arm" is its strength or its allies (m1, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 32:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · qinah "哀歌" (L, B), "葬式の歌" (R), as 28:12; kefir "若獅子" (L), "獅子" (B), "若いライオン" (R); tannim "竜" (L, B), "怪物" (R), the monster of 29:3. — A lament over Pharaoh (m2), addressed おまえ as the king of Tyre in 28:12; the lion image is uncertain (m3, footnoted).
- [ ] [EZK 32:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · ramutekha "おまえの高さ" (L), "おまえのしかばね" (B), "おまえの死体" (R). — Probably the huge heap of the carcass (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Literal keeps the Hebrew word.
- [ ] [EZK 32:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v9) L/B · word_order · The first two lines keep the Hebrew cola by inverting the Japanese order ("わたしは悩ませる、 / 多くの民の心を。"). — The English splits "I will trouble / the heart of many peoples"; Japanese can keep the split only with the object after the verb, a poetic order. "your destruction" (m1, uncertain) is "破滅".
- [ ] [EZK 32:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · ashqia' "澄ませる" (L), "静める" (B), "きれいにする" (R); kashshemen "油のように" (every level). — The stillness of a land without life (m1, uncertain); the image is kept, not explained.
- [ ] [EZK 32:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v17) B/R · supplied_words · "ある月の十五日" (B, R) where the Hebrew names no month; Literal "その月の十五日". — The month is not named (m1, uncertain, footnoted); as EZK 26:1 (EZK-4), Japanese does not say "the month" as if one had been named.
- [ ] [EZK 32:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · nehe "嘆き悲しむ" (every level); horidehu "下らせなさい" (L), "送り下しなさい" (B), "送りなさい" (R); 'erets tachtiyyot and yorde vor as 26:20, 31:14. — The prophet's word does what it says (m2); the daughters of the nations are the nations or their women (m3, uncertain), kept as 娘たち at L and B.
- [ ] [EZK 32:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · cherev nittanah "剣は渡された" (L), "剣は備えられている" (B), "剣は…待ち構えている" (R); "her" (Egypt) "それ" (L), "エジプト" (B, R), never 彼女. — A hard line (m1, uncertain); Egypt as a feminine noun has no gendered Japanese match.
- [ ] [EZK 32:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ele gibborim "勇士たちの長たち" (L), "最も力ある勇士たち" (B), "いちばん強い指導者たち" (R); She'ol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R) as 31:15; 'ozrav "彼の助け手たち" (L), "同盟者たち" (B), "味方たち" (R); the … — The great warriors and rulers of the past (m2) greet the newcomer (m1); "with his helpers" is uncertain (m3).
- [ ] [EZK 32:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · gibborim noflim me'arelim "割礼のない者たちのうちの倒れた勇士たち" (L, B), "…倒れた英雄たち" (R); 'avonotam 'al-'atsmotam "その咎はその骨の上にあった" (L), "その罪は骨の上にとどまっている" (B), "その罪は骨に残ったままだった" … — An honoured warrior's burial (m2); "of the uncircumcised" and "iniquities on their bones" are uncertain texts (m1, m3, footnoted), and the verse may be a question (m4): the Japanese keeps the English statement.
- [ ] [EZK 32:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · nicham "慰められる" (every level), as 31:16. — The grim comfort of a shared fate (m1, uncertain); the word is the one used of the trees of Eden in 31:16.
- [ ] [EZK 32:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/32/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · chittiti "わたしの恐怖" (L), "わたしへの恐れ" (B), "人々がわたしを恐れるようにした" (R). — The text "I put my terror" is uncertain (m1, footnoted); read with the English as the terror of the LORD.
- [ ] [EZK 33:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/33/#v21) L/B/R · register · The news of Jerusalem's fall in the narrative frame: plain at L and B, です・ます at R (notes EZK-1); the fugitive's cry plain at every level; galutenu "わたしたちの捕囚" … — The messenger promised in 24:26 (m3); the date is uncertain (m1, m2, footnoted) and kept as the Hebrew gives it.
- [ ] [EZK 33:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/33/#v30) L/B/R · register · The exiles speak of the LORD plainly, without respect forms ("主から出る言葉", "主からの新しいお告げ"), as 8:12; "talk about you" "あなたのことを語り合う" / "話している", leaving open whether … — m1: perhaps against him (uncertain); the places where neighbours met (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 33:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/33/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · 'agavim "愛[の歌]" (L), "愛を語る" (B), "わたしをどんなに愛しているかを語る" (R, as the English R); betsa' "利得" (L), "もうけ" (B), "お金をもうけること" (R). — They speak warmly but do not mean it (m1, uncertain, footnoted); their gain is unjust profit (m2).
- [ ] [EZK 38:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/38/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · nesi' rosh "大君主" (L, B), "最高の指導者" (R), as the English "chief prince" — nasi' is Ezekiel's "君主" (L, B) / "指導者" (R) (EZK-1); rosh is read as "chief" (m1, footnoted, where the reading "prince of Rosh" is given), so a single title is formed rather than a place name "ロシュ".
- [ ] [EZK 41:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/41/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · heikhal (the main room of the temple building) "広間" (L), "大広間" (B, R), as 1 Kings 6:3-5, 6:17; 'ohel "天幕" (L, B), "テント" (R). — The 1 Kings 6 words for the same rooms; the inner room is "奥の間" (L), "奥の部屋" (B, R) and the most holy place "至聖所" / "いちばん聖なる所". "the width of the tent" is a puzzling phrase (m2, footnoted) and is kept as it stands; R writes ohel as "テント" (GEN-consistency).
- [ ] [EZK 41:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/41/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · gavah "高い台" (every level); 'atsilah "継ぎ目" (L, B), left unsaid at R as the English R. — A raised platform on which the temple stood (m1); 'atsilah is a hard word (m2, uncertain), so the plain "継ぎ目" that the English "joint" gives.
- [ ] [EZK 41:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/41/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · 'attiqim "回廊" (every level, also 41:16, 42:3, 42:5). — A hard word, perhaps terraces, balconies or passages (m1, uncertain, footnoted); "回廊" is a neutral word for a gallery round a building.
- [ ] [EZK 41:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/41/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · mezuzah "戸口の柱" (L), "戸口の枠" (B, R), as 1 Kings 6:31-33; haqqodesh "聖所" (L), "至聖所" (B), "いちばん聖なる所" (R). — A terse and difficult verse (m1, uncertain); L keeps the Hebrew "the holy" and the appearance formula ("有様", 1:5), B, R name the most holy place as the English B, R.
- [ ] [EZK 42:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/42/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'attiq 'el-pene 'attiq bashelishim "回廊が回廊に向かい合って三階に" (L, B), "三階にわたって、向かい合う回廊" (R); "空き地" for the twenty-cubit space, as 41:10. — A hard phrase (m2, uncertain, footnoted); the gallery word of 41:15-16 is kept.
- [ ] [EZK 42:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/42/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · mahalakh "通路" (every level, also 42:11-12); derekh "道". — Plain words; the measure "a way of one cubit" is uncertain (m1, footnoted: the Septuagint has a hundred cubits long) and is kept as the English has it.
- [ ] [EZK 43:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/43/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · The altar: cheq "溝"; gevul "縁取り"; zeret "一ゼレト" (L, as Exodus 28:16), B, R "{{units:約27センチ|半キュビト}}" as the English; gav "台座" (every level); 'azarah "張り出し" (L, … — The tabernacle altar words where Exodus has them; the rare words (gutter, base, hearth) are uncertain (m2, m4; 43:15 m1) and get plain descriptive words.
- [ ] [EZK 46:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/46/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · qeturot "囲まれた" (every level) — A rare word (m1, footnoted); the English's "enclosed" is followed.
- [ ] [EZK 46:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/46/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · tur "[石の]列" (L), "石の段" (B, R) — A course or ledge of stone (m1); B and R follow the English "ledge".
- [ ] [EZK 47:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/47/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · mefakkim "ちょろちょろと流れ出ていた" (every level) — A rare word, a small stream (m2); a thin trickle at the start makes the river's growth in 47:3-5 the more striking.
- [ ] [EZK 47:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/47/#v9) L · word_choice · nachalayim "川" (singular) — The dual is probably to be read "the river" (m1, footnoted), as the English L.
- [ ] [EZK 47:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/47/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · Hethlon "ヘトロン"; Lebo "レボ"; Zedad "ツェダド" (NUM 34) — NUM 34 forms where the names recur; the verse runs on into 47:16 as the English does.
- [ ] [EZK 47:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/47/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · Berothah "ベロタ", Sibraim "シブライム", Hazer-hattikon "ハツェル・ハティコン", Hauran "ハウラン"; Hamath "ハマト", Damascus "ダマスコ" — New names transcribed from the Hebrew; places not certainly known (m1).
- [ ] [EZK 47:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/47/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · Tamar "タマル"; "メリボト・カデシュ" here (plural spelling), "メリバト・カデシュ" in 48:28; mei "水" (L, B), "泉" (R); nachalah "[エジプトの]川" (L), "エジプトの涸れ川" (B), "エジプト川" (R) — The Hebrew spells Meribot here and Meribath in 48:28 (the English Meribath-kadesh both times); each spelling is kept. The wadi as NUM 34 / JOS 15.
- [ ] [EZK 48:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/48/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · terumiyyah "特別な献納地" (L), "特別な取り分" (B), "祭司たち自身の分" (R) — A rare form of terumah (m1); L marks it off from the plain 献納地 by 特別な, B and R say it is the priests' own share.
- [ ] [EZK 48:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/48/#v22) L · supplied_words · L supplies "[ので、これらを除いて]" — The "from" of the Hebrew excludes the Levites' and the city's holdings (m1); the English L supplies [apart].
- [ ] [EZK 48:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/ezk/48/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · "メリバト・カデシュ" (the Hebrew spelling here), against "メリボト・カデシュ" in 47:19; the rest of the border in the words of 47:19 — See 47:19.

## ダニエル書 (41)

- [ ] [DAN 1:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/1/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · chartummim "魔術師" (every level, as GEN 41 and EXO 7); 'ashshafim "祈祷師" (L, B), "呪文を唱える人" (R); for 2:2 mekhashshefim "呪術師" (L, B), "まじない師" (R) as EXO 7:11 — The court experts are listed together in 2:2, 2:10, 2:27, 4:4, 5:7, 5:11; each Hebrew/Aramaic word keeps one Japanese word so the lists stay distinct.
- [ ] [DAN 3:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/3/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · The officials, one word each through the chapter (3:2, 3:3, 3:27, and 6:2-8): achashdarpenaya "太守" (L, B), "地方長官" (R), as Ezra; signaya "長官"; pachavata "総督"; … — The list is repeated word for word in the Aramaic; Japanese keeps it fixed, with plainer words at Readable as the English Readable has.
- [ ] [DAN 3:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/3/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · The instruments, the same each time (3:5, 3:7, 3:10, 3:15): qarna "角笛"; mashroqita "笛"; qitros "竪琴" (L, B), "たて琴" (R); sabbekha "三角琴"; pesanterin "琴"; … — The identifications are uncertain (meaning decision); the Japanese follows the English choices, with kinnor-type lyres as 1SA (竪琴 / たて琴) and a modern word for the bagpipe the reader can picture.
- [ ] [DAN 6:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/6/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · dachavan "慰みの物" (L), "楽しみごと" (B, R) — The word is not certain (m1); a general word for entertainment, as the English, without choosing music, food or women.
- [ ] [DAN 7:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "片側に身を起こして" (L), "片側を持ち上げて" (B, R) — The sense is not certain (m1); the wording keeps the picture without choosing between rearing up and one side higher.
- [ ] [DAN 7:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · 'attiq yomin "日を重ねた方" (L, B), "遠い昔からおられる方" (R), with the light respect forms for God ("座に着かれた"); 7:13, 7:22 the same — God pictured as an aged judge (m1). The traditional "日の老いたる者" is literary (文語) and "年を経た方" is one Bible's wording; "日を重ねた方" keeps the "days" of the Aramaic in modern Japanese. Readable says what the title means, as the English "the Ancient One".
- [ ] [DAN 7:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · kevar 'enash "人の子のような者" (L, B), "人間のような姿をした者" (R); "その者" with plain verbs, no respect forms and no "彼" — A figure in human form (m1). "のような" keeps it from reading as the Gospels' title "人の子" alone, as Revelation 1:13 does; the meaning decision leaves the figure's identity open (m2), so the narration gives it neither the respect forms used for God nor a pronoun, matching the English lowercase.
- [ ] [DAN 7:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · qaddishei 'elyonin "いと高き方の聖なる者たち" (L, B), "神の聖なる民" (R); qaddishin "聖なる者たち" (L, B) through chapter 7 — "いと高き方" as the DAN-1 notes; the holy ones may be God's people, heavenly beings or both (m1): "者たち" leaves that open at Literal and Balanced, and Readable follows the English "God's holy people".
- [ ] [DAN 7:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "…のために裁きが行われ" (L), "…のために裁きを行われる" (B), "…の味方となって裁きをなさいました" (R) — The verdict went in the holy ones' favor (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the passive, Balanced and Readable name the Ancient of Days as the judge, as the English does.
- [ ] [DAN 7:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · 'iddan ve'iddanin ufelag 'iddan "ひと時と、ふた時と、半時" (L, B), "三つの期間と、さらに半分の期間" (R); 12:7 the same — Revelation 12:14 has "ひと時と、ふた時と、半時" (L, B) for this phrase; the Aramaic plural is read as two times. Readable follows the English "three and a half periods" and does not say years, which m3 leaves uncertain. Readable's 期間 is the word it uses for the seven "times" of 4:13 (七つの期間).
- [ ] [DAN 7:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/7/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · 'am qaddishei 'elyonin "いと高き方の聖なる者たちの民" (L, B), "いと高き神のものである民、神の聖なる民" (R); the singular pronoun "その" / "それ" (L), "彼ら" (B, R) — Literal keeps the open reading of whose kingdom it is (m2, m3); Balanced and Readable follow the English "their / them".
- [ ] [DAN 8:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/8/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · tseva hashshamayim "天の万軍" (L, B), "天の軍勢である星" (R) — As Kings ("天の万軍" L, B); the host may stand for heavenly beings and for God's people (m1, uncertain), so the wording keeps the picture.
- [ ] [DAN 8:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/8/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · sar hatstsava "万軍の長" (L, B), "天の軍勢の長" (R), as God: "その方", no "彼" — The Prince of the host is God himself (m1, uncertain), as the English shows with a capital and the deity pronoun; Japanese marks it with "その方".
- [ ] [DAN 8:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/8/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "万軍が…渡される" (L, B), "神の民と毎日のいけにえは…渡されます" (R); pesha' "背き" (L), "背きの罪" (B), "罪" (R); 'emet "真理" — A hard sentence (m1, uncertain); Literal and Balanced keep its words, Readable follows the English reading. Truth is the true teaching and worship of God (m2).
- [ ] [DAN 8:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/8/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · 'az panim "顔の強い" (L), "厳しい顔つきで" (B), "残酷で" (R); mevin chidot "なぞを解く" (L), "策略にたけた" (B), "人をだますのがうまい" (R) — Harsh and shameless (m2), skilled in double-dealing (m3): Literal keeps the Hebrew pictures, the other levels say what they mean.
- [ ] [DAN 9:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · ha'ish Gavri'el "あの人、ガブリエル" (L), "あの人" (B), "人のような姿をした者" (R); mu'af bi'af "速く飛んで" — The man of 8:15-16 (m3). "In swift flight" follows the English reading; the alternative "when I was utterly weary" (m1) is for a footnote.
- [ ] [DAN 9:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · shavu'im shiv'im "七十の七" (L, B), "七を一組として七十組の期間" (R); 9:25-27 the same — The English "seventy sevens" / "seventy sets of seven": the word means a unit of seven, and the readings of 9:24-27 are left open (m1), so Japanese does not say weeks or years.
- [ ] [DAN 9:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "幻と預言者を封じ" (L), "幻と預言に封印をし" (B), "幻と預言を実現させ" (R); qodesh qodashim "至聖なるもの[の所]" (L), "最も聖なる所" (B, R) — Sealing confirms the prophecy by fulfilling it (m3); a holy place or a holy person is anointed (m4, uncertain), and the Japanese follows the English "most holy place".
- [ ] [DAN 9:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · mashiach nagid "油注がれた者、指導者" (L), "油注がれた指導者" (B), "選ばれた指導者" (R); no christos span and no respect forms — An anointed leader (m3), as the English, which has no christos setting and no deity pronoun here; nagid is "指導者" as in the OT books so far. The division after the seven sevens follows the Hebrew accents, as the English (m1).
- [ ] [DAN 9:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v26) L/B/R · word_choice · yikkaret mashiach ve'ein lo "油注がれた者は断たれ、彼には何もない" (L), "…何も残らない" (B), "油を注がれた人が殺され、何も持たなくなる" (R) — Cut off: put to death or removed (m1, uncertain); "and will have nothing" is short and hard (m2): Literal keeps it bare.
- [ ] [DAN 9:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "憎むべきものの翼の上に、荒廃をもたらす者が[来る]" (L); "破滅をもたらす憎むべきもの" (B); "滅ぼす恐ろしいもの" (R); shomem "荒廃をもたらす者" (L), "破滅をもたらす者" (B), "彼" (R) — Balanced and Readable use the wording of Matthew 24:15 and Mark 13:14 for the phrase Jesus quotes from Daniel (m4), as the English "the abomination that brings ruin" / "the horrible thing that brings ruin" does; Literal keeps the Hebrew's order, with the one who makes desolate (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [DAN 9:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/9/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "堅い契約を結ぶ" (L, B), "固い約束を交わす" (R); "一つの七" (L, B), "七年" (R) as the English; the subject "彼" (L, B), "その支配者" (R) — The coming prince of 9:26 (m1, uncertain), named at Readable as the English "That ruler".
- [ ] [DAN 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/10/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "ペルシアの王キュロス"; "ベルテシャツァル" (R in parentheses, as DAN-1 2:26); tsava gadol "大きな戦い" — Names as chapters 1-6; the conflict may be war or hardship (m3, uncertain), and Japanese follows the English "great conflict / war".
- [ ] [DAN 10:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/10/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "亜麻布の衣を着た人" (L, B), "亜麻布の服を着た人" (R); ketem 'ufaz "ウファズの金" (L), "ウファズの純金" (B), "純金" (R); plain verbs — A heavenly being, not named (m1, uncertain); the English gives him no deity pronoun, so the narration uses no respect forms (12:6-7 the same).
- [ ] [DAN 10:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/10/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · tarshish "かんらん石" (L, B), "宝石" (R) — A gold-colored precious stone, not certain (m1); the English chrysolite, a precious stone at Readable.
- [ ] [DAN 10:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/10/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · sar (a heavenly power) "君" (L, B), "国を治める天使" / "天使の長" (R); chapters 10-12 the same — A heavenly power set over Persia (m1, uncertain); "君" keeps the Hebrew "prince" apart from human kings and from "長" for the Prince of the host (8:11); Readable says ruling angel, as the English.
- [ ] [DAN 10:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/10/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしが出て行くと" (L), "わたしがそれを終えると" (B, R) — When I go out: when done with Persia, or when going out to battle (m1, uncertain); Balanced and Readable follow the English.
- [ ] [DAN 11:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v1) R · supplied_words · "ミカエル" named (R) — "Him" is Michael, the prince just named (m1, uncertain); Readable names him, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [DAN 11:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · "彼女を生んだ者" (L), "父親" (B, R) — The Hebrew can also be read "her child" (m2, uncertain); the Japanese follows the English "the one who fathered her".
- [ ] [DAN 11:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · benei paritsei 'ammekha "あなたの民の中の乱暴な者たち" — Lawless men among the Jews (m1, uncertain); "乱暴な" covers violent and lawless without naming a party.
- [ ] [DAN 11:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · erets hatstsevi "麗しい地" (L, B), "美しい国イスラエル" (R); khalah beyado "その手には滅びがある" (L), "滅ぼす力を手にする" (B, R) — The beautiful land as 8:9; destruction in his hand is power to destroy, or completeness (m2, uncertain), and the Japanese follows the English.
- [ ] [DAN 11:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · noges "取り立てる者"; heder malkhut "王国の栄光[の地]" (L), "王国の栄華" (B), "王の富" (R); yamim 'achadim "数日のうちに" (L), "数年のうちに" (B), "まもなく" (R) — The glory of the kingdom may be its splendor or the glorious land (m2, uncertain); "a few days" is a short time (m3), and Balanced follows the English "within a few years".
- [ ] [DAN 11:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · negid berit "契約の君" (L), "契約の指導者" (B), "神と契約を結んだ民の指導者" (R) — Possibly the high priest (m2, uncertain); nagid is "指導者" (B, R) as 9:25, Literal "君" as the English prince.
- [ ] [DAN 11:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · tsiyyim kittim "キティムの船" (L), "キティムの船団" (B), "西の方の国々から船" (R) — Kittim, usually taken as the Roman fleet (m1, uncertain); Readable follows the English "lands of the west".
- [ ] [DAN 11:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "わずかな助け" / "少しの助け" — Usually taken as the Maccabean revolt (m1, uncertain); the Japanese leaves it unnamed, as the English.
- [ ] [DAN 11:37](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v37) L/B/R · word_choice · chemdat nashim "女たちの慕うもの" (L), "女たちが愛する神" (B, R) — Probably a god loved by women (m1, uncertain); Literal keeps the Hebrew open.
- [ ] [DAN 11:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v38) L/B/R · word_choice · eloah ma'uzzim "要塞の神" (L, B), "とりでの神" (R); kibbed "あがめる" (L, B), "拝む" / "うやまう" (R) — Perhaps a war god (m1, uncertain); a foreign god takes no respect forms.
- [ ] [DAN 11:39](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/11/#v39) L/B/R · word_choice · "異国の神と共に" (L), "異国の神の助けを得て" (B, R) — The Hebrew may also mean he will act for the defenders of the fortresses (m1, uncertain); Japanese follows the English reading.
- [ ] [DAN 12:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/12/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · yeshotetu rabbim vetirbeh hadda'at "多くの者が行き巡り、知識が増す" (L), "…あちこちと探し回り、知識は増していく" (B, R) — A hard saying (m2, uncertain); Balanced and Readable follow the English reading of a search for understanding.
- [ ] [DAN 12:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/12/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · mo'ed mo'adim vachetsi "ひと時と、ふた時と、半時" (L, B), "三つの期間と、さらに半分の期間" (R) — As 7:25 (m2, uncertain); Literal reports the oath without quotation marks, as the English Literal does.
- [ ] [DAN 12:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/dan/12/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "聖なる民の力を打ち砕くことが終わるとき" (L), "聖なる民の力が完全に打ち砕かれると" (B, R) — A hard phrase (m3, uncertain); Balanced and Readable follow the English.

## ホセア書 (20)

- [ ] [HOS 2:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/2/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · The wife who stands for Israel (m1) is "彼女" in the LORD's speech, and her children are addressed as "あなたがた" (L, B), "あなたたち" (R). Readable keeps the English … — In the figure of the marriage she is a woman, so "彼女" reads naturally; the rule against "彼女" for a personified city (Isaiah, Lamentations) concerns cities named as cities.
- [ ] [HOS 5:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/5/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · shofar "角笛", chatsotsrah "ラッパ" (m1); Gibeah "ギブア", Ramah "ラマ", Benjamin "ベニヤミン", as 1 Samuel; the alarm to the towns is a call to arms, so Balanced keeps the … — A war alarm in Japanese is short and sharp; the uncertain cry is footnoted (m3).
- [ ] [HOS 5:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/5/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · Assyria "アッシリア"; melekh yarev "the great king" "大王" at every level (m1, footnoted); rafa' "癒やす" (L, B), "いやす" (R), as Isaiah and the Psalms; "you" (m4) is … — The prophetic conventions; the uncertain title is left to the footnote.
- [ ] [HOS 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · The people's hesed toward the LORD (m2): "誠実" (L, B), as hesed between people in 2 Samuel and the Psalms, and "誠実な愛" (R) for the English Readable "faithful … — The people's loyalty is not God's steadfast love; at Readable a bare "誠実" would read as honesty only.
- [ ] [HOS 6:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/6/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · ke'adam "アダムのように" at every level, the first man (m1, uncertain and footnoted; a place called Adam is the other reading); 'avar berit "契約を破る"; bagad "裏切る", as … — As the English; the footnote carries the other reading.
- [ ] [HOS 7:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/7/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · padah "贖い出す" (L), "解き放つ" (B), "救い出す" (R), as Isaiah 1:27, again in 13:14; pasha' "背く" (L, B), "逆らう" (R). — The prophetic conventions for these words.
- [ ] [HOS 7:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/7/#v16) B/R · word_choice · lo' 'al "not upward" (m1, uncertain): "いと高き方には向かわない" (B, for the English Balanced "the One above"), "天におられる神のほうには向かない" (R); Literal keeps "上へではない". — The Psalms' "いと高き方" is the usual Japanese for God as the One on high.
- [ ] [HOS 8:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/8/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · shofar "ラッパ" (L, R) and "角笛" (B), following the English per level ("trumpet" L, R; "ram's horn" B), though 5:8 keeps shofar "角笛" at every level beside … — The English chose a different word per level here; Japanese follows it.
- [ ] [HOS 8:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/8/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · 'egel "子牛" (L, B), "子牛の像" (R), the golden calf of the north (m2), as 1 Kings 12 and Exodus 32; "he has rejected" (m1) is "[神は]…退けた" at Literal, inside the … — The prophetic conventions; the uncertain subject (m1) is footnoted.
- [ ] [HOS 8:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/8/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · The LORD speaks of himself in the third person in the middle of his own speech ("the LORD is not pleased"); as the voice shifts to the prophet's, the light … — Where the Hebrew names the LORD in the third person, the prophet's register is used, as Isaiah.
- [ ] [HOS 9:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/9/#v14) L/B/R · register · The prophet's prayer inside the oracle (m1): God addressed as "{{divine_name:主|ヤハウェ}}よ" in plain style, the request "〜てほしい" and never an imperative or … — The prophetic conventions (ISA.2.9).
- [ ] [HOS 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/10/#v1) L · word_choice · matstsevot "石の柱" at Literal here as well as Balanced and Readable (3:4 Literal has "柱"), where the English Literal says "sacred pillars". — The English Literal names the object more fully here.
- [ ] [HOS 11:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/11/#v7) B/R · word_choice · 'al "on high" (m3, uncertain) "いと高き方" (B), "いと高き神" (R), as 7:16 and the Psalms' Elyon; the LORD speaking of himself in the third person here takes "方" as a … — The uncertain wording is footnoted; the title matches 7:16.
- [ ] [HOS 11:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/11/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · Admah "アドマ" and Zeboiim "ツェボイム", the forms of Genesis 10 and 14 and Deuteronomy 29:22 (m1); nehpakh "覆る" (L), "激しく揺れ動く" (B), "ひっくり返る" (R), the verb of the … — One form per city; the verb of the heart echoes the cities' fate.
- [ ] [HOS 11:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/11/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · The prophet speaks of the LORD for a moment (m1), "その方"; the verb "ほえる" stays plain, since "ほえられる" reads as passive; 'aryeh "獅子" (L, B), "ライオン" (R); banim "子ら" … — The Psalms' rule against respect forms that misread.
- [ ] [HOS 12:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/12/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · qedoshim (m3, a plural used for God) "聖なる方" (every level), as "イスラエルの聖なる方"; ne'eman "真実な" ('emunah's word family, as 2:22). The verse numbering is the Hebrew … — The title of God in the third person as Isaiah.
- [ ] [HOS 13:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/13/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · massekhah "鋳物の像" (L, B), "銀を溶かして…造る" (R), as Deuteronomy; zovchei 'adam "いけにえをささげる人間たち" (L), following m2 (human beings who offer sacrifice), not "人をいけにえにする者". — The reading the meaning decision gives; the other is footnoted.
- [ ] [HOS 13:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/13/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "Give me a king and princes" (m2, Israel's demand in 1 Samuel 8): "与えよ" (L) keeps the bare imperative; Balanced and Readable make it a request to God, … — The quotation recalls a prose request in the narrative, where people speak politely.
- [ ] [HOS 13:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/13/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · She'ol "陰府" (L, B), "死者の世界" (R) even where the English Readable says "grave", as Isaiah and the Psalms; padah "贖い出す" (L), "解き放つ" (B), "救い出す" (R) and ga'al "贖う" … — The Hebrew sense, with the words Paul's Japanese uses wherever they agree.
- [ ] [HOS 14:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hos/14/#v3) L/B/R · register · The prayer the people are to say (14:3-4) is plain, God "あなた", the requests "〜てほしい", as Isaiah's convention for God addressed inside an oracle (and 9:14); one … — The prophetic conventions; the Psalter's "〜てください" is kept for the psalms themselves.

## ヨエル書 (9)

- [ ] [JOL 1:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/1/#v4) L/B · word_choice · The four locust words are kept apart by one fixed form each, used again in 2:25: gazam "かみつくいなご" (as Amos 4:9), 'arbeh "群がるいなご", yeleq "跳ねるいなご", chasil … — Each word is a kind of いなご with a describing verb, as the English L and B have; plain "いなご" or "ばった" would not show which of the four is meant, and the series must read as four distinct waves.
- [ ] [JOL 1:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/1/#v17) L/B · word_choice · "種は土くれの下でしなび" for the three rare words (m1), as the English L and B. — The line is uncertain; the reading in the English is followed.
- [ ] [JOL 2:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/2/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · hatstsefoni "北から来る者" (L), "北から来た軍勢" (B), "北から来た軍隊" (R); "東の海 / 西の海" (L, B), "東の死海 / 西の地中海" (R), as the English. — The northerner is uncertain (m1); L keeps the bare word. L 2:20 "大いなることをした" and 2:21 "大いなることをなさった" keep the Hebrew echo.
- [ ] [JOL 2:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/2/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · moreh / malqosh "先の雨と後の雨" (L), "秋の雨と春の雨" (B), "秋にも春にも" (R), as the Deuteronomy notes; litsdaqah "義にかなって" (L), "真実をもって" (B), "あなたたちに真実なので" (R), as the English. — The sense is uncertain (m2); the English reading is followed.
- [ ] [JOL 4:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/4/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · 'emeq Yehoshafat "ヨシャファトの谷" at every level (the king's name as 1 Kings 15:24 and Matthew 1:8), with no gloss in the text: the meaning "the LORD judges" (m1) is … — The English names the valley the same way at every level; the place is not known (m2).
- [ ] [JOL 4:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/4/#v11) L/B/R · register · The prophet's cry to the LORD inside the oracle is a plain request "〜てほしい", as 2:17 and the ISA convention. — No imperative to God and no です・ます inside a verse oracle.
- [ ] [JOL 4:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/4/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · 'emeq hecharuts "判決の谷" at every level (the LORD's verdict, m1). — The valley is where the LORD gives his verdict, not where people decide; "判決" says so.
- [ ] [JOL 4:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/4/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · niqqeti ... lo' niqqeti "罪なしとする…罪なしとしていなかった" (L), "報復をする" (B), "罰する" (R), as the English per level. — The line is uncertain (m1); L keeps the Hebrew's one verb twice.
- [ ] [JOL 4:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jol/4/#v21) L/B/R · register · The closing line takes the light respect form ("住まわれる / 住んでおられる"), read as the prophet's closing word about the LORD, as "主が語られた" (4:8). — The book ends with the LORD's presence (m2); the speaker shifts from "I" to the LORD.

## オバデヤ書 (4)

- [ ] [OBA 1:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/oba/1/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · mazor "わな" (uncertain, m2 footnote); tevunah "悟り" (L, B; also 8), "何も気づかない" (R). — As the English; the meaning decision marks mazor uncertain.
- [ ] [OBA 1:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/oba/1/#v12) L/B/R · register · The prohibitions (12-14): "〜するな" (L) as the Hebrew; "〜べきではなかった" (B), "〜てはいけなかった" (R), as the English B, R "You should not have" (m1). — Balanced and Readable follow the English reading of the commands as condemnation of what is past; Literal keeps the imperative form.
- [ ] [OBA 1:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/oba/1/#v16) L/B/R · supplied_words · The plural "you have drunk" has no subject in Japanese. — Japanese must choose おまえたち (Edom, scorned) or あなたがた (God's people) for a plural you, and that would settle who is meant, which m1 leaves open; Japanese can leave the subject unstated.
- [ ] [OBA 1:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/oba/1/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · Zarephath サレプタ (Luke 4:26, 1 Kings 17:9); Sepharad セファラド (new; place unknown, m3); galut "捕囚の民" (L, B), "連れて行かれた人々" (R); "{{gender:children|sons}} of Israel" … — Sepharad transcribed from the Hebrew; the other names as settled.

## ヨナ書 (4)

- [ ] [JON 2:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jon/2/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · hevle shav "偽りの空しいもの" (L), "空しい偶像" (B), "何の役にも立たない偶像" (R), as 1 Kings 16:13; chasdam "自分たちの誠実" (L), "自分たちの誠実な愛" (B), "変わらない愛" (R) — Japanese keeps the ambiguity the meaning decision records: "their" loyalty, or the loyal love that would be theirs from God; いつくしみ would decide for God's love.
- [ ] [JON 3:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jon/3/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · 'ir gedolah le'lohim "神にとって大いなる都" (L), "非常に大きな都" (B), "とても大きな町" (R); mahalakh shloshet yamim "歩いて三日の道のり" (L), "歩いて通り抜けるのに三日" (B, R) — As the English per level; both phrases are uncertain (meaning decisions m1, m2).

L keeps the OT key word "真実" (Lamentations 3:23, Hosea 2:22); B uses "誠実", as Isaiah 11:5 for a person's 'emunah, so the line is not read as "truth"; R says what it is. Romans 1:17, Galatians 3:11 and Hebrews 10:38 were checked: their Japanese …

- [ ] [HAB 3:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hab/3/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · tefillah "祈り"; 'al shigyonot "シグヨノトに合わせて" (L, B), "歌として歌う" (R), as the Psalter's titles (shiggayon シガヨン, music terms "Xに合わせて", R "歌"). — Chapter 3 is a psalm with a psalm title (m1); the Psalter conventions (PSA-PSA-1) govern its title, Selah and register. - [ ] [HAB 3:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/hab/3/#v3) L/B/R · word_order · Selah "セラ" at every level, moved with the English to the end of the verse, straight after the closing 。. — The Psalter convention (PSA-PSA-1); the meaning of the word is unknown (m3). The same in 3:9 and 3:13.

- [ ] [ZEC 1:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/zec/1/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · hammal'akh haddover bi "わたしと話していた使い" (L), "…天使" (B, R), the same at every occurrence in 1-6; 'adoni to the angel "わが主よ" (L, B), "天使さま" (R). — Bare mal'akh 使い (L) / 天使 (B, R) as Hosea 12:5 and Daniel 3:28; 'adoni to an angel as Daniel 10:16-19 (わが主よ); Readable avoids 主よ, which a new reader would take as an address to God.

- [ ] [ZEC 10:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/zec/10/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · pinnah "隅の石" (L, R), "隅の親石" (B), as Job 38:6; yated "杭" (L), "テントの杭" (B, R); qeshet milchamah "戦いの弓"; nogesh "支配者" (every level; at 9:8 the same word is … — m2 and the English levels; m3 (uncertain) reads nogesh here as every kind of leader, so the oppressor sense of 9:8 is not used.
