## マタイによる福音書 (24)

- [ ] [MAT 1:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/1/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "シェアルティエル", "ゼルバベル", "アビウド", "エリアキム", "アゾル", "ツァドク", "アキム", "エリウド", "エルアザル", "マタン" — Names known from the Old Testament (Shealtiel, Zerubbabel) and names that match Old Testament ones (Zadok, Eleazar) take their Old Testament forms, as in the English; the others are transliterated from the Greek in the same pattern.
- [ ] [MAT 1:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/1/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "おとめ" at every level — The word is the one for a woman who has never had sexual relations (m1). "おとめ" carries that sense and reads naturally in Scripture; "処女" is explicit but clinical. Verses 18, 20 and 25 make the sense plain.
- [ ] [MAT 3:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/3/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "ふるい分けの熊手" (L, B); "麦ともみ殻をより分ける……道具" (R) — The tool is a wooden fork used to toss threshed grain into the wind (m1). "箕" is a basket, and its kanji is hard to read; "ふるい分けの熊手" names a forked tool and its use.
- [ ] [MAT 4:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/4/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "神殿の突き出た端" (L); "神殿の境内の高い角" (B); "神殿の境内でいちばん高い所" (R) — The word means a little wing, a high projecting edge of the temple buildings, and the exact place is not known (m2). Literal keeps the picture; Balanced and Readable name a high corner. The temple here is the whole precinct, so Balanced and Readable say "神殿の境内", as the Gospels do in Japanese.
- [ ] [MAT 6:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/6/#v22) L/B · word_choice · "健やか" (L, B); "よい" (R) — The word is sound and undivided, with a hint of generosity (m1); "健やか" says a sound, healthy eye without narrowing it to clear sight, and stands over against the "悪い" eye of 6:23.
- [ ] [MAT 7:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/7/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · the wording of John the Baptist's warning (3:10), to be kept identical there — Jesus repeats John's words word for word (m1), and the levels repeat the wording they use at 3:10. 3:10 was drafted in parallel; if its Japanese differs, one of the two should be brought into line.
- [ ] [MAT 8:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/8/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · the fulfilment formula as in 1:22 and 4:14: "……を通して言われたことが実現するためであった" (L, B); "……本当になるためでした" (R), with "[預言者は]こう言っている" (L) and "預言者はこう言いました" (R) for "saying" — Matthew uses this formula through the book (1:22; 2:15, 17, 23; 4:14; 12:17), so each level keeps the one wording settled at 1:22.
- [ ] [MAT 8:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/8/#v22) R · supplied_words · "神から離れて死んでいる人たち" (R) — Readable gives the likely sense of the first "dead" (m1), as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [MAT 9:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/9/#v28) B/R · word_choice · "主よ" at every level — The men are strangers, but they have just called Jesus Son of David, and the meaning decision reads their Lord as the address of faith (m1). So Japanese keeps "主よ" at every level here.
- [ ] [MAT 10:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/10/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "[回り]終える" (L); "回り終える" (B, R) — To finish the towns may mean completing the mission there or running out of places to flee (m2). "回り終える" leaves both open; Literal brackets the supplied verb.
- [ ] [MAT 12:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/12/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "まむしの子孫たちよ" (L); "まむしの一族よ" (B); "毒へびの一族のようだ" (R) — Jesus uses John the Baptist's words of 3:7 (m1), which should read the same at each level; the forms here are to be matched with 3:7 and 23:33. Readable makes the picture a comparison, as the English does.
- [ ] [MAT 13:41](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/13/#v41) L/B/R · word_choice · "泣くことと歯ぎしり" (L); "泣きわめき、歯ぎしりをする" (B, R) — Weeping and gnashing of teeth mean anguish and rage (m4). Literal keeps the two nouns; Balanced and Readable make them verbs. The phrase should match 8:12 and its later uses.
- [ ] [MAT 15:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/15/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "イスラエルの家の失われた羊たち" (L); "失われた羊であるイスラエルの民" (B); "迷子になった羊" (R) — The words are those of 10:6 (m1), which should read the same at each level; the coordinator should check them against the Japanese of 10:6. Readable names God as the sender (m2), as the English does.
- [ ] [MAT 16:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/16/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "悪い、姦淫の時代" (L); "悪い、神に不忠実な時代" (B); "今の時代の人々は悪く、神に対して不誠実だ" (R), as in 12:39 — Adulterous is the prophets' word for unfaithfulness to God (m2); Literal keeps the word and Balanced and Readable give its sense. Jesus repeats his answer of 12:39 (m1, r-en-1), so each level repeats its Japanese there, without "the prophet", which only 12:39 has.
- [ ] [MAT 17:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/17/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · God's words as in 3:17 at each level, with "この者に聞きなさい" (L) and "この子の言うことを聞きなさい" (B, R) added as in Mark 9:7 — God speaks plainly, as Father, and the voice says "この者 / この子", not "彼", for Jesus, as in Mark 9:7. The words repeat 3:17 word for word (m2), so each level repeats its Japanese there; "behold" in narration is "なんと" at Literal, as through the book.
- [ ] [MAT 21:41](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/21/#v41) L/B/R · register · the leaders answer in the plain style; "悪人どもを悪人にふさわしく" (L) — The chief priests and elders (21:23) are rulers giving a verdict, not petitioners, so their answer is plain, not です・ます. Literal keeps the play on bad men and a bad end (m1) with "悪人……悪人にふさわしく"; Balanced and Readable say only that the end will be terrible.
- [ ] [MAT 22:42](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/22/#v42) L/B/R · register · the Pharisees answer in the plain style — Leaders questioned by Jesus answer plainly, as the chief priests and elders do in 21:41; those who come to him with a question addressed to the Teacher speak politely (22:16, 24, 36).
- [ ] [MAT 24:42](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/24/#v42) L/B/R · register · "主が……来る" (plain verb) — Jesus is speaking of himself, so the verb stays plain, as with the Son of Man in 24:44; respect forms are for the narrator and for others speaking of God or Jesus.
- [ ] [MAT 25:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/25/#v8) L/B/R · register · です・ます between the young women (25:8-9) — A request for help and its refusal read naturally in polite speech in Japanese, and polite speech avoids marked women's speech forms.
- [ ] [MAT 26:41](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/26/#v41) L/B/R · word_choice · "試み" (L); "試練" (B); "つらい試し" (R), as in 6:13 — The meaning decision (m1) reads the word as in the Lord's Prayer (6:13): a testing that could make them fall away. "誘惑" in Japanese means only enticement to sin, so Literal takes "試み", the word of 6:13 that holds both senses, and Balanced and Readable use the wider words of 6:13, as the English …
- [ ] [MAT 26:49](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/26/#v49) L/B/R · word_choice · "ラビ、ごきげんよう" (L, B); "先生、こんばんは" (R) — The word is an ordinary greeting here; Readable uses the everyday evening greeting and gives the title's meaning, as the English does.
- [ ] [MAT 27:50](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/27/#v50) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊を手放された" at every level; Readable adds "死なれました" — Matthew's verb is not Mark's "breathed his last" or John's "handed over": it means let go (meaning decision m1), and suggests that Jesus gave up his life himself. "手放す" keeps that picture; Readable says plainly that he died, as the English does.
- [ ] [MAT 27:51](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/27/#v51) L/B/R · word_order · "イエスの復活の後" between leaving the tombs and entering the city (L); with leaving the tombs (B); with entering the city (R) — The phrase may belong with either verb (meaning decision m2). Literal keeps the Greek order so both readings stay open; Balanced and Readable follow the English Balanced and Readable.
- [ ] [MAT 28:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mat/28/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "おはよう" at every level — The word is the everyday greeting (meaning decision m1), and the women meet Jesus at dawn (28:1), so Japanese uses its everyday morning greeting, spoken plainly by Jesus. The link to the word for joy is left to the meaning note.

## マルコによる福音書 (6)

- [ ] [MRK 5:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/5/#v6) L/B/R · register · the spirit speaks to Jesus in the polite style ("あるのですか", "苦しめないでください") — The words are hostile (meaning decision m2), but the spirit has fallen down before one stronger than itself and goes on to plead (5:7, 10, 12). The polite style suits that cornered pleading; the hostility is carried by the words themselves.
- [ ] [MRK 6:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/6/#v18) L/B/R · register · John speaks to the king plainly ("許されていない", "言っている") — John rebukes Herod as a prophet, not as a petitioner; the plain style carries a prophet's authority, as Jesus' speech does.
- [ ] [MRK 6:50](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/6/#v50) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしである" (L); "わたしだ" (B); "わたしだ。イエスだ" (R) — In ordinary speech the words mean "it is I", and they also echo the name God gave himself (meaning decision m1). Literal uses "わたしである", the form used for Jesus' words at his arrest in John 18:5-8, which can carry both; Balanced and Readable give the plain sense, Readable adding the name as the …
- [ ] [MRK 7:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/7/#v28) B/R · word_choice · "主よ" at every level — Strangers usually say "先生" to Jesus at Balanced and Readable, but here the meaning decision (m1) makes her address count: she is the only person in Mark who calls Jesus Lord, and she trusts his power over the demon. So "主よ" stays at every level, as the English keeps "Lord". She speaks politely …
- [ ] [MRK 9:49](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/9/#v49) L/B/R · word_choice · "塩をふられる" at every level — The meaning of the saying is uncertain (m2), so every level keeps the picture of salting, as the English does. 塩をふる suits the salting of an offering and does not choose between purifying and preserving.
- [ ] [MRK 13:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/mrk/13/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "荒廃の憎むべきもの" (L); "破滅をもたらす憎むべきもの" (B); "滅ぼす恐ろしいもの" (R); "立ってはならない所に立っている" with no pronoun — The phrase comes from Daniel (m1). Literal keeps the genitive; Balanced and Readable say what it does. The Greek participle is masculine and points to a person (m2), which Japanese cannot show without adding a pronoun; the wording leaves it open as a thing or a person standing in the holy place.

## ルカによる福音書 (14)

- [ ] [LUK 1:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/1/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · "喜びなさい" at every level — The word is the everyday Greek greeting, literally "rejoice", with an undertone of the prophets' call to rejoice (m1). Japanese has no greeting that means both; "喜びなさい" keeps the word and its undertone, and the narrative already says it is a greeting (1:29). "おめでとう" is the wording of familiar …
- [ ] [LUK 1:41](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/1/#v41) L/B/R · register · Elizabeth speaks to Mary in the plain style, warmly, with respect forms for what Mary does as the Lord's mother ("来てくださる", 1:43) — Elizabeth is the older kinswoman speaking with joy to a young relative, so the plain style is natural; but she honours Mary as the mother of her Lord (1:43), which Japanese shows in the verbs for Mary's coming. This holds through 1:45.
- [ ] [LUK 1:46](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/1/#v46) L/B/R · word_order · the song set in lines (\q) at every level: the words introducing it are a line of their own, and each verse a line, with a second line where a verse holds two … — Mary's song is Hebrew-style poetry in parallel lines (m2). The English renderings of Luke 1 carry no line markers yet, so the Japanese lines follow the song's parallel clauses by the rules of the English style guide's section on poetry: a break only between clauses, never inside a phrase, and never …
- [ ] [LUK 1:76](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/1/#v76) L/B/R · word_choice · "平和の道" — Peace here is the whole well-being God gives, peace with God (m7), so the key term takes "平和" rather than the inner "平安".
- [ ] [LUK 3:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/3/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · names known only from Luke transliterated from the Greek: エリ, マタト, メルキ, ヤンナイ, マタティア, エスリ, ナガイ, マアト, セメイン, ヨセク, ヨダ, ヨハナン, レサ, ネリ, アディ, コサム, エルマダム, エル, ヨリム, ヨナム, … — These names are not in the Old Testament, or not as these people, so they follow the Greek spelling by the style guide's pattern. Sala (サラ) and the two Cainans (カイナム) keep Luke's own forms (m6, m8), as the English does.
- [ ] [LUK 5:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/5/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "師よ" (L); "先生" (B, R) — Luke's word is not the usual word for teacher but one used of an overseer or commander, always for Jesus (m1). Literal marks the difference with "師よ"; Balanced and Readable use "先生", the natural way for a disciple to address Jesus. Simon speaks politely to Jesus.
- [ ] [LUK 8:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/8/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "師よ" (L); "先生" (B, R) for Luke's word for Master — As at 5:5: Luke's word is not the usual word for teacher but one used of an overseer (m1). Literal marks the difference with "師よ" every time (5:5; 8:24, 45; 9:33, 49; 17:13); Balanced and Readable use "先生", the natural Japanese address for someone with authority who leads and teaches.
- [ ] [LUK 9:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/9/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · "旅立ち" (L); "旅立ち、すなわちイエスの出エジプト" (B); "この世を去られる" (R) — The word means a going out, and in this setting death (meaning m1). "旅立ち" keeps the picture of a journey out; Balanced adds the name of Israel's going out of Egypt, "出エジプト", so that the link with Moses can be heard, as the English Balanced keeps "exodus". Readable says plainly that he would leave …
- [ ] [LUK 9:59](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/9/#v59) L/B/R · word_choice · "主よ" at every level, here and in 9:61 — The men on the road speak as would-be disciples who mean to follow Jesus, so they take the disciples' address rather than a stranger's.
- [ ] [LUK 11:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/11/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · the divided kingdom as in Matthew 12:25; "家は家の上に倒れる" (L), "家々は折り重なって倒れる" (B), "家々も、次々と倒れてしまう" (R) — The first half keeps the Japanese of Matthew 12:25. Luke's short "house falls upon house" can picture the ruined kingdom or a divided household (m1); like the English, Japanese keeps the picture of houses falling, which fits both readings at Literal.
- [ ] [LUK 16:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/16/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "力ずくで…入ろうとしている" (L); "力を尽くして…入ろうとしている" (B); "なんとかして…入ろうと押し寄せている" (R) — The verb means to use force, and it is taken of people pressing eagerly to enter (m3). Literal keeps the force of the word; Balanced and Readable say the eagerness.
- [ ] [LUK 18:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/18/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "あわれんでください" — Japanese has no everyday word for being turned from anger to mercy; "あわれんでください" is the plea a reader knows, and the meaning decision (m1) explains how this verb differs from the usual word for mercy.
- [ ] [LUK 20:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/20/#v34) L/B · word_choice · "あの世" (L); "来たるべき世" (B); "やがて来る世" (R) — "That age" is the age to come, after the resurrection (m4). Literal keeps "that" as "あの世", set against "この世"; Balanced and Readable say "the age to come" so that it is not read as the world of the dead.
- [ ] [LUK 22:51](https://openwordbible.org/ja/luk/22/#v51) L/B/R · word_choice · "そこまでに[させて]おきなさい" (L); "もうやめなさい" (B); "やめなさい！もうこれ以上してはいけない！" (R) — The short words most likely tell the disciples to stop (m1). Literal keeps "let [it be] as far as this" with the supplied verb in brackets; Balanced and Readable say the likely sense.

## ヨハネによる福音書 (12)

- [ ] [JHN 2:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/2/#v3) L/B/R · register · Jesus's mother speaks politely ("ありません", "してください") — She only states the need and leaves the answer to him (m2), and in verse 5 she tells the servants to do whatever he says. Gentle polite speech fits that deference; Jesus answers her in his own plain style.
- [ ] [JHN 2:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/2/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · "起こす" here, in 2:20, and "死者の中から起こされた" in 2:22 — The verb is used both of putting up a building and of raising the dead (m3). "建て直す" would keep only the first sense and "よみがえらせる" only the second; "起こす" can say raising up, and is the word for raising the dead in 2:22, so the double meaning stays.
- [ ] [JHN 4:26](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/4/#v26) L/B · word_order · "わたしが[それ]である" (L) / "わたしがそれだ" (B) first, then "the one speaking" — Literal and Balanced put the "I am" first, as the Greek does, so the plain sense "I am he" leads and the possible echo of God's words (m1) is not hidden; Literal brackets the "he" Japanese needs. Readable names the Messiah, as the English does.
- [ ] [JHN 5:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/5/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "ベテスダ" — The SBLGNT reads Bethesda (meaning decision m2), so the name is transliterated from that form. "ベトザタ", familiar from versions based on another reading, would name the variant, which the footnote gives.
- [ ] [JHN 6:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/6/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしである" (L); "わたしだ" (B, R) — The plain sense is "it is I", with a likely echo of the way God names himself (m1). Literal's "わたしである" adds no predicate, so it leaves the bare words in view; Balanced and Readable say the plain sense in everyday words. The absolute "I am" of 8:24, 28 and 58 is "わたしはある" in this book, so a reader …
- [ ] [JHN 8:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/8/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしは世の光である" (L, B); "わたしが、この世を照らす光だ" (R) — "I am" with a predicate takes the book's form "わたしは……である" at Literal and Balanced (m2). Readable puts the stress on Jesus with "が", says "この世" for the world, and keeps Jesus's own speech in the plain style, as through the book.
- [ ] [JHN 8:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/8/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしはある" with nothing supplied, at every level (also 8:28, 8:58) — The echo of God's name is uppermost (meaning decision m1), so the bare words stand as in the English; "わたしはある" is how Japanese readers know the divine name of Exodus 3:14. It is set off with commas, not brackets, so that no quotation is added inside Jesus' speech. The plain reading is the …
- [ ] [JHN 8:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/8/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "そもそも" (every level), with the sentence as a question — Following the SBLGNT punctuation (meaning decision m1), the opening words mean "at all"; "そもそも" says that, and its own sense of "from the start" keeps a trace of the other reading. Marked uncertain as the meaning decision is.
- [ ] [JHN 11:41](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/11/#v41) L/B/R · register · Jesus prays to the Father plainly and intimately ("父よ", "あなた", "感謝する", "ありがとう"); the Father's hearing as "聞いてくださった", his sending plain "遣わした" — A son speaks to his Father plainly, as in the prayers of 12:27-28 and John 17, which keep the same register; the polite style would make the prayer sound formal. The benefactive "くださる" for what the Father does for him keeps the warmth; Jesus's authority before the crowd is unchanged.
- [ ] [JHN 12:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/12/#v10) B/R · supplied_words · "祭司長たちから離れて" (B, R); "去って行き" (L) — As the English does, Balanced and Readable follow m1 and say whom the people were leaving; Literal keeps the open "going away". The choice is uncertain (m1 has a footnote).
- [ ] [JHN 12:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/12/#v21) L/B/R · register · "だんなさま" (L, B); "すみません" (R); "お目にかかりたい" (L, B), "お会いしたい" (R) — The address is a courtesy to Philip, not to Jesus (m2), so not 主よ; the Japanese brief gives だんなさま for "sir" to a stranger, and Readable uses the everyday polite opener. お目にかかる keeps "see" while saying, politely, that they want to meet him (m3).
- [ ] [JHN 21:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jhn/21/#v15) R · supplied_words · "この者たち以上に" (L), "この人たち以上に" (B); "ほかの弟子たちよりももっと" (R) — Literal and Balanced keep "these" with a word for people that still leaves open whether the comparison is with what the others love or with what Peter loves. Readable says what meaning decision m2 finds most likely, so this is marked uncertain.

## 使徒言行録 (16)

- [ ] [ACT 3:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/3/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "命の創始者" (L); "命の源である方" (B); "命を与える方" (R) — The word means one from whom something begins and who leads the way (m3). Japanese has no single word for both; Literal keeps the sense of origin, and Balanced and Readable say that life comes from him. "命の君" is the wording of an older version.
- [ ] [ACT 7:51](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/7/#v51) L/B/R · register · です・ます kept through the rebuke of 7:51-53 — Stephen's charge grows sharp, but he is still a man speaking to the council; keeping the polite style lets the words carry the force, without a change of register the Greek does not show.
- [ ] [ACT 8:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/8/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "大いなる者と呼ばれる神の力" (L, B); "人々が大いなる力と呼んでいる神の力" (R) — The title is unclear (m4). Literal and Balanced keep "Great" as a name for the power; Readable makes it plain that it is a name people used, as the English Readable does. "大能" is avoided as old church language.
- [ ] [ACT 9:33](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/9/#v33) L/B/R · word_choice · "アイネア" — The Greek name is transliterated by the same pattern as other Greek names in Acts (dropping the case ending, as in アナニア).
- [ ] [ACT 10:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/10/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "あなたさま" (L); address left out (B, R) — Cornelius addresses the angel, not God (m1). Japanese "主よ" would be read as a prayer to God, and "だんなさま" is the address of a servant to a master; Literal keeps the respectful address as "あなたさま", and Balanced and Readable let the polite "でしょうか" carry the respect, as Japanese naturally does.
- [ ] [ACT 10:42](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/10/#v42) L/B/R · supplied_words · no subject (L, B); "イエスは" (R) — The Greek leaves the subject to be understood; the meaning note reads it as the risen Jesus (m1). Japanese can leave the subject out, so Literal and Balanced keep the Greek's openness; Readable names Jesus, as the English Readable does. The reading is not certain.
- [ ] [ACT 11:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/11/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "ギリシア語を話す人々" (L); "ギリシア語を話す異邦人たち" (B); "ユダヤ人でない人々、つまりギリシア語を話す人たち" (R) — Here the word names Greek-speaking people who were not Jews, set against "Jews only" in 11:19 (m2), so the usual Acts term, which says Jews, is not used. Literal keeps the word's own sense, people who speak Greek, without deciding; Balanced and Readable say they were Gentiles, as the English does.
- [ ] [ACT 13:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/13/#v32) B/R · word_choice · "起こして" at every level here, not "復活させ" / "よみがえらせ" — The Acts terms give 復活させる and よみがえらせる where God raises Jesus from the dead. Here the verb may mean either bringing Jesus on the scene or raising him from the dead (m2), and 13:34 adds "from the dead" separately; 起こす keeps both readings open, as the English "raising up" does.
- [ ] [ACT 15:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/15/#v7) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "兄弟である皆さん" (L); "兄弟の皆さん" (B); "兄弟のみなさん" (R), with no gender span — As the English decides here, the address is taken as spoken to the apostles and elders, who were men (meaning decision m1), so the word is not widened, as at 23:1. "皆さん" carries the first Greek word; the same address is used in 2:37, 13:15, 15:13, 23:1, 23:6 and 28:17. If reviewers take the whole …
- [ ] [ACT 15:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/15/#v13) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "兄弟である皆さん" (L); "兄弟の皆さん" (B); "兄弟のみなさん" (R), with no gender span — As at 15:7: the English takes the address as spoken to the apostles and elders, and the Japanese follows it.
- [ ] [ACT 16:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/16/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "先生方" for the address "Sirs" — The word is a polite address to men of standing (meaning decision m2), not yet a confession of the Lord, so "主よ" would say too much. "先生方" is how a Japanese speaker would respectfully address two teachers he now holds in awe; "だんなさま方" would suggest masters of a household.
- [ ] [ACT 18:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/18/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "法" — The accusers may mean Jewish or Roman law (m4). "律法" would name the Jewish Law and "法律" suggests the civil code; "法" leaves both open.
- [ ] [ACT 18:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/18/#v18) L/B/R · word_order · no subject for "shaved his head" at Literal and Balanced; "パウロは" at Readable — Japanese with no subject keeps the topic of the sentence, Paul, which is the probable reading (m1), without ruling out Aquila any more than the Greek does. Readable names Paul, as the English Readable implies.
- [ ] [ACT 18:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/18/#v20) R · supplied_words · "エルサレムに上って行って" (R) — "Going up" is the usual way of speaking of travel to Jerusalem (m4), and the English Readable names it. Luke does not, so this is the probable reading, not a certain one.
- [ ] [ACT 19:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/19/#v19) B/R · setting_alternative · {{units:五万日分の賃金|銀貨五万枚}} (B, R); "銀五万[枚]" (L) — The Japanese units setting gives coins as wages by default, with the coin as the alternative. The silver coin was most likely the drachma, about a day's pay (meaning note m3), so the wage figure is an approximation, not an exact modern value. Literal keeps the coin.
- [ ] [ACT 28:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/act/28/#v17) L/B/R · setting_alternative · "兄弟である皆さん" (L); "兄弟の皆さん" (B); "兄弟のみなさん" (R), with no gender span — The hearers are the leaders of the Jewish community, who were men, and the meaning note says the address needs no inclusive form; the English has no span here either. The same address is used wherever Acts has it without the gender setting (2:37, 13:15, 15:7, 15:13, 23:1, 23:6, 28:17): Literal …

## ローマの信徒への手紙 (6)

- [ ] [ROM 3:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/3/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "贖いの座" (L); "罪を償う供え物" (B); "罪をゆるし、神の怒りを取り除くためのいけにえ" (R) — The word is the mercy seat of the ark and the atonement it stood for, which both wipes away sin and turns away God's wrath (m1). Literal names the mercy seat, Balanced the atoning offering, and Readable spells out both effects, as the English Readable does. Reviewers should check that Readable does …
- [ ] [ROM 3:27](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/3/#v27) L/B/R · word_choice · "律法" (L); "原理" (B, R), here and in the rest of verse 27 — The word can mean the law of Moses or a ruling principle (m1 in the sentence "by the law of faith"). Literal keeps "律法"; Balanced and Readable say "原理", since "the law of faith" is set against "the law of works" as two ways of standing before God. "法則" is kept for 7:21-8:2.
- [ ] [ROM 7:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/7/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "律法" (L); "法則" (B, R) for nomos as a rule that always holds — Here nomos seems to mean a pattern that always holds (m1), as in 3:27 and 8:2. Literal keeps "律法" so the reader sees Paul's play on the one word through 7:21-25; Balanced and Readable say "法則", a rule at work. "神の律法" stays "律法" at every level.
- [ ] [ROM 8:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/8/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "律法" (L); "法則" (B, R) — Here the word most likely means a ruling power or order, the Spirit's against sin's (m1), as in 7:21-23. The Romans terms keep "律法" at Literal, so the reader sees the same Greek word as the law of Moses, and use "法則" at Balanced and Readable for a rule that is at work. The other reading, the law of …
- [ ] [ROM 10:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/10/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "終わり" (every level) — The meaning decision favours "end" while leaving "goal" open (m1). 終わり follows the reading the levels take; 目標 or 完成 would choose "goal" alone. Japanese has no single common word that carries both senses as English "end" can; a footnote may be wanted at review.
- [ ] [ROM 11:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rom/11/#v30) L · word_order · "あなたがたへのあわれみのために" before the verb "不従順となりました" (L) — The Greek places the phrase between the verb and "so that", and it can go with either (m2). Literal keeps it inside the first clause, where Japanese "ために" leaves both senses open; Balanced and Readable join it to what follows, as the English Balanced and Readable do.

## コリントの信徒への手紙一 (43)

- [ ] [1CO 2:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/2/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊的なことを霊的な[言葉]で解き明かす" (L); "霊が与えてくださる言葉で説明する" (B, R) — Follows the shared reading (m3, m4, both uncertain); the other reading is in the meaning decisions.
- [ ] [1CO 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/3/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "同労者" (L, B); "いっしょに働く仲間" (R) — As in Thessalonians and Philemon; "神のために" keeps the reading of m3.
- [ ] [1CO 4:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/4/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "くず…あか" (L); "掃き捨てるごみ…削りかす" (B); "ごみ…ちり" (R) — The dirt removed in cleaning, and so the most worthless of people (m5, uncertain).
- [ ] [1CO 5:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/5/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "肉の滅び" (L); "肉が滅ぼされる" (B); "この人を支配している罪の欲望が滅ぼされ" (R) — Follows m6 (uncertain): the man's sinful self, not his body; Readable explains it as the English does.
- [ ] [1CO 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_order · Read as a question at every level — Follows m1 (uncertain, footnoted), as the English does.
- [ ] [1CO 6:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/6/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "柔弱な[男]", "男と寝る[男]" (L); "男性との性行為で受け身の側になる男", "男性と寝る男" (B); "ほかの男に自分と性的な関係を持たせる男", "ほかの男と性的な関係を持つ男" (R): two items, describing acts — Book terms: the two words stay two items, describe acts and not orientation, and never become a word for a kind of person; the evidence is in m3-m4.
- [ ] [1CO 6:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/6/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · The Corinthian saying in 「」 at every level; Readable adds "…と言う人がいます" — Book terms on the Corinthian slogans (m1, footnoted); Literal supplies "[と言いますが]" so the Japanese sentence holds together.
- [ ] [1CO 6:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/6/#v13) L/B/R · word_order · The whole sentence is the Corinthians' saying, in one pair of 「」 — Follows m1 (uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 7:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/7/#v1) L/B/R · word_order · The Corinthians' saying in 「」 at every level; Readable adds "…と言う人がいます" — Book terms on the slogans (m1, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 7:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/7/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "聖なる者とされ" at every level — Set apart through the marriage, but not saved (m1, uncertain, footnoted); the wording does not say more.
- [ ] [1CO 7:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/7/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "奴隷にされて" (L); "縛られて" (B); "その結婚に縛られて" (R) — As the English (enslaved, bound, tied); m2 is uncertain and footnoted.
- [ ] [1CO 7:21](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/7/#v21) L/B/R · word_choice · "むしろそれを用いなさい" (L); "それを生かしなさい" (B); "それを受け入れてください" (R) — Follows m1 (uncertain, footnoted): take the chance of freedom.
- [ ] [1CO 7:36](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/7/#v36) B/R · word_choice · "婚約している女性" (B); "婚約している…若い女の人" (R); Literal "自分のおとめ" — Follows the meaning decision that "his virgin" is the woman a man is engaged to (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [1CO 8:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/8/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "滅びてしまう" (L, B); "だめにしてしまいます" (R) — The weak believer is ruined in faith (m1, uncertain); Readable keeps the sense of ruin without naming final loss.
- [ ] [1CO 11:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/11/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "頭" at every level; "男" / "女" (L, B), "男の人" / "女の人" (R), not "夫" / "妻" — The sense of "head" is disputed (m1, uncertain), so the plain word stays; men and women in general, not only husbands and wives (m2, uncertain). The same words are used through 11:3-16.
- [ ] [1CO 11:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/11/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "権威を持つ" at every level, with "頭の上に" — The sense is uncertain (m2, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese keeps "authority" without adding "sign", as the English does.
- [ ] [1CO 12:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/12/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊的な[賜物]" (L); "霊の賜物" (B); "聖霊がくださる贈り物" (R) — Read as the gifts that come from the Spirit (m2, uncertain); R keeps "贈り物" for the gifts as in 1:7, and "聖霊" for the Spirit.
- [ ] [1CO 12:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/12/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "異言" at every level; "異言の解き明かし" (L), "異言を解き明かす力" (B), "異言で語られたことの意味を説明する" (R); R explains "異言" once in brackets — Speech given by the Spirit in a language the speaker has not learned (m6, uncertain); "異言" is the only current name for the gift, so R keeps it and explains it the first time.
- [ ] [1CO 12:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/12/#v13) L/B · word_choice · "一つの霊において" (L); "一つの霊によって" (B, R) — The Spirit is the one in whom believers are baptized (m1, uncertain); L keeps "in", B and R say it in natural Japanese.
- [ ] [1CO 12:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/12/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · "助け、導き" (L); "助ける力、導く力" (B); "ほかの人を助ける人、人々を導く人" (R) — Acts of help for the weak, and the gift of giving direction to the church (m4, m5, uncertain).
- [ ] [1CO 12:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/12/#v31) L/B/R · word_choice · Command "熱心に求めなさい" (L, B), "熱心に求めてください" (R) — Read as a command (m1, uncertain, footnoted in the English).
- [ ] [1CO 13:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/13/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "焼かれるように" (L), "炎に引き渡した" (B), "体を焼かれる" (R) — Follows the SBLGNT reading, "so that I will be burned" (m1, uncertain, footnoted in the English).
- [ ] [1CO 13:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/13/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "こらえ" (στέγει); "耐え抜きます" (ὑπομένει) (L), kept apart at every level — στέγει read as putting up with whatever comes (m1, uncertain, footnoted); "耐え抜く" for enduring under hardship (m4), matching "忍耐".
- [ ] [1CO 14:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v2) L/B · word_choice · "霊によって" (L, B); "聖霊が助けて話させてくださって" (R) — Read as the Holy Spirit giving the speech (m3, uncertain, footnoted in the English).
- [ ] [1CO 14:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v5) L/B/R · word_order · The "unless he interprets" clause follows the comparison as a separate "ただし……" sentence — Japanese puts a condition before its main clause; a following "ただし" keeps Paul's order of thought (m3, uncertain).
- [ ] [1CO 14:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v9) L/B · word_choice · "舌" (L, B), not "異言" — Here the word is the tongue as the organ of speech (m1, uncertain); Japanese has to choose, and "舌" keeps the picture of the instruments.
- [ ] [1CO 14:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊[の働き]" (L); "霊の働き" (B); "聖霊が自分を通して働いてくださること" (R) — The plural "spirits" read as the ways the one Spirit is at work in the gifts (m1, uncertain, footnoted in the English).
- [ ] [1CO 14:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの霊" for the speaker's own spirit — The speaker's inner self, moved by God's Spirit (m1, uncertain); "霊" keeps it apart from the R word "聖霊".
- [ ] [1CO 14:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "事情を知らない人" (L, B); "その言葉が分からない人" (R); also 14:23-24 — Someone in the gathering who cannot understand the tongue, perhaps not yet fully part of the church (m3, uncertain, footnoted); the wording leaves the second sense open.
- [ ] [1CO 14:31](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v31) L/B/R · word_order · "as in all the churches" closes verse 33 as its own sentence — The phrase belongs with what comes before (m5, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 14:34](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v34) L/B/R · word_choice · "女たち" (L, B), "女の人たち" (R); "従う立場にいる" with no object — Women in the gathering in general (m2, uncertain), and to whom they are subject is not stated (m5); Japanese keeps both open, and the word for women matches 11:3-16.
- [ ] [1CO 14:38](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/14/#v38) L/B/R · word_choice · "認めない" / "認められません" (L, B), with God named only at R — A statement, as the SBLGNT prints it (m1), with God as the one who does not recognize (m2); both uncertain and footnoted. L and B keep the play on one verb (m3).
- [ ] [1CO 15:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "月足らずで生まれた者" (L); "時が満ちる前に生まれた子" (B); "生まれるべきではない時に生まれた赤ちゃん" (R) — A child born dead or too early, a picture of Paul's unfitness and his abrupt call (m1, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 15:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v24) L/B · supplied_words · "[キリストが]" (L) and "キリストは" (B), each in a christos span, name the subject — The subject of hands over, does away with and must reign is Christ (m1, m2); Japanese does not use "彼" for him, so the name is supplied.
- [ ] [1CO 15:28](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v28) L/B/R · word_choice · "従われます" (L, B); "ご自分を置かれます" (R) — The Son's own willing act (m3, uncertain, footnoted); the respect form presents it as something he does.
- [ ] [1CO 15:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v29) L/B/R · word_choice · "死者のために" at every level — A practice the Corinthians knew but which is not known now (m1, uncertain, footnoted); "ために" leaves open both "on behalf of" and "for the sake of" (m2).
- [ ] [1CO 15:32](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v32) L/B/R · word_choice · "野獣と戦った" at every level, kept as a picture — Most likely a picture of fierce human opponents (m1, uncertain, footnoted); Japanese keeps the words and leaves the reading open.
- [ ] [1CO 15:42](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v42) L/B/R · word_choice · "蒔かれ" (L, B); "種のように土の中に埋められ" (R) — The body buried in death, like a seed put in the ground (m1, uncertain, footnoted); L and B keep the picture.
- [ ] [1CO 15:45](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v45) L/B/R · word_choice · "命を与える霊" with "霊", not "聖霊" — The risen Christ, who gives the life of the Spirit (m4, uncertain, footnoted); "霊" leaves the English lowercase reading.
- [ ] [1CO 15:49](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/15/#v49) L/B/R · word_choice · Future "着るようになります" (L), "身にまとうことになります" (B), "持つようになるのです" (R) — Follows the SBLGNT, "we will wear", a statement about the future (m1, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 16:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/16/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "兄弟たち" with no gender span — Particular men travelling with Timothy, as the English keeps no setting; and Timothy comes back with them (m3, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 16:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/16/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "[彼の]意志" (L); "彼には今行く気は……ありませんでした" (B, R) — Read as Apollos's own will (m3, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [1CO 16:22](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1co/16/#v22) L/B/R · word_choice · "マラナ・タ" (L); "主よ、来てください" (B); "主イエスよ、来てください" (R) — An Aramaic prayer kept in its own words at L (m1); B and R read it as a prayer (m2, uncertain, footnoted).

## コリントの信徒への手紙二 (9)

- [ ] [2CO 4:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/4/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "信仰の霊" (L, B); "同じ聖霊" (R) — Read as the Holy Spirit who gives faith (m2, uncertain), as the English capital does; "霊" at L and B leaves the other reading, a disposition of faith, possible.
- [ ] [2CO 10:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/10/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "卑屈" (L); "おとなしい" (B); "おとなしくて弱い" (R) — The word is meant in the bad sense the critics gave it, lowly or feeble (m5), so Japanese avoids "謙遜" or "へりくだる", which would praise Paul. Literal uses the sharpest word; Balanced and Readable use "おとなしい", which can sound either mild or timid, so the irony of m5 is left for the reader to hear.
- [ ] [2CO 11:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/11/#v1) B · register · "ぜひ我慢してほしいのです" — Paul's appeal (m2) is warm and half-pleading. Balanced gives it as a request rather than a bare "〜なさい", which here would sound harsher in Japanese than the Greek; Literal keeps the command form.
- [ ] [2CO 11:29](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/11/#v29) R · word_choice · "燃えるように痛みます" — The burning is distress and indignation (m2); Readable says it is pain, while Literal and Balanced keep the picture of burning.
- [ ] [2CO 12:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/12/#v1) B/R · supplied_words · "主が与えてくださった" (B); "主がわたしに見せてくださった" (R) — The visions and revelations are given by the Lord (m3); Balanced and Readable say so, as the English does, with the light respect form for the Lord. Literal keeps the bare "主の".
- [ ] [2CO 12:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/12/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "幕屋を張る" (L); "天幕を張るように" (B); "テントのように" (R) — The verb means to pitch a tent over (m2). Literal uses "幕屋", the word for the tabernacle, which keeps the possible echo of God dwelling with his people (m3); Balanced and Readable use everyday words for a tent.
- [ ] [2CO 12:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/12/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊" (L); "心" (B, R) — The sense is most likely the same attitude and way of acting (m1). English marks this with a small letter; Japanese cannot, and "霊" alone reads as the Holy Spirit, so Balanced and Readable say "心". Literal keeps "霊", leaving open the reading that the Spirit is meant.
- [ ] [2CO 13:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/13/#v11) L/B · word_choice · "慰めを受けなさい" (L); "慰めと励ましを受けなさい" (B); "元気を出してください" (R) — The verb is the letter's word for comfort, "慰める", but here it may also mean "be encouraged" or "listen to my appeal" (m3). Literal keeps "慰め"; Balanced adds "励まし"; Readable follows the English "take courage".
- [ ] [2CO 13:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2co/13/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "交わり" at every level — The fellowship or sharing that the Holy Spirit gives (m4); "交わり" covers both a shared part in the Spirit and the fellowship it creates, so Literal does not need a separate word for "sharing".

## ガラテヤの信徒への手紙 (7)

- [ ] [GAL 1:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/1/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "呪われるべきです" (L); "神の呪いを受けるべきです" (B); "神の呪いが下りますように" (R) — The Greek is a third-person command. Japanese has no polite form of it: the written imperative "呪われよ" breaks the letter's です・ます体, so Literal and Balanced say what must happen to that one, and Readable puts it as a solemn wish. Balanced and Readable name God, as the English does (m3).
- [ ] [GAL 2:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/2/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "割礼の者たち" (L); "割礼を求める人々" (B); "ユダヤ人でない信者も割礼を受けなければならない、と言い張る人たち" (R) — Here the phrase is read as those who insisted on circumcising Gentile believers (m4), not simply Jews, so Balanced and Readable say so, as the English does.
- [ ] [GAL 2:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/2/#v14) L/B/R · register · Paul's words to Cephas in the plain style ("強いるのか"), inside the polite letter — Paul rebukes a fellow apostle to his face before the whole church; the plain style is how one says that among equals in Japanese, and it sets the reported speech off from the letter's です・ます体.
- [ ] [GAL 3:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/3/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "契約" (L); "遺言" (B, R); "契約" again at every level from verse 17 — As the English: the word is used in its everyday sense of a will (m2), and Paul turns it to God's covenant in verse 17. Literal keeps one Japanese word for the one Greek word; Balanced and Readable say will here, where the illustration needs it.
- [ ] [GAL 4:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/4/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "世のもろもろの要素" (L); "この世を支配する諸力" (B); "この世を動かしている力や決まりごと" (R) — The meaning is uncertain (m2): the basic powers and principles thought to govern the world, and the religious rules bound up with them. Literal keeps the bare word; Balanced and Readable follow the English. The same forms are used in 4:9.
- [ ] [GAL 4:30](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/4/#v30) L/B/R · word_choice · "追い出せ" (L); "追い出しなさい" (B); "出て行かせなさい" (R) — Sarah's words to Abraham (m1), quoted as Scripture in the plain style. Literal keeps the bare command "drive out"; Balanced and Readable soften it as the English "send away" does, without the polite request form, which would not be plain style.
- [ ] [GAL 6:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/gal/6/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "新しい創造" (L, B); "神が造っておられる新しい世界の一員になる" (R) — The meaning decision reads both the new world God has begun in Christ and each person made new within it (m2). "新しい創造" keeps both; Readable follows the English.

## フィリピの信徒への手紙 (7)

- [ ] [PHP 1:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/1/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "親衛隊全体" (L); "皇帝の親衛隊全体" (B); "皇帝の親衛隊の兵士たち" (R) — Read as the imperial guard, the soldiers who guarded Paul (m3, uncertain); "親衛隊" names a ruler's guard. The other reading, the governor's residence, is in the meaning decision's footnote.
- [ ] [PHP 2:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/2/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "利用すべきもの" (L); "ご自分のために利用しよう" (B); "ご自分の得のために使おう" (R) — Follows m6 (uncertain): he did not use his equality with God for his own advantage. "固守する" (hold on to) and "奪い取る" (seize) are other readings and stay in the footnote.
- [ ] [PHP 2:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/2/#v14) L/B/R · word_choice · "堅く保っています" (L); "しっかりと握っています" (B, R), as a statement after the picture of the stars — Follows m5 (uncertain): holding fast the word of life. Japanese cannot hang the participle after "輝いています" without inverting the sentence, so it becomes its own clause in verse 16 at every level.
- [ ] [PHP 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/3/#v8) L/B/R · register · "糞土" (L); "ふんのようなもの" (B); "ふんと同じで、何の値打ちもないもの" (R) — A deliberately coarse word for filth fit only to be thrown out (m4, uncertain). "ちりあくた" and "くず" soften it; "ふん" keeps the shock, as the English "dung" does, and is written in kana at Balanced and Readable.
- [ ] [PHP 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/3/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "{{christos:キリスト|メシア}}への信仰" (L, B); "{{christos:キリスト|メシア}}を信じること" (R) — Follows m7 (uncertain): faith in Christ. The reading "Christ's faithfulness" is in the meaning decision's footnote; Japanese の alone (キリストの信仰) would leave it unclear whose faith is meant.
- [ ] [PHP 3:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/3/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "上への召しの賞" (L); "上へと召してくださる神が約束しておられる賞" (B); "天に呼んでくださるとき、神が差し出してくださる賞" (R) — Follows m6 (uncertain): the prize that God's call upward holds out. The race picture of m5 is kept at every level ("身を伸ばし", "目標", "賞"); Readable says "ゴール" as the English says finish line.
- [ ] [PHP 4:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/php/4/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "真実のくびきを共にする者" (L); "真の同志" (B); "忠実な仲間" (R); Clement as "クレメンス" — An unnamed loyal partner (m1, uncertain); the footnote gives the reading as a name. "同労者" is kept for the fellow workers in the same verse, and "協力" for the partnership in the gospel (1:5, 4:15), so the yokefellow takes other words. クレメンス follows the Latin-derived name's usual Japanese form.

## コロサイの信徒への手紙 (7)

- [ ] [COL 1:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/1/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "ご自分と和解させる" (L, B); "ご自分のもとに連れ戻す" (R) — Follows m9 (uncertain): to himself, to God. "ご自分" refers back to God, the one who was pleased. The verb "和解させる" is kept at 1:21-22; Readable says bring back, as the English does.
- [ ] [COL 1:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/1/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの番として満たしています" (L); "まだ欠けているところを満たしています" (B); "まだ受けなければならない苦しみを……受けている" (R); "務め" for the stewardship — Paul does his part in the sufferings still to be borne by Christ's people (m2, m3); nothing is lacking in Christ's saving death. Readable says this as the English does. "務め" is the task entrusted to him (m4).
- [ ] [COL 2:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/2/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "世の諸要素" (L); "この世を支配する諸力" (B); "この世を動かしている力や決まり" (R), the same at 2:20 — Follows m5 (uncertain): the basic powers and principles thought to govern this world, with the rules bound up with them. Literal keeps the bare word; Balanced and Readable say what it means, as the English does.
- [ ] [COL 2:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/2/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "肉の体を脱ぎ捨てる" (L); "肉が支配していた古い生き方が……脱ぎ捨てられた" (B); "罪に支配されていた……古い生き方を取り去って" (R) — Follows m6 (uncertain): the old life ruled by sin is put off, the circumcision Christ performs. Balanced and Readable say so, as the English does, so that no level suggests leaving the physical body.
- [ ] [COL 2:18](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/2/#v18) L/B/R · word_choice · "天使の礼拝" (L); "天使礼拝" (B); "天使を拝まなければならない" (R) — Follows m4 (uncertain): angels as the ones worshiped. Literal keeps the genitive; Readable has to choose, and chooses with the meaning decision. The other reading is in its footnote.
- [ ] [COL 3:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/3/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "詩編", "賛歌" / "賛美の歌", "霊的な歌" (L), "聖霊が与える歌" (B, R); "感謝のうちに" (L), "感謝に満ちた心で" (B) — Every kind of song the church sings (m3); "霊的" means given by the Spirit, as Balanced and Readable say. The Greek word is "grace", here read as gratitude (m4, uncertain); "恵みのうちに" is the footnoted alternative.
- [ ] [COL 3:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/col/3/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "奴隷として仕えている" (L, B); "主{{christos:キリスト|メシア}}の奴隷なのです" (R), as a statement — Follows m3 (uncertain): a statement of fact, with the command reading in the footnote. The verb is the slave's word, so "奴隷" stays, as the English keeps "slaves".

## テサロニケの信徒への手紙一 (2)

- [ ] [1TH 2:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1th/2/#v5) L/B/R · word_choice · "重くのしかかる" (L); "重んじられ、負担をかける" (B); "いろいろなことを求める" (R) — To be a weight means both making demands for support and asserting authority (m5). Literal keeps the picture of weight, which carries both; Balanced names both senses; Readable follows the English "made demands on you".
- [ ] [1TH 2:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1th/2/#v16) L/B · word_choice · "極みにまで" (L); "ついに" (B, R) — The phrase can mean at last, fully, or to the end (m1, uncertain). Literal's "極みにまで" keeps the range of the Greek; Balanced and Readable follow the English "at last".

## テサロニケの信徒への手紙二 (2)

- [ ] [2TH 1:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2th/1/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "神の正しい裁きのしるし" (L); "神の裁きが正しいことを示すしるし" (B); "神が正しく裁かれることを示しています" (R) — The readers' faithful endurance shows that God's coming judgment will be just (m3, uncertain). Japanese keeps the Greek's compact noun at Literal and spells out the link at Balanced and Readable, as the English does, without deciding more than it does.
- [ ] [2TH 3:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2th/3/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "平和の主" for the title; "平安" for the peace he gives — The title matches "平和の神" (1 Thessalonians 5:23; Romans). The peace given to the readers in every circumstance is the inner peace of a blessing, which the style guide writes "平安", as in the greetings.

## テモテへの手紙一 (9)

- [ ] [1TI 1:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/1/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "男と寝る男" (L); "男性と性的な関係を持つ男性" (B, R) — The Greek compound is rare and its exact sense is debated (m3, uncertain, footnoted). Japanese follows the English levels: Literal keeps the picture in the compound, Balanced and Readable say it in plain modern words. The older "男色をする者" sounds dated and judges more than the Greek word shows.
- [ ] [1TI 2:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/2/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "学ぶべきです" for the third-person command; "女" (L), "女性" (B), "女の人たち" (R), not "妻" — Japanese has no third-person imperative, so 〜べきです gives the force of "let her learn". The word can mean women in general or wives (m4, uncertain); 女 / 女性 leave that open as the English "woman" does. "静けさ" / "静かに" keep a calm manner without saying total silence (m2).
- [ ] [1TI 2:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/2/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "権威をふるう" (L), "上に立って権威を行使する" (B), "上に立つ" (R) — The verb is rare and its exact shade is debated (m1, uncertain, footnoted). The Japanese stays as neutral as the English: it says having or using authority over a man, without adding "usurp" or "domineer". Balanced and Readable say 許していません, a present instruction (m3).
- [ ] [1TI 2:15](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/2/#v15) L/B/R · word_choice · "彼女" then "彼女たち" (L); "女" with the plural left unstated (B); "女の人たち" (R) — The Greek moves from "she" to "they" (m2). Literal shows the shift; Balanced lets Japanese, which need not mark number, carry it; Readable says women, as the English Readable does. "を通して" keeps the sense of the debated phrase as open as the English "through" (m1, uncertain).
- [ ] [1TI 3:2](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/3/#v2) L/B/R · word_choice · "一人の妻の夫" (L); "一人の妻に忠実な夫" (B); "妻は一人だけ" (R) — The phrase is debated (m2, uncertain, footnoted): faithfulness to one wife, or no second marriage or polygamy. Each level follows the English level's reading of the wording, so Balanced and Readable differ in wording just as the English ones do. Used again at 3:12 and 5:9.
- [ ] [1TI 3:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/3/#v5) L · word_choice · "悪魔の裁き" (L), "悪魔と同じ裁き" (B, R) — Whose judgment is meant is uncertain (m3). Literal keeps the bare genitive の, which can be read either way; Balanced and Readable take the reading the English gives, the judgment the devil fell under.
- [ ] [1TI 3:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/3/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "女たち" (L), "女性たち" (B), "女の人たち" (R), not "妻たち"; "中傷する" for slanderers — Whether these are women deacons or deacons' wives is uncertain (m1, footnoted); the plain word for women leaves the question open as the English does. The slanderer's word is the one used for the devil (m2); Japanese cannot keep that link and says what they must not do.
- [ ] [1TI 3:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/3/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊において義とされ" (L), "霊によって正しいと認められ" (B), "聖霊は……正しい方であることを示されました" (R) — Vindicated means shown to be in the right, likely in the resurrection (m4, uncertain). Literal keeps the Greek verb usually rendered 義とする; Balanced and Readable say what it means.
- [ ] [1TI 5:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/1ti/5/#v11) L/B/R · word_choice · "最初の誓い" (L, B), "{{christos:キリスト|メシア}}にした約束" (R) — The first pledge is most likely their promise to serve Christ as enrolled widows (m2, uncertain). 誓い keeps a solemn promise without saying to whom; Readable names Christ, where the English Readable has "to him", since Japanese does not use a pronoun for Christ.

## テモテへの手紙二 (7)

- [ ] [2TI 1:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/1/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊" at every level, not "精神" or "心" — The spirit may be the Spirit God gives (m1, uncertain). Japanese 霊 has no capital and can mean either a spirit or God's Spirit, so it keeps the question open as the English does, while 精神 would choose a human disposition.
- [ ] [2TI 1:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/1/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "わたしの託したもの" (L), "わたしが委ねたもの" (B), "わたしがお任せしたもの" (R); "かの日" (L), "その日" (B), "主がもう一度来られる日" (R) — Whether the deposit is what Paul entrusted to God or what God entrusted to Paul is uncertain (m1, footnoted); Japanese follows the English reading at every level. That day is the day of Christ's coming and judgment (m2), named plainly at Readable, as the Matthew notes settled かの日 for Literal.
- [ ] [2TI 2:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/2/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "不真実" / "真実" (L), "不誠実" / "誠実" (B), "忠実でなくても" / "忠実な" (R) — The Greek sets faithless against faithful with one root (m2); each level keeps a matching pair. Whether the line is promise or warning is debated (m1, footnoted); the Japanese, like the English, leaves it as said.
- [ ] [2TI 2:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/2/#v24) L · word_order · "彼に生け捕りにされてあの者の意志に従わされていた" (L), keeping two different pronouns; Balanced and Readable say the devil's will — Whose captive and whose will is debated (m2, uncertain, footnoted). Literal keeps the Greek's two pronouns apart so a reader can see the question; Balanced and Readable take the English reading, the devil's.
- [ ] [2TI 3:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/3/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "神の息によるもの" (L), "神の息吹によって与えられたもの" (B), "神の息から出たもの" (R), not "霊感" — The word says breathed out by God (m2). 霊感 is the usual theological term but in everyday Japanese means artistic inspiration; every level keeps the picture of God's breath, as the English does. Like the English, the Japanese reads "is God-breathed and useful" (m1, uncertain, footnoted).
- [ ] [2TI 4:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/4/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "立派な戦いを戦い抜き" (L), "立派に戦い抜き" (B), "競技をする選手のように" (R); "信仰を守り通しました" — The good fight is the athlete's contest (m1), the same words as 1 Timothy 6:12. 守る can mean both staying faithful and guarding the faith entrusted to him, so it keeps the uncertain sense open (m2).
- [ ] [2TI 4:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2ti/4/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "獅子の口" (L), "ライオンの口" (B), "ライオンの口から救い出すように……大きな危険から" (R); Gentiles "異邦人" (L, B), "ユダヤ人でない人々" (R) — The lion's mouth is most likely deadly danger (m2, uncertain); Literal and Balanced keep the picture and Readable explains it, as the English Readable does. Gentiles take the brief's table forms; the English Readable keeps "Gentiles".

## テトスへの手紙 (5)

- [ ] [TIT 1:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/tit/1/#v5) L · supplied_words · "[その人を任命しなさい]" supplied to close the condition — Japanese cannot leave "if anyone is ..." hanging after a full stop; the bracket says what the condition is for. Marked uncertain because the English Literal leaves it open.
- [ ] [TIT 2:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/tit/2/#v11) L/B/R · word_order · "偉大な神であり、わたしたちの救い主であるイエス・{{christos:キリスト|メシア}}": one person, as in the English — Follows the meaning decision (uncertain) that God and Savior both name Jesus Christ; the Japanese apposition with "であり" cannot be read as two persons.
- [ ] [TIT 3:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/tit/3/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "再生の洗いと聖霊の更新" (L); "新しく生まれさせる洗いと、聖霊によって新たにされること" (B); "洗って清め、新しく生まれさせ……聖霊がわたしたちを新しくして" (R) — Literal keeps the two genitives so that the grammar stays as open as the Greek; Balanced and Readable follow the English reading, which the meaning decision marks uncertain.
- [ ] [TIT 3:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/tit/3/#v8) L/B · word_choice · "善い行いに励む" (L, B) for devoting oneself to good works, here and in 3:14 — Follows the English reading; the meaning decision marks it uncertain ("engage in honest occupations" is another reading).
- [ ] [TIT 3:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/tit/3/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "ゼナス" for Zenas, "アポロ" for Apollos; "法律家" for the lawyer — "法律家" leaves open whether Zenas was an expert in Roman or in Jewish law; "律法の専門家" would choose the second.

## フィレモンへの手紙 (2)

- [ ] [PHM 1:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/phm/1/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "交わり" (L); "惜しみない分かち合い" (B); "人々に惜しみなく分け与える" (R) — Literal keeps the church word for koinonia; Balanced and Readable follow the meaning decision that it is Philemon's generous sharing with other believers, which is uncertain.
- [ ] [PHM 1:8](https://openwordbible.org/ja/phm/1/#v8) L/B/R · word_choice · "老人" (L), "年老いた者" (B), "年を取っています" (R) — Follows the meaning decision "old man", which is marked uncertain (some read "ambassador").

## ヘブライ人への手紙 (18)

- [ ] [HEB 1:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/1/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "風" for pneumata — The meaning decision reads "winds" (uncertain; the word can also mean spirits, as in 1:14). Japanese "風" follows it; the footnote carries the other reading.
- [ ] [HEB 2:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/2/#v9) L/B/R · word_choice · "神から離れて" at every level — The meaning decision follows the reading "apart from God" and reads it as Jesus going through death fully, without God sparing him; Japanese says it as plainly as the Greek and leaves the sense to the footnote.
- [ ] [HEB 5:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/5/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "義の言葉" (L, B), "正しいことについての教え" (R) — The meaning decision reads it as teaching about righteousness that trains a person to tell right from wrong (uncertain); Balanced keeps the Greek phrase open.
- [ ] [HEB 6:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/6/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "洗い" (L), "清めの洗い" (B, R) for baptismoi, not "洗礼" — The plural word covers ritual washings, including the difference between Christian baptism and other washings (uncertain); "洗礼" would narrow it to baptism.
- [ ] [HEB 6:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/6/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "離れ落ちた" (L, B), "離れて行ってしまいました" (R) for parapiptō; "再び十字架につける" for anastauroō — The meaning decision reads falling away as abandoning the faith; Japanese "離れ落ちる" says turning away from Christ without the church word "背教". The warning is rendered as plainly as the Greek states it, and the footnote carries the open questions.
- [ ] [HEB 7:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/7/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "移ることのない" (L), "ほかの人に移ることは決してありません" (B) — The meaning decision (uncertain) reads it as a priesthood that never passes to a successor; Japanese follows it, and the footnote keeps the sense "permanent".
- [ ] [HEB 7:25](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/7/#v25) L/B/R · word_choice · "完全に" for eis to panteles; "とりなす" (L, B), "神に願ってくださる" (R) for entynchano — "完全に" leaves room for the sense "for all time" that the meaning decision keeps close at hand; "とりなす" is the living church word for pleading on someone's behalf, which Readable spells out.
- [ ] [HEB 9:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/9/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "金の香の祭壇" (L), "香をたく金の祭壇" (B, R) for thymiaterion; "ケルビム" for the cherubim — The meaning decision reads it as the golden altar of incense (uncertain; the word can mean a censer); "ケルビム" is the conventional form.
- [ ] [HEB 9:13](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/9/#v13) L/B/R · word_choice · "永遠の霊" at every level — The meaning decision reads it as the Holy Spirit (uncertain); Japanese "霊" keeps the Greek open, as "聖霊" would not, and the footnote carries the question.
- [ ] [HEB 9:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/9/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "遺言" for diatheke in 9:16-17 at every level, "契約" elsewhere — The author plays on the double sense of the word (meaning decision, uncertain); Japanese needs two words, so the switch is marked here and the footnote explains the play.
- [ ] [HEB 10:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/10/#v12) L/B/R · word_choice · "永遠に" placed with the sacrifice (L: "一つのいけにえを永遠にささげて"; B, R: a sacrifice valid for all time) — The meaning decision takes "for all time" with the sacrifice, not with his sitting down (uncertain); Japanese word order makes the link plain.
- [ ] [HEB 10:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/10/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · "垂れ幕、すなわちご自分の肉" (L, B), "その垂れ幕とは、イエスご自身の体" (R) — The meaning decision reads his flesh as the curtain through which the way passes (uncertain); Japanese keeps the apposition and leaves the other reading to the footnote.
- [ ] [HEB 11:11](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/11/#v11) L/B/R · supplied_words · Abraham as the subject: "[彼は]" (L), "アブラハムは" (B, R), with Sarah as "サラ自身と共に" / "サラと共に" — Japanese must name or bracket the subject; the meaning decision reads Abraham as the one who received power, together with Sarah (marked uncertain, with a footnote).
- [ ] [HEB 11:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/11/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "たとえとして" (L), "たとえて言えば" (B), "まるで……かのよう" (R) for en parabolē — As "たとえ" for parabolē in 9:9; Isaac's return is a picture of resurrection (meaning decision, uncertain).
- [ ] [HEB 11:23](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/11/#v23) L/B/R · word_choice · "美しい" (L), "優れた子" (B), "特別な子ども" (R) for asteios — A fine, well-formed child, perhaps marked out by God (meaning decision, uncertain); Balanced and Readable leave room for more than looks.
- [ ] [HEB 12:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/12/#v17) R · word_choice · "一度したことを取り消す道" (R) for "no place of repentance"; "悔い改めの余地" / "悔い改めの機会" (L, B) — The meaning decision reads it as no way for what was done to be undone (uncertain); Readable says that, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [HEB 13:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/13/#v20) L/B/R · word_choice · "この方に" at every level for "to whom" — The meaning decision leaves open whether the glory is to God or to Jesus Christ (uncertain, footnoted); "この方" keeps both readings, where the English Readable's "he" leans to God.
- [ ] [HEB 13:24](https://openwordbible.org/ja/heb/13/#v24) L/B/R · word_choice · "イタリアからの人々" (L, B), "イタリアの信者たち" (R) — The meaning decision leaves open whether these are Italians away from home or people in Italy (uncertain); "からの" and plain "の" keep both readings.

## ヤコブの手紙 (3)

- [ ] [JAS 2:20](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jas/2/#v20) B · word_choice · "何の働きもしない" (B) — m2: the adjective is a play on the word for work. Balanced lets "行い" and "働き" echo each other, as the English Balanced keeps works and does no work.
- [ ] [JAS 4:5](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jas/4/#v5) B · supplied_words · "ねたむほどの愛で" — Balanced follows the reading in which God is the one who longs (m1, m2): his jealousy is the jealousy of love for his people. Literal keeps "ねたみをもって". This reading is not certain.
- [ ] [JAS 5:6](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jas/5/#v6) L/B/R · word_choice · No subject (B); "[彼は]" (L); "その人たち" (R). "敵対する" (L), "立ち向かう" (B, R), the words used for the same verb in 4:6 — The subject may be the righteous one or God (m2). Japanese can leave it unstated, and Balanced does. Literal brackets a plain "彼", which Japanese does not use for God. Readable, which needs a subject, follows the plural it gave the righteous in the sentence before; this takes one side of an open …

## ヨハネの手紙二 (1)

- [ ] [2JN 1:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/2jn/1/#v10) L · word_choice · Literal quotes the greeting as "「ようこそ」" (also 1:11) — The English Literal quotes "Greetings", so Japanese Literal keeps one quotation. The word said is a welcome that receives someone as a fellow believer (m2); "ようこそ" says that, where "ごきげんよう" is a mere pleasantry and a literal "rejoice" is not Japanese.

## ヨハネの手紙三 (1)

- [ ] [3JN 1:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/3jn/1/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "御名" (L, B), "イエスの名" (R); "異邦人" (L), "異教徒" (B), "信仰を持っていない人たち" (R) — The word here means pagans, people who are not believers (m3), not non-Jews as such, so Balanced and Readable follow the English (pagans; people who are not believers). "御名" is the respectful church word for the Name.

## ユダの手紙 (1)

- [ ] [JUD 1:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/jud/1/#v14) L/B/R · register · the quotation in the plain style, with "見なさい" at every level; "来られた" (L), "来られる" (B, R) — Quoted writing inside a letter keeps the plain style; "見てください" would clash with the plain verbs that follow, so Readable keeps "見なさい" here. The tenses follow the English (m3).

## ヨハネの黙示録 (14)

- [ ] [REV 1:7](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/1/#v7) L/B/R · word_choice · "見よ" (L, B); "見てください" (R) in John's words, "見なさい" (R) in the speech of God and Christ — In the letters the style guide uses "見なさい" for the word "look", but in Revelation it most often opens a prophetic announcement or a vision, not an instruction to a reader, and the solemn plain "見よ" fits that register. Readable, which is polite throughout, says "見てください". Used for the whole book in …
- [ ] [REV 1:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/1/#v9) R · supplied_words · "神の言葉とイエスの証しを伝えたために" (R) — Readable gives the cause the grammar decision takes as most likely (m4), as the English Readable does; the text does not say outright that John was exiled.
- [ ] [REV 1:10](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/1/#v10) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊のうちにあった" (L); "霊にとらえられた" (B); "聖霊がわたしをとらえました" (R) — The style guide writes "霊" for the Spirit; Literal keeps the phrase open as the Greek does, and Balanced says that John was under the Spirit's power without saying more (m1). Readable names the Holy Spirit and says what the phrase means, which depends on m1. The same words are used for "in the …
- [ ] [REV 11:16](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/11/#v16) L/B/R · word_choice · "座" for the elders' thrones; "玉座" for God's — As in 4:4: the elders' thrones are "座", so that "玉座" stays the throne of God and the Lamb.
- [ ] [REV 12:12](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/12/#v12) L/B/R · register · "喜べ" (every level) — A call to the heavens in a hymn (m1) takes the short solemn imperative; "〜なさい" is kept for commands to people.
- [ ] [REV 13:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/13/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "屠られて" (L, B); "殺された" (R) — The verb used of the Lamb "as though slaughtered" (5:6); the same Japanese verb lets the reader see the parody (m1). "屠" is not a Jōyō kanji, so Readable says killed.
- [ ] [REV 14:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/14/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "童貞" (L); "純潔な者たち" (B); "おとめのように清い" (R) — The Greek word is used of men here. Literal keeps a word for a man who has not been with a woman; Balanced says what the word stands for, purity and devotion to the Lamb alone (m1); Readable shows that it is a picture, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [REV 14:14](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/14/#v14) L/B/R · register · "人の子のような方" with respect forms ("座っておられた", "持っておられた") — The meaning decision takes the figure to be Christ (m1), so the narration gives him the light respect forms used for God and the Lamb, here and in 14:15-16. If a reviewer takes him for an angel, these become plain verbs.
- [ ] [REV 17:1](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/17/#v1) L/B/R · word_choice · "大淫婦" (L, B); "大娼婦" (R) for "the great prostitute"; without "great" (17:15, 16) "淫婦" (L, B), "娼婦" (R) — The book's shared terms give "大淫婦" (L, B) and "娼婦" (R). Where the Greek has only "the prostitute" (17:15-16), Literal and Balanced drop the "大" that stands for "great". The picture is of a city's corrupting power, not a statement about women (m1). The first kanji of "娼婦" is not a Jōyō kanji; a …
- [ ] [REV 17:3](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/17/#v3) L/B/R · word_choice · "霊のうちに" (L); "霊にとらえられた" (B); "聖霊がわたしをとらえ" (R) — "In the Spirit" marks a new vision, in the same words as 1:10 (m1); Readable names the Spirit taking hold of John, as the English Readable does.
- [ ] [REV 18:9](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/18/#v9) L/B/R · register · the city is addressed as "おまえ" (every level) — Where the city is spoken to, in the laments and in 18:14 and 18:22-23, Japanese uses "おまえ", the plain word for addressing one who is denounced or mourned from below, and keeps it at every level.
- [ ] [REV 20:4](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/20/#v4) L/B/R · word_choice · "座" — 玉座 is kept for the throne of God (and the Lamb); the thrones of those who judge here are simply 座.
- [ ] [REV 21:19](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/21/#v19) L/B/R · word_choice · 碧玉, サファイア, 玉髄, エメラルド, 赤縞めのう, 赤めのう, 貴かんらん石, 緑柱石, トパーズ, 緑玉髄, ヒヤシンス石, 紫水晶; at Readable へきぎょく and 赤じまめのう — Traditional Japanese names of the stones where they exist, kept the same at every level, since the ancient stones cannot be identified with certainty (m2). The eleventh keeps the Greek name, hyacinth stone, rather than a modern identification. Readable writes non-Jōyō kanji in kana.
- [ ] [REV 22:17](https://openwordbible.org/ja/rev/22/#v17) L/B/R · word_choice · "来てください" — Japanese must choose a polite or a plain imperative. The call is most likely a prayer to Christ (m1), so it is the polite request used to the Lord, as in 22:20. Readers who take it as an invitation lose little, since the same form invites the thirsty at Readable.
